###############################################################################
# Warrior society events: warrior lodges, warbands and war aid
#
# Part 1: Warrior lodges
# Namespace: lodge (was HF.10003 - HF.10071 in hf_warrior_lodge_events;
#   lodge.65 - lodge.81 were HF.25007 - HF.25070 and WULRIC.30 - WULRIC.31
#   in HF_warrior_lodge_powers_events)
# Faction: Warrior societies
# Serves: the warrior lodges (Warriors of Ulric, the Knightly Orders, the
#   Warriors Guild of the Dawi); the joining trial, the rank-up duel and the
#   AI rank-ups also serve the warbands (Chaos, Skaven, Gork and Mork)
#
# A character who asks to join a lodge or a warband is met by one of its
# champions and must win (or at least survive) a sparring duel against a
# member. The champion's voice is the society's voice: a Chosen of the Dark
# Gods, a Skaven Warlord, an Orc boss, a Wolf-priest's warrior, a knight of
# an Imperial order or a Dawi veteran. A parent in the society can push an
# adult child into the trial; the child's success or failure comes home to
# dinner. The powers of the lodges follow: the military path (duelist,
# strategist or hunter), warrior training, the toughness of greenskins, the
# berserk rage of Ulric's warriors and the Norscans (in the hall or in a
# cell) and the call to arms. Hidden upkeep fills the lodges with AI members,
# ranks them up and refounds a lodge that died out. A member at rank 3 can
# fight a Hero for a seat at the table; a Hero's household may follow the
# winner. Veterans and Heroes of Ulric and the Norscans wear the lodge's
# tattoos. In a war, the Warriors Guild can call on a thane's throng of
# warriors, thunderers and hammerers, who may become a friend or a lover.
# The call to war speeds the levies of any lodge or Chaos warband; a
# knight of the Knightly Orders swears to one order of the Old World,
# picked by region and faith. Heroes set weak members to spar, strong
# warriors seek duels to the death with rival societies, members teach
# each other courage, ambition, patience and diligence, and the friend or
# lover of a fallen warrior may forge a soulbound blade by a forbidden rite.
# Once in a life a Hero hunts the great beast of their faith. At feasts
# the members arm-wrestle, throw dice, trade insults and drink each other
# under the table. When a society's legend is full, its Heroes go on a
# legendary journey that can found a bloodline.
#
# The joining trial            lodge.1 - lodge.9
# A child joins the lodge      lodge.10 - lodge.15
# The military path            lodge.20 - lodge.28
# Warrior training             lodge.30
# Toughness                    lodge.35
# Berserk rage                 lodge.40 - lodge.44
# The call to arms             lodge.45
# Upkeep                       lodge.50 - lodge.54
# The seat at the table        lodge.60 - lodge.64
# Scars and tattoos            lodge.65 - lodge.66
# The clans muster (Dawi)      lodge.70 - lodge.76 (was HF.25007 - HF.25013)
# Call to war, knightly oath   lodge.80 - lodge.81 (was WULRIC.30 - WULRIC.31)
# The young recruit            lodge.100 - lodge.135 (was HF.25400 - HF.25435)
# Missions                     lodge.200 - lodge.247 (was HF.25500 - HF.25511,
#                              HF.10500 - HF.10521, WULRIC.1 - WULRIC.8 and
#                              their WB twins)
# Flavour                      lodge.300 - lodge.351 (was HF.500, HFP.501 -
#                              HFP.506, HF.550, HF.560 - HF.561, HF.14000 -
#                              HF.14009 and WB.51000 - WB.51011, with their
#                              WB twins)
# The Great Hunt               lodge.400 - lodge.432 (was HF.25600 - HF.25631
#                              and their WB twins; 432 is new)
# Feasts                       lodge.500 - lodge.601 (was HF.25200 - HF.25301
#                              and their WB twins)
# Legendary journey            lodge.700 - lodge.755 (was HF.11999 - HF.12405
#                              and their WB twins: 11999 -> 700, 12000 -
#                              12012 -> 701 - 713, 121xx -> 720 - 724,
#                              122xx -> 730 - 735, 123xx -> 740 - 745,
#                              124xx -> 750 - 755)
#
# Saved: character flags awaiting_initiation_trial, society_join_block,
#   they_accepted@, they_declined@, mock_duel, show_once_flag,
#   accepted_trial, refused_trial, parent_was_supportive, parent_was_mean,
#   choosing_military_aspect, choosing_warrior_training, opt_A/B/C,
#   no_harm, someone_is_hurt, hurt_self, may_join_society,
#   lodge_got_wolf_pelt, lodge_got_household (once per life),
#   chose_knighthood_to_pledge_to, flag_child_is_a_warrior,
#   flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_lazy/scholar/lady/coward,
#   flag_child_abandoned_warband, lodge_got_mentor_blade (once per life);
#   character modifiers
#   warband_failed_joining_cd (ten-year ban after a failed trial),
#   lodge_growth_cooldown, lost_rank_up_duel_cooldown,
#   lodge_slavic_stand_together_cooldown, knights_call_of_the_war_boost and
#   the knightly order modifiers (reiksguard_knight, knight_of_*); earmarks
#   lodge_household, lodge_stand_together; event targets
#   martial_society_to_join, new_recruit, lodge_hero_spoils,
#   champion_character, duel_target, combatant_1, combatant_2,
#   recruiting_parent, mentor_target, kinslayer_target,
#   scoped_slavic_savior (the thane of the muster), child_hero_mentor,
#   fleeing_target; for the missions the quests quest_warband_raid_and_loot,
#   quest_warband_weak_focus_to_*, quest_warband_duel_honor/_deadly,
#   quest_military_war, quest_warband_war, quest_grudge_war and
#   quest_warband_recruit, the modifier quest_cooldown_timer, the flags
#   flag_renounce_focus_for_*, won_war@/white_peace@/lost_war@,
#   impressed@ and deadly_duel@, and the event targets lodge_hero,
#   home_capital, wl_looting_target, duel_target, my_war_target,
#   my_war_target_holder, recruit_to_be, attacker_target, defender_target;
#   for the flavour the flags easy_duel/even_duel/hard_duel,
#   notify_instigating_hero, appreciated_duel, beneath_me_duel,
#   feared_for_life_duel, learning_*@ and teaching_*@ (the lesson),
#   they_gained_*/they_lost_*, great/good/bad_outcome, kiss_of_gratitude,
#   lodge_got_soulbound (once per life), lodge_soulbound_forging,
#   decided_to_dig_up/kidnap and crucible_steel_axe/sword/mace, the
#   modifiers teaching_moment_cooldown, lodge_soulbound_strength and
#   crucible_weapon_*, and the event targets hero_instigating_duel,
#   society_practice_duelist_target_1 - 3, lodge_member_killed/killer,
#   teacher, student, mourning_friend/lover, dead_warrior, weapon_forger,
#   potential_sacrifice, witness1/2 and unfortunate_child; for the hunt
#   the flags flag_WL_had_ritual_hunt_event (once per life),
#   lodge_hunt_wolf/griffon/troll/beast and do_not_disturb, the variable
#   wl_ritual_hunting, the modifiers ritual_hunt_trophy_*,
#   lodge_troll_burned, lodge_pet_griffon, lodge_griffon_head and the
#   warpaint, and the event targets strong/smart/loyal_hunting_partner
#   and scoped_WL_co_hunter; for the feasts the modifiers
#   arm_wrestling_challenge_cooldown and flyting_challenge_cooldown, the
#   flags arm_wrestling_*, flyting_*, insulting_*, flag_WL_drinking_*,
#   flag_betting_money_*, lodge_dice_bet_* and lodge_dice_is_*, and the
#   event targets arm_wrestling_fighter_1/2, wl_arm_wrestling_winner/loser,
#   flyting_contender_1/2, warrior_lodge_drinking_finalist_1/2,
#   WL_host_drinking_game, WL_drinking_contest_winner and
#   random_society_winner; for the legendary journey the society flag
#   block_society_progress, the flags call_to_legend_active,
#   start/mid/end_bracket, HF_legend_*, WL_desc_*, is_legendary_duel and
#   rank_up_blocked, the variable legendary_event_progress, the modifier
#   lodge_legend_news_cooldown, and the event targets legendary_society,
#   original_hero, fellow_hero_1 - 3, target_ruler and target_title (also
#   persistent), home_capital, my_duel_target, combatant_1/2 and
#   new_story_owner.
#
# Callers: common/on_actions/00_on_actions.txt (on_character_ask_to_join_
#   society 1, on_five_year_pulse 50, on_society_bi_yearly_pulse 54,
#   on_society_destroyed 53); decisions/warrior_lodge_decisions.txt (20,
#   21, 30, 35, 40, 43, 45); decisions/hf_warrior_lodge_decisions.txt (10);
#   decisions/mnm_society_decisions.txt (60);
#   decisions/warrior_lodge_decisions.txt (warrior_lodge_slavic_stand_
#   together 70, knights_call_of_the_war 80, pledge_to_knighthood 81);
#   on_startup (65); events/HF_warrior_lodge_mission_events.txt (66);
#   lodge.12 (100, the young recruit); missions: on_society_bi_yearly_pulse
#   (200), on_loot_settlement (203), on_focus_selected (206 - 209),
#   on_new_holder* (210), on_death (222), on_war_ended_* (230 - 234),
#   events/DoW_Adventurers.txt (DoWA.200, DoWA.250: 201, 204, 220, 225,
#   227, 235, 242); flavour: on_society_bi_yearly_pulse (300, 310, 320),
#   on_death (311, 340), the duel effects (301 - 306); the hunt:
#   on_society_bi_yearly_pulse (400), common/scripted_effects/
#   00_scripted_effects.txt (WL_ritual_hunt_flavor_events_effect: 403,
#   406, 408 - 415), the eagle sources in wh_offmap_effects.txt,
#   06_scripted_effects_legendary_bloodlines.txt and the lodge legends
#   (431); the feasts: on_society_bi_yearly_pulse (500, 526, 550, 580) and
#   common/scripted_effects/05_scripted_effects_competitions.txt (505, 515,
#   518, 522, 562, 586, 588, 589, 592); the legendary journey:
#   on_society_progress_full (700), on_death (744),
#   on_become_imprisoned_any_reason (745), HF_battle_events.txt (755),
#   06_scripted_effects_legendary_bloodlines.txt (704, 723, 740 - 742,
#   750, 751) and 01_scripted_effects_duels.txt (730 - 735);
#   common/scripted_effects/01_scripted_effects_duels.txt (9, 61, 301 -
#   306);
#   events/hfp_duel_events.txt (13).
# Support: common/scripted_triggers/wh_warband_triggers.txt (voices, lodge
#   recruits), common/scripted_effects/wh_warband_effects.txt
#   (lodge_household_spawn_effect, lodge_child_*, lodge_sparring_*,
#   lodge_teach_*, lodge_soulbound_*, lodge_hunt_*), common/event_modifiers/
#   wh_warband_modifiers.txt (lodge_growth_cooldown,
#   lodge_soulbound_strength, lodge_pet_griffon, lodge_griffon_head,
#   lodge_troll_burned), WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect and the dice effects
#   in 00_scripted_effects.txt, the feast steps in
#   05_scripted_effects_competitions.txt, GetWLDieName, GetWLDieBetName and
#   GetLodgeDrink in localisation/customizable_localisation/
#   04_customizable_localisation_warrior_lodges.txt.
#
# Fixed from vanilla: a captive leader made two champions answer the same
# recruit, and the gating event could send the verdict three times; the ten
# year ban after a failed trial was promised but never set; the hunter path
# showed Chaos torturer text to every lodge; "kill your son" killed no one
# and had no text; berserk rage looked for fellows of a lodge that does not
# exist in the mod and could hurt nobody while saying someone was hurt; the
# prison rage could pick no victim; the revival made every courtier join and
# paid the leader twice; AI recruits of the Old World faith picked the
# Knights or Ulric at fixed odds, now their temper decides; Kislev's
# warriors could never reach the Circle of the Bear; the growth notice used
# a cooldown modifier that did not exist; the seclusion clean-up HFP.19000
# moved to WHG_maintenance_events (aiclean.53). The scars of a scarred
# character never showed, as the start-up event was not called, and the
# lodge tattoos went only to vanilla lodges the mod does not have. The
# muster could find no thane and still take the price (now refunded). In
# the knightly oath, the Knights of the Fiery Heart checked a region on
# the knight instead of the capital, the Knights of the Bull looked for a
# culture called stubborn, and a knight with no order open got an empty
# event (now: wait and ask again). The young recruit: the brawn-for-looks
# pick tested an impossible build, the tooltip and the effect of losing
# your looks did not match, a child who gave in or endured was dropped
# from the training for good, and an already miserable child who was
# told no could be left with no choice but death; a child too fine for
# the barracks is no longer only a girl. The missions: the lodges and the
# warbands had two copies of every mission, now one serves both (and the
# pirates, the League and the Amazons, whose focus and duel missions were
# refused by a lodge-only check); a society with no Hero never handed out
# a mission; the war letter was signed by a name that was never saved;
# the grudge mission looked for the holder of a character and never ended
# after a lost war; the raid target was cleared only for lodge members;
# Norscans were checked by a religion the mod does not have; the Warriors
# of Ulric's reclaim of the old lands is back. The flavour: the witch
# hunters' captains could never train their members (a stray rank check
# shut them out); the sparring report cleared the wound notice on the wrong
# character, so a member read as wounded in every later report; a lesson
# left its flags on the student for good, and one meant to cure cowardice,
# contentment or sloth either did nothing or never removed the vice; the
# "nice students learn" weights read the teacher; the "no friends yet"
# weight could never be true; the gear meant to cost players only was
# paid by all; the kiss was allowed only by real-world faiths (now
# Slaanesh and faiths of the holy family). The soulbound weapon, cut from
# the lodges, is back for lodges and warbands, once per life: its luck
# weights favoured getting caught, the kin to be bled was looked up in the
# mourner's own family and never bled, "only running late" had the forger
# executed, the legendary blade was the same as the whispering one, the
# fallback made a hurt child a hundred times likelier, and a mourner with
# artifacts counted as having none. The Great Hunt matched no lodge of the
# mod, so the lodges hunted with empty text and every warband hunted
# Ulric's wolf; now each faith hunts its own beast. The partner never lost
# the do_not_disturb flag after the hunt, the friend could be the same man
# as the strong partner, the hunt after a bloody kill was only cleaned up
# for one of four choices, and the Karhunpeijaiset, Perun, Tengri and the
# Eternal Blue Sky are gone. The feasts: the dice game served only two
# vanilla lodges and is back for all; the flyting served only Ulric's
# warriors and now serves every society; the drinking contest saved its
# finalists under one name and read them under another, so the lodges'
# contest could never name a finalist; the dice bets named Hindu ages
# and Greek gods (now the Skull, the Sword, the Crown and the Coin); a
# caught cheat was scorned by the whole society, the cheat included; the
# winner's admirer could be kin only in real-world faiths (now Slaanesh
# or the holy family); a beaten wrestler's duel had no tooltip. The
# legendary journey: the lodges and warbands had two copies of every
# event, now one serves both; the text named the Heroes by targets that
# were never saved, so their names were blank; the heir who hears the
# tale was saved for any fallen duellist, not only the Hero; the warbands
# never had the invincible check; a new eagle came after five days, a
# test value; the news cooldown did not exist; warbands looked for a
# foe's realm nearby and lodges anywhere (now nearby first).
#
# Also in this file (each part keeps its own header and namespace):
#   Warbands (namespace warband, was WHG_warband_events)
#   War aid (namespace waraid, was WHG_war_aid_events)
###############################################################################

namespace = lodge

###############################################################################
# The joining trial
###############################################################################

# lodge.1 A recruit knocks
# From on_character_ask_to_join_society. A free champion of the society
# (the leader if free, else the highest-ranked free member) is told about
# the recruit.
# Scope: ROOT is the recruit, FROM the society.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.1
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = is_being_recruited_to_warband_by_parent }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = awaiting_initiation_trial }
		FROM = {
			leader = {
				OR = {
					is_member_of_any_warrior_lodge_trigger = yes
					is_member_of_any_warband_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = martial_society_to_join }
		set_character_flag = society_join_block
		set_character_flag = awaiting_initiation_trial
		save_event_target_as = new_recruit
		# The leader if free, else the highest-ranked free member
		event_target:martial_society_to_join = {
			any_society_member = {
				limit = { prisoner = no }
				score_value = {
					value = 1
					additive_modifier = { value = 10000 is_society_grandmaster = yes }
					additive_modifier = { value = 1000 society_rank == 4 }
					additive_modifier = { value = 100 society_rank == 3 }
					additive_modifier = { value = 10 society_rank == 2 }
				}
				count = 1
				save_event_target_as = lodge_free_champion
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:lodge_free_champion = { always = yes } }
			event_target:lodge_free_champion = { character_event = { id = lodge.2 } }
		}
		else = {
			event_target:martial_society_to_join = {
				leader = { character_event = { id = lodge.2 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# lodge.2 The champion meets the recruit
# Scope: ROOT is the champion.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.2
	desc = EVTDESClodge2
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_chaos_warband
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_skaven_war }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_greenskinanimosity
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_ulric_warriors }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_imperial_knight }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_dwarf_fighter }
	picture = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_melee
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { save_event_target_as = champion_character }
	}

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAlodge2_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAlodge2_skaven }
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge2_greenskin
		}
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAlodge2_ulric }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAlodge2_knight }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAlodge2_dawi }
		name = {
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			text = EVTOPTAlodge2_any
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge2_TT }
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = { event_target:new_recruit = { character_event = { id = lodge.3 } } }
	}
}

# lodge.3 The challenge
# Fight one of ours, or walk away. A recruit of a legendary warrior's
# bloodline is greeted as such.
# Scope: ROOT is the recruit, FROM the champion.

	id = lodge.3
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { any_owned_bloodline = { has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_legendary_warrior } }

		trigger = { event_target:champion_character = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } }

		trigger = { event_target:champion_character = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } }

	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { event_target:champion_character = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } }
		picture = GFX_evt_dwarf_fighter

		}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_owned_bloodline = { has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_legendary_warrior } }
			random_owned_bloodline = {
				limit = { has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_legendary_warrior }
				preferred_limit = { founder = { NOT = { character = ROOT } } }
				founder = { save_event_target_as = founder_target }
			}
		}
	}

	# I'll fight
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = {
				event_target:champion_character = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
			}
			text = EVTOPTAlodge3_chaos
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { event_target:champion_character = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge3_skaven
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				event_target:champion_character = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
			}
			text = EVTOPTAlodge3_greenskin
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { event_target:champion_character = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge3_ulric
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { event_target:champion_character = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge3_knight
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { event_target:champion_character = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge3_dawi
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				event_target:champion_character = {
					NOR = {
						warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
					}
				}
			}
			text = EVTOPTAlodge3_any
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge3_TT }
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:champion_character = {
				set_character_flag = they_accepted@ROOT
				character_event = { id = lodge.4 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { NOT = { is_alive = yes } } }
			reset_martial_joining_chain_effect = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}

	# I changed my mind
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = {
				event_target:champion_character = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
			}
			text = EVTOPTBlodge3_chaos
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { event_target:champion_character = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTBlodge3_skaven
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				event_target:champion_character = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
			}
			text = EVTOPTBlodge3_greenskin
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { event_target:champion_character = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTBlodge3_ulric
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { event_target:champion_character = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTBlodge3_knight
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { event_target:champion_character = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTBlodge3_dawi
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				event_target:champion_character = {
					NOR = {
						warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
					}
				}
			}
			text = EVTOPTBlodge3_any
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge3_TT }
		tooltip = {
			reverse_opinion = {
				who = event_target:champion_character
				modifier = opinion_disappointed
				years = 5
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_character_flag = society_join_block
			clr_character_flag = awaiting_initiation_trial
			event_target:champion_character = {
				set_character_flag = they_declined@ROOT
				character_event = { id = lodge.4 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = is_being_recruited_to_warband_by_parent }
		}
	}
}

# lodge.4 The champion picks a sparring partner
# A free AI member close to the recruit's skill, ideally of low rank, takes
# up the challenge. If the recruit walked away, the champion is scornful.
# Scope: ROOT is the champion, FROM the recruit.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.4
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = they_accepted@FROM } text = EVTDESClodge4_yes }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = they_declined@FROM } text = EVTDESClodge4_no }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_chaos_warband
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_skaven_war }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_greenskinanimosity
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_ulric_warriors }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_imperial_knight }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_dwarf_fighter }
	picture = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_melee
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = they_accepted@FROM }
				any_society_member = {
					limit = { same_society_as = ROOT }
					score_value = {
						value = 1
						# Low ranks first
						additive_modifier = { value = 100000 society_rank == 1 }
						additive_modifier = { value = 1000 society_rank == 2 }
						additive_modifier = { value = 10 society_rank == 3 }
						additive_modifier = { value = 1 society_rank == 4 }
						# An even match
						additive_modifier = {
							value = 1000
							OR = {
								AND = {
									duelist_skill_is_low_group_trigger = yes
									FROM = { duelist_skill_is_low_group_trigger = yes }
								}
								AND = {
									duelist_skill_is_medium_group_trigger = yes
									FROM = { duelist_skill_is_medium_group_trigger = yes }
								}
								AND = {
									duelist_skill_is_high_group_trigger = yes
									FROM = { duelist_skill_is_high_group_trigger = yes }
								}
							}
						}
						additive_modifier = {
							value = 1000
							OR = {
								AND = {
									is_the_worst_duelist_trigger = yes
									FROM = { is_the_worst_duelist_trigger = yes }
								}
								AND = {
									is_terrible_duelist_trigger = yes
									FROM = { is_terrible_duelist_trigger = yes }
								}
								AND = {
									is_bad_duelist_trigger = yes
									FROM = { is_bad_duelist_trigger = yes }
								}
							}
						}
						# New bonds can form
						additive_modifier = {
							value = 100
							NOR = { is_rival = FROM is_lover = FROM is_friend = FROM }
						}
						additive_modifier = {
							value = 100
							OR = {
								reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
									who = FROM
									modifier = opinion_hate
								}
								reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
									who = FROM
									modifier = opinion_tortured_me
								}
								reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
									who = FROM
									modifier = opinion_mutilated_me
								}
								reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
									who = FROM
									modifier = opinion_imprisoned
								}
							}
						}
						additive_modifier = {
							value = 200
							FROM = {
								PREV = { prev_is_lonely_this_romance_makes_sense_trigger = yes }
							}
						}
						# Free, healthy, AI and not the champion
						additive_modifier = { value = -5000 block_general_event_trigger = yes }
						additive_modifier = { value = -1000 character = ROOT }
						additive_modifier = { value = -1000 NOT = { ai = yes } }
						additive_modifier = {
							value = -500
							OR = {
								has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness
								has_character_modifier = severe_illness
								character_disease_trigger = yes
								has_severe_disability_trigger = yes
							}
							NOR = { religion = nurgle trait = norscan_patron_nurgleth }
						}
						additive_modifier = {
							value = -500
							OR = { age = 60 is_maimed_trigger = yes }
						}
					}
					count = 1
					save_event_target_as = duel_target
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# Show them the ropes
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge4
		trigger = { has_character_flag = they_accepted@FROM }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge4_TT }
		show_portrait = event_target:duel_target
	}

	# That little coward
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge4
		trigger = { has_character_flag = they_declined@FROM }
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_disappointed years = 5 }
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = they_accepted@FROM }
				event_target:new_recruit = { character_event = { id = lodge.5 } }
			}
			clr_character_flag = they_accepted@FROM
			clr_character_flag = they_declined@FROM
		}
	}
}

# lodge.5 The duel is set
# Sets the duel scopes and sends the challenge (duel.1 in the duel
# events). The duel's clean-up sends lodge.9.
# Scope: ROOT is the recruit.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.5
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:duel_target = { NOT = { is_alive = yes } } }
			reset_martial_joining_chain_effect = yes
		}
		set_character_flag = mock_duel
		save_event_target_as = combatant_1
		event_target:duel_target = {
			save_event_target_as = combatant_2
			character_event = { id = duel.1 }
		}
	}
}

# lodge.6 The verdict
# The champion judges the duel and writes the welcome (lodge.7) or the
# refusal (lodge.8).
# Scope: ROOT is the champion, FROM the recruit.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.6
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				NOR = {
					has_character_flag = was_mildly_injured
					has_character_flag = was_actually_injured
					has_character_flag = duel_fled
				}
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge6_great
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				has_character_flag = was_mildly_injured
				NOT = { has_character_flag = duel_fled }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge6_mild
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				has_character_flag = was_actually_injured
				NOR = { has_character_flag = was_mildly_injured has_character_flag = duel_fled }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge6_serious
	}
	desc = { trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = duel_fled } } text = EVTDESClodge6_fled }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_chaos_warband
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_skaven_war }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_greenskinanimosity
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_ulric_warriors }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_imperial_knight }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_dwarf_fighter }
	picture = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_melee
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = {
				FROM = {
					NOR = {
						has_character_flag = was_mildly_injured
						has_character_flag = was_actually_injured
						has_character_flag = duel_fled
					}
				}
			}
			text = EVTOPTAlodge6
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				FROM = {
					has_character_flag = was_mildly_injured
					NOT = { has_character_flag = duel_fled }
				}
			}
			text = EVTOPTAlodge6b
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				FROM = {
					has_character_flag = was_actually_injured
					NOR = { has_character_flag = was_mildly_injured has_character_flag = duel_fled }
				}
			}
			text = EVTOPTAlodge6c
		}
		name = { trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = duel_fled } } text = EVTOPTAlodge6d }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { NOT = { has_character_flag = duel_fled } } }
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge6_TT }
			add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
			FROM = { letter_event = { id = lodge.7 days = 5 } }
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge6_TTb }
			FROM = { letter_event = { id = lodge.8 days = 5 } }
		}
	}
}

# lodge.7 Welcome to the society

# through without a scratch may, once in a life, be given a white wolf pelt.

letter_event = {
	id = lodge.7
	desc = {

			NOR = {

				has_character_flag = was_actually_injured

	}

					warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes

				has_character_flag = was_mildly_injured
				has_character_flag = was_actually_injured

		}

				has_character_flag = was_actually_injured

	}

				has_character_flag = was_actually_injured
			}
			FROM = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes }
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge7b_dawi
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = was_mildly_injured
				has_character_flag = was_actually_injured
			}
			FROM = {
				NOR = {
					warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge7b_any
	}
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = show_once_flag }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = show_once_flag
	}

	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge7_chaos
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge7_skaven
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge7_greenskin
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge7_ulric
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge7_knight
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge7_dawi
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				FROM = {
					NOR = {
						warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
					}
				}
			}
			text = EVTOPTAlodge7_any
		}
		join_society = event_target:martial_society_to_join
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = minor_reward }
			add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = medium_reward }
			add_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				society_member_of = warriors_of_ulric
				NOR = {
					has_character_flag = was_mildly_injured
					has_character_flag = was_actually_injured
					has_character_flag = lodge_got_wolf_pelt
					has_artifact = white_wolf_pelt
				}
			}
			random = {
				chance = 10
				set_character_flag = lodge_got_wolf_pelt
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge7_pelt_TT }
				add_artifact = white_wolf_pelt
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_character_flag = was_actually_injured
			clr_character_flag = was_mildly_injured
			clr_character_flag = awaiting_initiation_trial
			clr_character_flag = duel_fled
			clr_character_flag = minor_reward
			clr_character_flag = medium_reward
			clr_character_flag = show_once_flag
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = is_being_recruited_to_warband_by_parent
				event_target:recruiting_parent = { is_alive = yes }
			}
			custom_tooltip = { text = your_parent_will_be_proud_tt }
			tooltip = {
				opinion = {
					modifier = opinion_grateful
					who = event_target:recruiting_parent
					years = 5
				}
				reverse_opinion = {
					modifier = opinion_impressed
					who = event_target:recruiting_parent
					years = 5
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = passed_initiation
			event_target:recruiting_parent = { character_event = { id = lodge.12 } }
		}
		clr_character_flag = society_join_block
	}
}

# lodge.8 Turned away
# The recruit fled the duel and may not ask again for ten years.
# Scope: ROOT is the recruit, FROM the champion.
letter_event = {
	id = lodge.8
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge8_chaos
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge8_skaven
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge8_greenskin
	}
	desc = { trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } } text = EVTDESClodge8_ulric }
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge8_knight
	}
	desc = { trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } } text = EVTDESClodge8_dawi }
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				NOR = {
					warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge8_any
	}
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge8_chaos
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge8_skaven
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge8_greenskin
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge8_ulric
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge8_knight
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { FROM = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge8_dawi
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				FROM = {
					NOR = {
						warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
						warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
					}
				}
			}
			text = EVTOPTAlodge8_any
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge8_TT }
		hidden_effect = {
			add_character_modifier = { name = warband_failed_joining_cd years = 10 hidden = yes }
			clr_character_flag = was_actually_injured
			clr_character_flag = was_mildly_injured
			clr_character_flag = awaiting_initiation_trial
			clr_character_flag = duel_fled
			clr_character_flag = minor_reward
			clr_character_flag = medium_reward
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = is_being_recruited_to_warband_by_parent
				event_target:recruiting_parent = { is_alive = yes }
			}
			custom_tooltip = { text = notify_parent_recruiter_tt }
			set_character_flag = failed_initiation
			event_target:recruiting_parent = { character_event = { id = lodge.13 } }
		}
		clr_character_flag = society_join_block
	}
}

# lodge.9 The duel is over
# Ten days after the duel the champion (or, if the champion cannot answer,
# another free champion) gives the verdict (lodge.6).
# Scope: ROOT is the recruit.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.9
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:champion_character = { block_general_event_trigger = no } }
			event_target:champion_character = { character_event = { id = lodge.6 } }
		}
		else = {
			event_target:martial_society_to_join = {
				any_society_member = {
					limit = { prisoner = no }
					score_value = {
						value = 1
						additive_modifier = { value = 10000 is_society_grandmaster = yes }
						additive_modifier = { value = 1000 society_rank == 4 }
						additive_modifier = { value = 100 society_rank == 3 }
						additive_modifier = { value = 10 society_rank == 2 }
					}
					count = 1
					save_event_target_as = lodge_free_champion
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:lodge_free_champion = { always = yes } }
				event_target:lodge_free_champion = { character_event = { id = lodge.6 } }
			}
			else = {
				event_target:martial_society_to_join = {
					leader = { character_event = { id = lodge.6 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# lodge.10 The parent's question
# From the decision warband_recruit_child (hf_warrior_lodge_decisions). An
# adult child is asked over dinner to take the trial of the parent's society.
# Scope: ROOT is the child; event target recruiting_parent.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.10
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:recruiting_parent = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge10_chaos
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:recruiting_parent = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge10_skaven
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:recruiting_parent = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge10_greenskin
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:recruiting_parent = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge10_ulric
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:recruiting_parent = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge10_knight
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:recruiting_parent = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge10_dawi
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:recruiting_parent = {
				NOR = {
					warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge10_any
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { event_target:recruiting_parent = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } }
		picture = GFX_evt_chaos_warband
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { event_target:recruiting_parent = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } }
		picture = GFX_evt_skaven_war
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { event_target:recruiting_parent = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } }
		picture = GFX_evt_greenskinanimosity
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { event_target:recruiting_parent = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } }
		picture = GFX_evt_ulric_warriors
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { event_target:recruiting_parent = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } }
		picture = GFX_evt_imperial_knight
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { event_target:recruiting_parent = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } }
		picture = GFX_evt_dwarf_fighter
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:recruiting_parent = {
				NOR = {
					warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_melee
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		event_target:martial_society_to_join = {
			any_society_member = {
				limit = { prisoner = no }
				score_value = { value = 10 warrior_hero_score = yes }
				count = 1
				save_event_target_as = champion_character
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { event_target:champion_character = { always = yes } } }
			event_target:martial_society_to_join = {
				leader = { save_event_target_as = champion_character }
			}
		}
	}

	# It's what I always wanted
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge10
		trigger = {
			is_secret_religious_society_member_trigger = no
			is_devil_worshiper_trigger = no
			NOT = { society_member_of = the_assassins }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge10_TT }
		event_target:champion_character = { character_event = { id = lodge.2 } }
		set_character_flag = accepted_trial
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 modifier = { factor = 0 is_in_society = yes } }
	}

	# I will carve my own path
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge10
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = {
					event_target:recruiting_parent = {
						has_quest = quest_warband_recruit
						quest_target = { character = ROOT }
					}
				}
				is_secret_religious_society_member_trigger = yes
				is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes
				society_member_of = the_assassins
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge10_TT }
		set_character_flag = refused_trial
		clr_character_flag = society_join_block
		clr_character_flag = awaiting_initiation_trial
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = lodge.15 days = 365 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = stubborn } }
	}

	after = {
		event_target:recruiting_parent = { character_event = { id = lodge.11 days = 1 } }
	}
}

# lodge.11 The child's answer
# Scope: ROOT is the parent, FROM the child.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.11
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = { has_character_flag = accepted_trial }
			NOT = { event_target:champion_character = { character = ROOT } }
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge11_yes
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = { has_character_flag = accepted_trial }
			event_target:champion_character = { character = ROOT }
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge11_yes_self
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = { has_character_flag = refused_trial }
			NOT = { event_target:champion_character = { character = ROOT } }
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge11_no
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = { has_character_flag = refused_trial }
			event_target:champion_character = { character = ROOT }
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge11_no_self
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_feast
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# I was waiting for you to be ready
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { event_target:champion_character = { NOT = { character = ROOT } } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge11
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { event_target:champion_character = { character = ROOT } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge11b
		}
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = accepted_trial } }
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:champion_character = { NOT = { character = ROOT } } }
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge11_TT }
			show_portrait = event_target:champion_character
		}
	}

	# Why, you ungrateful little...
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge11
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = refused_trial } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge11_TT }
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:champion_character = { NOT = { character = ROOT } } }
			show_portrait = event_target:champion_character
		}
	}

	after = {
		FROM = { clr_character_flag = accepted_trial clr_character_flag = refused_trial }
	}
}

# lodge.12 The child passed
# Scope: ROOT is the parent, FROM the child.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.12
	desc = EVTDESClodge12
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = show_only_once@FROM }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = show_only_once@FROM
	}

	# I knew you had it in you
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge12
		trigger = { same_society_as = FROM }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge12_TT }
		prestige = 20
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_grateful who = FROM years = 5 }
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_impressed who = FROM years = 5 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_quest = quest_warband_recruit quest_target = { character = FROM } }
			clr_quest = quest_warband_recruit
			add_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
		}
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
	}

	# You make me miss it (if the parent has left)
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge12
		trigger = { NOT = { same_society_as = FROM } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge12_TT }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = depressed } }
			random = { chance = 10 add_trait_depressed_effect = yes }
		}
	}

	# I'm not impressed
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTClodge12
		trigger = { NOT = { same_society_as = FROM } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTClodge12_TT }
		if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = kind trait = cruel } } add_trait = cruel }
		else_if = { limit = { trait = kind } remove_trait = kind }
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_disappointed who = FROM years = 5 }
	}

	after = {
		FROM = {
			clr_character_flag = passed_initiation
			clr_character_flag = is_being_recruited_to_warband_by_parent
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.100 } } # The young recruit
		}
		clr_character_flag = show_only_once@FROM
	}
}

# lodge.13 The child failed
# Also sent by the duel events when the child turns the duel down. A cruel
# parent of the Dark Gods, the Horned Rat or the greenskins may decide that
# weakness has no place in the bloodline.
# Scope: ROOT is the parent, FROM the child.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.13
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { has_character_flag = chickened_out } } }
		text = EVTDESClodge13_failed
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = chickened_out } }
		text = EVTDESClodge13_fled
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_feast
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = show_only_once@FROM }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = show_only_once@FROM
	}

	# I'm still proud to call you family
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge13
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge13_TT }
		if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = kind trait = cruel religion = khorne } } add_trait = kind }
		else_if = { limit = { trait = cruel NOT = { religion = khorne } } remove_trait = cruel }
		FROM = { set_character_flag = parent_was_supportive }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = cruel }
		}
	}

	# I expected more
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge13
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge13_TT }
		prestige = 20
		if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = kind trait = cruel } } add_trait = cruel }
		else_if = { limit = { trait = kind } remove_trait = kind }
		else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = honest } } add_trait = honest }
		FROM = { set_character_flag = parent_was_mean }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}

	# Weakness has no place in my blood
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTClodge13
		trigger = {
			NOT = { trait = kind }
			OR = {
				warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
				AND = {
					trait = cruel
					OR = { trait = wroth is_lunatic_trigger = yes trait = possessed }
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTClodge13_TT }
		prestige = 20
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cruel } } add_trait = cruel }
		FROM = {
			save_event_target_as = kinslayer_target
			death = { death_reason = death_murder killer = ROOT }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			add_kinslayer_trait_effect = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 2
			modifier = { factor = 5 religion = khorne }
			modifier = { factor = 3 warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 0 is_close_relative = FROM NOT = { trait = cruel } }
		}
	}

	after = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_quest = quest_warband_recruit quest_target = { character = FROM } }
			clr_quest = { id = quest_warband_recruit failure = yes }
		}
		FROM = {
			clr_character_flag = failed_initiation
			clr_character_flag = chickened_out
			clr_character_flag = is_being_recruited_to_warband_by_parent
			if = { limit = { is_alive = yes } character_event = { id = lodge.14 days = 5 } }
		}
		clr_character_flag = show_only_once@FROM
	}
}

# lodge.14 What the parent said
# Scope: ROOT is the child, FROM the parent.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.14
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = parent_was_supportive } text = EVTDESClodge14_kind }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = parent_was_mean } text = EVTDESClodge14_mean }
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	# Family comes first
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge14
		trigger = { has_character_flag = parent_was_supportive }
		if = { limit = { has_dlc = "Way of Life" } set_focus = focus_family }
		opinion = { modifier = comforted_opinion who = FROM years = 5 }
	}

	# I'm lucky to have them
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge14
		trigger = { has_character_flag = parent_was_supportive }
		opinion = { modifier = comforted_opinion who = FROM years = 5 }
	}

	# This is not the end of my story
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTClodge14
		trigger = { has_character_flag = parent_was_mean }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTClodge14_TT }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } } add_trait = stressed }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = ambitious } } add_trait = ambitious }
	}

	# I am an embarrassment
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDlodge14
		trigger = { has_character_flag = parent_was_mean }
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = parent_was_supportive
		clr_character_flag = parent_was_mean
	}
}

}

# The military path (rank 4 power of Ulric, the Knights and the Dawi)

# lodge.20 A new path
# The member already walks one path and may take another.
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.20
	desc = { trigger = { trait = duelist } text = EVTDESClodge20_duelist }
	desc = {
		trigger = { trait = strategist NOT = { trait = duelist } }
		text = EVTDESClodge20_strategist
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { trait = hunter NOR = { trait = duelist trait = strategist } }
		text = EVTDESClodge20_hunter
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_armory
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge20
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = duelist } }
		if = { limit = { trait = strategist } remove_trait = strategist }
		if = { limit = { trait = hunter } remove_trait = hunter }
		add_trait = duelist
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge20
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = strategist } }
		if = { limit = { trait = duelist } remove_trait = duelist }
		if = { limit = { trait = hunter } remove_trait = hunter }
		add_trait = strategist
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTClodge20
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = hunter } }
		if = { limit = { trait = duelist } remove_trait = duelist }
		if = { limit = { trait = strategist } remove_trait = strategist }
		add_trait = hunter
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDlodge20
		add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = choosing_military_aspect
	}
}

# lodge.21 Choose a path
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.21
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge21_ulric }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge21_knight }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge21_dawi }
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge21_any
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_armory
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# The blade
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge21
		show_trait = duelist
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = EVTOPTAlodge21_TT
			character_event = { id = lodge.22 days = 20 random = 20 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 1.1 has_duel_exp_modifier_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = brave }
		}
	}

	# The war table
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge21
		show_trait = strategist
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = EVTOPTBlodge21_TT
			character_event = { id = lodge.25 days = 20 random = 20 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_education_stewardship_trigger = yes }
		}
	}

	# The hunt
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTClodge21
		show_trait = hunter
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = EVTOPTClodge21_TT
			character_event = { id = lodge.27 days = 20 random = 20 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = falconer }
			modifier = { factor = 2 warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes }
		}
	}

	# Not now
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTD_MNM_1900
		add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = choosing_military_aspect
	}
}

# lodge.22 The way of the blade
# Ask a fellow member to teach you, hire a fencing master, or train alone.
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.22
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge22_ulric }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge22_knight }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge22_dawi }
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
				warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge22_any
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_armory
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					ai = yes
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { block_general_event_trigger = yes }
					trait = duelist
				}
				save_event_target_as = mentor_target
			}
		}
	}

	# Ask a fellow member
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge22
		trigger = { event_target:mentor_target = { always = yes } }
		set_character_flag = opt_A
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = event_target:mentor_target
						modifier = opinion_budding_friendship
					}
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = event_target:mentor_target
						modifier = opinion_budding_friendship
					}
				}
			}
			tooltip = { add_friend = event_target:mentor_target }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:mentor_target = {
					potentially_interested_in_rivaling_root_trigger = no
				}
			}
			tooltip = {
				reverse_opinion = {
					who = event_target:mentor_target
					modifier = opinion_was_praised
					years = 10
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			tooltip = {
				reverse_opinion = {
					who = event_target:mentor_target
					modifier = opinion_strangely_flattered
					years = 10
				}
			}
		}
		prestige = -25
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:mentor_target = { is_alive = yes } }
			event_target:mentor_target = { character_event = { id = lodge.23 } }
		}
		else = {
			clr_character_flag = choosing_military_aspect
			add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 2 has_pleasant_personality_trigger = yes } }
	}

	# Hire a master
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge22
		set_character_flag = opt_B
		scaled_wealth = { value = -0.4 min = -40 }
		add_trait_silently_diligent_effect = yes
		remove_trait_silently_slothful_effect = yes
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = rigorous_practice duration = 3650 }
		hidden_effect = {
			clear_event_target = mentor_target
			character_event = { id = lodge.24 days = 60 random = 30 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { wealth = 50 } }
		}
	}

	# Alone
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTClodge22
		set_character_flag = opt_C
		hidden_effect = {
			clear_event_target = mentor_target
			character_event = { id = lodge.24 days = 90 random = 30 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# lodge.23 A fellow asks for lessons
# Scope: ROOT is the mentor, FROM the pupil.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.23
	desc = EVTDESClodge23
	picture = GFX_evt_teaching_moment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
		is_alive = yes
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_alive = yes } }
			FROM = {
				clr_character_flag = choosing_military_aspect
				add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge23
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = duelist_mentor duration = 730 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge23
		scaled_wealth = { value = -0.2 min = -20 }
	}

	after = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = FROM
						modifier = opinion_budding_friendship
					}
					has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_budding_friendship }
				}
			}
			add_friend = FROM
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROM = { root_potentially_interested_in_rivaling_this_trigger = no } }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_was_praised years = 10 }
		}
		else = {
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_strangely_flattered years = 10 }
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.24 days = 60 random = 30 } }
	}
}

# lodge.24 A duelist
# Scope: ROOT is the member, FROM the mentor (path A).
character_event = {
	id = lodge.24
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = opt_A FROM = { NOT = { is_alive = yes } } }
		text = EVTDESClodge24_mentor_died
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = opt_A FROM = { is_alive = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge24_mentor
	}
	desc = { trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = opt_A } } text = EVTDESClodge24 }
	picture = GFX_evt_duel_team
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = opt_A } text = EVTOPTAlodge24 }
		name = {
			trigger = { has_character_flag = opt_B NOT = { has_character_flag = opt_A } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge24b
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { NOR = { has_character_flag = opt_A has_character_flag = opt_B } }
			text = EVTOPTAlodge24c
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = { limit = { lifestyle_traits = 1 } remove_lifestyle_trait_effect = yes }
		}
		add_trait = duelist
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = choosing_military_aspect
		clr_character_flag = opt_A
		clr_character_flag = opt_B
		clr_character_flag = opt_C
	}
}

			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = erudite }
		}

# lodge.30 Training complete
# Ulric's warriors become Fearsome; pirates and corsairs pick plunder,
# raiding or slaving. A member who does not share the lodge's faith loses
# piety.
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.30
	desc = { trigger = { society_member_of = warriors_of_ulric } text = EVTDESClodge30_ulric }
	desc = {
		trigger = { society_member_of = adventurers_druchii_corsairs }
		text = EVTDESClodge30_corsair
	}
	desc = { trigger = { society_member_of = adventurers_pirates } text = EVTDESClodge30_pirate }
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				society_member_of = warriors_of_ulric
				society_member_of = adventurers_druchii_corsairs
				society_member_of = adventurers_pirates
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge30_any
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { society_member_of = adventurers_druchii_corsairs }
		picture = GFX_evt_dark_elf_corsair
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { society_member_of = adventurers_pirates }
		picture = GFX_evt_pirate_crew
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { society_member_of = warriors_of_ulric }
		picture = GFX_evt_ulric_warriors
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				society_member_of = warriors_of_ulric
				society_member_of = adventurers_druchii_corsairs
				society_member_of = adventurers_pirates
			}
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_teaching_moment
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { select_and_save_fellow_society_member_effect = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge30
		trigger = { is_member_of_any_piratic_society_trigger = no }
		if = {
			limit = { has_religion_matching_joined_warrior_society_trigger = no }
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge30_piety_TT }
			piety = -150
		}
		add_warrior_lodge_leader_trait_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge30
		trigger = { is_member_of_any_piratic_society_trigger = yes }
		add_trait = plunderer_leader
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTClodge30
		trigger = { is_member_of_any_piratic_society_trigger = yes }
		add_trait = reaver_leader
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDlodge30
		trigger = { is_member_of_any_piratic_society_trigger = yes }
		add_trait = slaver_leader
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = choosing_warrior_training
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Toughness (rank 1 power of Gork and Mork)
###############################################################################

# lodge.35 Too tough to stay hurt
# The worst wound closes. Greenskins heal like no other race.
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.35
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = removed_wounded } text = EVTDESClodge35_wounded }
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = removed_severely_injured }
		text = EVTDESClodge35_severe
	}
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = removed_maimed } text = EVTDESClodge35_maimed }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = removed_mangled } text = EVTDESClodge35_mangled }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = removed_infection } text = EVTDESClodge35_infection }
	picture = GFX_evt_greenskinanimosity
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				1 = { trigger = { trait = wounded } set_character_flag = removed_wounded }
				100 = {
					trigger = { trait = severely_injured }
					set_character_flag = removed_severely_injured
				}
				100 = { trigger = { trait = maimed } set_character_flag = removed_maimed }
				1000 = { trigger = { trait = mangled } set_character_flag = removed_mangled }
				100 = { trigger = { trait = infection } set_character_flag = removed_infection }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = pending_infection }
				clr_character_flag = pending_infection
			}
		}
	}

	# Good as new
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge35
		lodge_toughness_heal_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = swollen_wrist }
			remove_character_modifier = swollen_wrist
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = black_eye }
			remove_character_modifier = black_eye
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = swollen_ankle }
			remove_character_modifier = swollen_ankle
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = concussion }
			remove_character_modifier = concussion
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = cracked_ribs }
			remove_character_modifier = cracked_ribs
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = badly_bruised }
			remove_character_modifier = badly_bruised
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = sore_muscles }
			remove_character_modifier = sore_muscles
		}
		add_scarred_tiered_effect = yes
	}

	# Gork (or Mork) is wiv me
	option = {
		name = { trigger = { society_member_of = warband_gork } text = EVTOPTBlodge35_gork }
		name = {
			trigger = { NOT = { society_member_of = warband_gork } }
			text = EVTOPTBlodge35_mork
		}
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = zealous } }
		piety = 5
		lodge_toughness_heal_effect = yes
		add_trait = zealous
		add_scarred_tiered_effect = yes
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				10 = { sound_effect = toughness_01 }
				10 = { sound_effect = toughness_02 }
				10 = { sound_effect = toughness_03 }
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = using_toughness
		clr_character_flag = removed_wounded
		clr_character_flag = removed_severely_injured
		clr_character_flag = removed_maimed
		clr_character_flag = removed_mangled
		clr_character_flag = removed_infection
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Berserk rage (rank 1 power of Ulric's warriors and the Norscans)
###############################################################################

# lodge.40 The rage in the hall
# One time in five someone gets in the way: a loved one, a courtier or a
# fellow member. Killing them makes the berserker; sparing them makes the
# berserker gentler.
# Scope: ROOT is the member.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.40
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = no_harm event_target:fellow_member = { always = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge40_fellow
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = no_harm
			event_target:my_beloved = { always = yes }
			NOT = { event_target:fellow_member = { always = yes } }
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge40_beloved
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = no_harm
			NOR = {
				event_target:fellow_member = { always = yes }
				event_target:my_beloved = { always = yes }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge40_alone
	}
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = someone_is_hurt } text = EVTDESClodge40_hurt }
	picture = GFX_evt_duel_team
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	portrait = event_target:kinslayer_target

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				random_list = {
					10 = { sound_effect = berserk_female_01 }
					10 = { sound_effect = berserk_female_02 }
					10 = { sound_effect = berserk_female_03 }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					10 = { sound_effect = berserk_male_01 }
					10 = { sound_effect = berserk_male_02 }
					10 = { sound_effect = berserk_male_03 }
				}
			}
			# Who stands closest?
			random = {
				chance = 50
				any_courtier_or_vassal = {
					limit = { is_adult = yes ai = yes prisoner = no }
					score_value = {
						value = 1
						beloved_target_selection_score = yes
						bad_soldier_outcome_score = yes
					}
					count = 1
					save_event_target_as = my_beloved
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { event_target:my_beloved = { always = yes } } }
				society = {
					any_society_member = {
						limit = { NOT = { character = ROOT } ai = yes prisoner = no }
						score_value = { value = 10 bad_soldier_outcome_score = yes }
						count = 1
						save_event_target_as = fellow_member
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						event_target:my_beloved = { always = yes }
						event_target:fellow_member = { always = yes }
					}
				}
				any_courtier_or_vassal = {
					limit = { is_adult = yes ai = yes prisoner = no }
					score_value = { value = 1 beloved_target_selection_score = yes }
					count = 1
					save_event_target_as = my_beloved
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:fellow_member = { always = yes } }
				event_target:fellow_member = { save_event_target_as = kinslayer_target }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { event_target:my_beloved = { always = yes } }
				event_target:my_beloved = { save_event_target_as = kinslayer_target }
			}
			# Does anyone get hurt?
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:kinslayer_target = { always = yes } }
				random_list = {
					2 = { set_character_flag = someone_is_hurt }
					8 = { set_character_flag = no_harm }
				}
			}
			else = { set_character_flag = no_harm }
		}
	}

	# I am stronger than ever
	option = {
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = no_harm } text = EVTOPTAlodge40 }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = someone_is_hurt } text = EVTOPTAlodge40b }
		prestige = 15
		add_trait = berserker
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = someone_is_hurt }
			event_target:kinslayer_target = {
				show_scope_change = no
				hidden_effect = {
					lodge_find_avenger_effect = yes
					event_target:potential_rival = { character_event = { id = lodge.41 days = 3 } }
				}
				death = { death_reason = death_berserker_rage killer = ROOT }
			}
			add_kinslayer_trait_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { event_target:kinslayer_target = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } } }
			reverse_opinion = {
				who = event_target:kinslayer_target
				modifier = opinion_impressed_infatuated
				years = 10
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { event_target:kinslayer_target = { always = yes } }
			reverse_opinion = {
				who = event_target:kinslayer_target
				modifier = opinion_impressed
				years = 5
			}
		}
	}

	# Whose blood is this?
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge40
		trigger = { has_character_flag = someone_is_hurt NOT = { trait = kind } }
		add_trait = berserker
		event_target:kinslayer_target = { show_scope_change = no add_maimed_trait_effect = yes }
		if = { limit = { trait = cruel } remove_trait = cruel }
		else = { add_trait = kind }
		lodge_rage_lunacy_effect = yes
	}

	# Someone help us (kind)
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTClodge40
		trigger = { has_character_flag = someone_is_hurt trait = kind }
		tooltip_info = kind
		add_trait = berserker
		event_target:kinslayer_target = { show_scope_change = no add_maimed_trait_effect = yes }
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = no_harm
		clr_character_flag = someone_is_hurt
		clr_character_flag = going_berserk
	}
}

# lodge.43 The rage in the cell
# A prisoner in a dungeon or oubliette goes berserk: half the time they only
# hurt themselves, otherwise they kill someone and break out.
# Scope: ROOT is the prisoner.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.43
	title = EVTNAMElodge43
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = hurt_self
			event_target:crossfire_target = { prisoner = no }
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge43_jailer
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = hurt_self
			event_target:crossfire_target = { prisoner = yes }
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge43_cellmate
	}
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = someone_is_hurt } text = EVTDESClodge43_escape }
	picture = GFX_evt_jailbreak
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	portrait = event_target:kinslayer_target

	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = yes

	trigger = {
		is_adult = yes
		OR = { has_character_modifier = the_oubliette has_character_modifier = the_dungeon }
		host = {
			any_courtier = {
				NOT = { character = PREV }
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				NOT = { is_friend = ROOT }
				ai = yes
				OR = { prisoner = yes NOR = { is_lover = PREV is_friend = PREV is_rival = PREV } }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				random_list = {
					10 = { sound_effect = berserk_female_01 }
					10 = { sound_effect = berserk_female_02 }
					10 = { sound_effect = berserk_female_03 }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					10 = { sound_effect = berserk_male_01 }
					10 = { sound_effect = berserk_male_02 }
					10 = { sound_effect = berserk_male_03 }
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				5 = { set_character_flag = someone_is_hurt }
				5 = { set_character_flag = hurt_self }
			}
			host = { save_event_target_as = host_target }
			random_list = {
				# A fellow prisoner
				100 = {
					trigger = {
						host = {
							any_courtier = {
								NOT = { character = PREV }
								NOT = { character = ROOT }
								NOT = { trait = incapable }
								prisoner = yes
								NOT = { is_friend = ROOT }
								ai = yes
							}
						}
					}
					host = {
						random_courtier = {
							limit = {
								NOT = { character = ROOT }
								NOT = { character = PREV }
								NOT = { trait = incapable }
								prisoner = yes
								NOT = { is_friend = ROOT }
								ai = yes
							}
							preferred_limit = { is_unimportant_trigger = yes }
							save_event_target_as = crossfire_target
						}
					}
				}
				# Someone unimportant who came to look
				10 = {
					trigger = {
						host = {
							any_courtier = {
								NOT = { character = PREV }
								NOT = { character = ROOT }
								NOT = { trait = incapable }
								prisoner = no
								NOT = { is_friend = ROOT }
								NOR = { is_lover = PREV is_friend = PREV is_rival = PREV }
								ai = yes
							}
						}
					}
					host = {
						random_courtier = {
							limit = {
								NOT = { character = ROOT }
								NOT = { character = PREV }
								NOT = { trait = incapable }
								prisoner = no
								NOT = { is_friend = ROOT }
								NOR = { is_lover = PREV is_friend = PREV is_rival = PREV }
								ai = yes
							}
							preferred_limit = { is_unimportant_trigger = yes }
							save_event_target_as = crossfire_target
						}
					}
				}
			}
			event_target:crossfire_target = { save_event_target_as = kinslayer_target }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = {
				has_character_flag = hurt_self
				event_target:crossfire_target = { prisoner = no }
			}
			text = EVTOPTAlodge43
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				has_character_flag = hurt_self
				event_target:crossfire_target = { prisoner = yes }
			}
			text = EVTOPTAlodge43b
		}
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = someone_is_hurt } text = EVTOPTAlodge43c }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = hurt_self
				event_target:crossfire_target = { prisoner = no }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { trait = one_handed } }
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						add_trait = one_handed
						hidden_effect = {
							add_trait = severely_injured
							resolve_severely_injured_effect = yes
						}
					}
					50 = { add_character_modifier = { modifier = swollen_wrist months = 12 } }
				}
			}
			else = { add_trait = severely_injured resolve_severely_injured_effect = yes }
			event_target:kinslayer_target = { show_scope_change = no add_maimed_trait_effect = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = hurt_self
				event_target:crossfire_target = { prisoner = yes }
			}
			add_maimed_trait_effect = yes
			event_target:kinslayer_target = { show_scope_change = no add_maimed_trait_effect = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = someone_is_hurt }
			custom_tooltip = { text = you_break_out_of_prison_tt prisoner = no }
			event_target:kinslayer_target = {
				show_scope_change = no
				hidden_effect = {
					lodge_find_avenger_effect = yes
					event_target:potential_rival = { character_event = { id = lodge.44 days = 3 } }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { event_target:potential_rival = { always = yes } }
					if = {
						limit = {
							event_target:potential_rival = { character = event_target:host_target }
						}
						event_target:potential_rival = {
							set_character_flag = i_am_hurt_and_original_captor
						}
					}
					else = {
						event_target:host_target = { character_event = { id = lodge.44 days = 1 } }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { prisoner = yes }
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = fellow_prisoner_dies_tt
						death = { death_reason = death_berserker_rage killer = ROOT }
					}
				}
				else = {
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = courtier_dies_tt
						death = { death_reason = death_berserker_rage killer = ROOT }
					}
				}
			}
			add_kinslayer_trait_effect = yes
		}
		prestige = 15
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = berserker } } add_trait = berserker }
	}

	# Are you hurt?
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge43
		trigger = { has_character_flag = someone_is_hurt NOT = { trait = kind } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = berserker } } add_trait = berserker }
		if = { limit = { trait = cruel } remove_trait = cruel }
		else = { add_trait = kind }
		event_target:kinslayer_target = { show_scope_change = no add_maimed_trait_effect = yes }
		lodge_rage_lunacy_effect = yes
	}

	# Someone help us (kind)
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTClodge40
		trigger = { has_character_flag = someone_is_hurt trait = kind }
		tooltip_info = kind
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = berserker } } add_trait = berserker }
		event_target:kinslayer_target = { show_scope_change = no add_maimed_trait_effect = yes }
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = hurt_self
		clr_character_flag = someone_is_hurt
		clr_character_flag = going_berserk
	}
}
