# lodge.50 The lodges look for recruits
# From on_five_year_pulse. Two AI lords and one other AI character of an
# independent realm whose lodge needs members are sent to join (lodge.51);
# so is the ruler, if AI.
# Scope: ROOT is an independent ruler.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.50
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	only_independent = yes

	trigger = {
		is_alive = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		any_realm_lord = {
			limit = {
				lodge_recruit_candidate_trigger = yes
				higher_tier_than = BARON
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
			}
			score_value = { value = 10 warrior_recruit_score = yes }
			count = 2
			character_event = { id = lodge.51 }
		}
		any_realm_character = {
			limit = { lodge_recruit_candidate_trigger = yes }
			score_value = { value = 10 warrior_recruit_score = yes }
			count = 1
			character_event = { id = lodge.51 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { lodge_recruit_candidate_trigger = yes }
			character_event = { id = lodge.51 }
		}
	}
}

# lodge.51 An AI recruit joins
# Ulric's faithful join the Warriors of Ulric (now and then the Knights);
# Kislevites join the Circle of the Bear; the other Old World faithful join
# the Knightly Orders or, if their temper is wild, the Warriors of Ulric;
# the Dawi join the Warriors Guild. The duel leaves a mark.
# Scope: ROOT is the recruit.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.51
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = { religion = ulrican trait = norscan_patron_ulric }
				can_join_society = warriors_of_ulric
			}
			random_list = {
				25 = { join_society = warriors_of_ulric }
				5 = {
					trigger = { can_join_society = knights_of_old_world }
					modifier = { factor = 2 trait = honest }
					modifier = { factor = 2 trait = chaste }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = wroth }
					join_society = knights_of_old_world
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { religion = kislev_gods can_join_society = circle_of_the_bear }
			join_society = circle_of_the_bear
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				religion_group = old_world_group
				OR = {
					can_join_society = knights_of_old_world
					can_join_society = warriors_of_ulric
				}
			}
			# The Knightly Orders take the proud, the pious and the
			# well-mannered; Ulric's warriors take the wild ones.
			random_list = {
				20 = {
					trigger = { can_join_society = knights_of_old_world }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = honest }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = proud }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = zealous }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 trait = chaste }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 trait = duelist }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 trait = strategist }
					modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = wroth }
					modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = gluttonous }
					join_society = knights_of_old_world
				}
				5 = {
					trigger = { can_join_society = warriors_of_ulric }
					modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wroth }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = hunter }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = berserker }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 trait = gluttonous }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 trait = cynical }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = zealous }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = craven }
					join_society = warriors_of_ulric
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { religion_group = dwarf_gods_group can_join_society = warriors_guild_dawi }
			join_society = warriors_guild_dawi
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_society = yes }
			apply_random_minor_injury_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					society = { NOT = { any_society_member = { is_society_grandmaster = yes } } }
				}
				set_society_grandmaster = yes
				add_society_currency_massive_effect = yes
			}
			society = {
				any_society_member = {
					limit = { ai = no society_rank = 4 }
					character_event = { id = lodge.52 days = 1 random = 15 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# lodge.53 A fallen lodge rises again
# From on_society_destroyed. A fitting courtier or vassal of the last member
# (or of their liege) refounds the lodge and leads it.
# Scope: ROOT is the last member; FROM is the society.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.53
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { this_society_is_a_warrior_lodge_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = warrior_lodge_to_revive }
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = yes }
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				limit = { lodge_revive_candidate_trigger = yes }
				score_value = {
					value = 10
					warrior_recruit_score = yes
					warrior_lodge_revival_selection_score = yes
				}
				count = 1
				lodge_revive_effect = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			liege = {
				any_courtier_or_vassal = {
					limit = { lodge_revive_candidate_trigger = yes }
					score_value = {
						value = 10
						warrior_recruit_score = yes
						warrior_lodge_revival_selection_score = yes
					}
					count = 1
					lodge_revive_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# lodge.54 AI members climb the ranks
# From on_society_bi_yearly_pulse. Also resets the Legendary Gathering after
# a century.
# Scope: an AI member of a lodge or warband.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.54
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	ai = yes
	is_in_society = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { is_member_of_any_warrior_lodge_trigger = yes is_member_of_any_warband_trigger = yes }
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		society_can_rank_up = yes
		has_society_currency_minor_trigger = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		society = { save_event_target_as = my_warrior_lodge }
		random_list = {
			50 = { }
			50 = {
				trigger = { society_rank < 3 }
				society_rank_up = 1
				detract_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
			}
			500 = {
				trigger = {
					society_rank == 3
					NOT = {
						is_society_rank_full = { society = event_target:my_warrior_lodge rank = 4 }
					}
				}
				society_rank_up = 1
				detract_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:my_warrior_lodge = {
					had_flag = { flag = used_legendary_gathering years = 100 }
				}
			}
			event_target:my_warrior_lodge = {
				clr_flag = used_legendary_gathering
				clr_flag = block_society_progress
				set_society_progress = 0
			}
		}
	}
}

# lodge.60 Fight for the seat
# From the rank-up decision (mnm_society_decisions) when every Hero seat is
# taken: a rank-3 player may challenge a Hero (duel in duel.10).
# Scope: ROOT is the player; event target my_society.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.60
	title = proving_your_worth_title
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:combatant_2 = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge60_chaos
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:combatant_2 = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge60_skaven
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:combatant_2 = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge60_greenskin
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:combatant_2 = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge60_ulric
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:combatant_2 = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge60_knight
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:combatant_2 = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESClodge60_dawi
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:combatant_2 = {
				NOR = {
					warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
					warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge60_any
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes
	ai = no

	portrait = event_target:combatant_2

	trigger = {
		is_alive = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		event_target:my_society = {
			any_society_member = {
				limit = {
					prisoner = no
					society_rank == 4
					ai = yes
					NOT = { trait = incapable }
					NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
				}
				score_value = { value = 10 bad_soldier_outcome_score = yes }
				count = 1
				save_event_target_as = combatant_2
			}
		}
		save_event_target_as = combatant_1
	}

	# I have been itching for a fight
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge60
		change_society_currency = -2000
		set_character_flag = fighting_to_rank_up
		character_event = { id = duel.10 }
	}

	# You misunderstand me
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge60
		clr_character_flag = has_sent_request_to_rank_up
	}
}

# lodge.61 The duel is over
# Sent by the duel (rank_up_to_hero_level_effect) to the Hero or, if the
# Hero died, another Hero, who writes the verdict.
# Scope: ROOT is the Hero who answers.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.61
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = may_join_society }
			clr_character_flag = may_join_society
			event_target:combatant_1 = { letter_event = { id = lodge.62 } }
		}
		else = { event_target:combatant_1 = { letter_event = { id = lodge.63 } } }
	}
}

# lodge.62 The seat is yours
# The beaten Hero steps down and yields one ordinary artifact as the spoils
# of the duel, if they have one the winner lacks. Once in a life, the
# Hero's household may follow the new Hero.
# Scope: ROOT is the winner, FROM the Hero who writes.
letter_event = {
	id = lodge.62
	desc = { trigger = { event_target:combatant_2 = { character = FROM } } text = EVTDESClodge62 }
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:combatant_2 = { NOT = { character = FROM } } }
		text = EVTDESClodge62_dead
	}
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes
	ai = no

	trigger = {
		is_alive = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge62
		society_rank_up = 1
		clr_character_flag = has_sent_request_to_rank_up
		clr_character_flag = fighting_to_rank_up
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { FROM = { character = event_target:combatant_2 } }
				FROM = { society_rank_down = 1 }
			}
		}
		# The spoils of the duel: the beaten Hero yields one ordinary artifact
		# (no unique, crown, society or holy item), war gear first.
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					character = event_target:combatant_2
					any_artifact = { lodge_hero_spoils_artifact_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				random_artifact = {
					limit = { lodge_hero_spoils_artifact_trigger = yes }
					preferred_limit = {
						OR = { has_artifact_flag = combat_weapon has_artifact_flag = armor }
						quality = 3
					}
					preferred_limit = {
						OR = { has_artifact_flag = combat_weapon has_artifact_flag = armor }
					}
					preferred_limit = { quality = 3 }
					preferred_limit = { quality = 2 }
					save_event_target_as = lodge_hero_spoils
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = { text = lodge_hero_spoils_tt }
			event_target:lodge_hero_spoils = {
				show_scope_change = no
				transfer_artifact = { from = FROM to = ROOT }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = yes NOT = { has_character_flag = lodge_got_household } }
			random = {
				chance = 25
				set_character_flag = lodge_got_household
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.64 days = 15 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

	}
}

###############################################################################
# Scars and tattoos
###############################################################################

# lodge.65 Battle scars and lodge tattoos at the start (hidden)
# On a new game (not a save): a scarred character gets the scars shown on
# the portrait, and a landless veteran or Hero of the Warriors of Ulric or
# the Norscan war-bands gets the lodge's tattoos. Scope: every character.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.65
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_save_game = no
		OR = {
			trait = scarred
			trait = scarred_mid
			trait = scarred_high
			lodge_tattoo_candidate_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { trait = scarred trait = scarred_mid trait = scarred_high } }
			add_scarred_start_effect = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { lodge_tattoo_candidate_trigger = yes } lodge_tattoo_effect = yes }
	}
}

# lodge.66 Lodge tattoos (hidden)
# Sometimes sent when a veteran finishes a lodge mission: the Warriors of
# Ulric ink the wolf on their veterans and Heroes, the Norscans their own
# marks. Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.66
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		lodge_tattoo_candidate_trigger = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		lodge_tattoo_effect = yes
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The clans muster (the Warriors Guild of the Dawi, rank 2)
###############################################################################

# lodge.70 The clans muster (hidden)
# From warrior_lodge_slavic_stand_together: picks a landless brother of
# the guild, brave and of the caller's clan if possible, to bring a
# throng to the war. Scope: the caller.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.70
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_society_member = {
			limit = {
				is_ruler = no
				religion = ROOT
				is_unimportant_trigger = yes
				NOR = {
					host = { character = ROOT }
					any_spouse = { is_ruler = yes }
					prisoner = yes
					any_close_relative = { ai = no }
					host = { ai = no }
					is_heir = ROOT
					trait = incapable
					is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
					in_command = yes
					has_minor_title = title_commander
					has_character_flag = no_court_invites
				}
			}
			preferred_limit = { trait = brave trait = stubborn dynasty = ROOT }
			preferred_limit = { trait = brave trait = stubborn }
			preferred_limit = { trait = brave dynasty = ROOT }
			preferred_limit = { trait = brave }
			preferred_limit = { martial = 12 }
			save_event_target_as = scoped_slavic_savior
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { always = yes } }
			character_event = { id = lodge.71 }
		}
		else = {
			change_society_currency = 500
			remove_character_modifier = lodge_slavic_stand_together_cooldown
		}
	}
}

# lodge.71 The throng arrives
# A thane of the guild brings a throng of warriors, thunderers and
# hammerers to the capital and takes a commander's post for the war. They
# may grow fond of each other: love (lodge.72) or friendship (lodge.76).
# Scope: the caller; event_target:scoped_slavic_savior.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.71
	desc = EVTDESClodge71
	picture = GFX_evt_dwarf_fighter
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			33 = { sound_effect = stand_together_01 }
			33 = { sound_effect = stand_together_02 }
			33 = { sound_effect = stand_together_03 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge71
		event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { move_character = ROOT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_realm_character = {
				limit = { has_minor_title = title_commander }
				preferred_limit = { NOT = { martial = 10 } }
				preferred_limit = { NOT = { martial = 15 } }
				remove_title = title_commander
			}
			event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { give_minor_title = title_commander }
		}
		capital_scope = {
			show_scope_change = no
			ROOT = {
				show_scope_change = no
				spawn_unit = {
					province = PREV
					home = PREV
					owner = THIS
					leader = event_target:scoped_slavic_savior
					match_character = THIS
					match_mult = 0.15
					troops = {
						heavy_infantry = { 125 125 }
						light_infantry = { 50 50 }
						pikemen = { 125 125 }
					}
					reinforces = yes
					reinforce_rate_multiplier = 0.1
					merge = yes
					attrition = 1.0
					earmark = lodge_stand_together
					disband_on_peace = yes
					can_toggle_looting = no
				}
				spawn_unit = {
					province = PREV
					home = PREV
					owner = THIS
					match_character = THIS
					match_mult = 0.15
					troops = {
						heavy_infantry = { 125 125 }
						light_infantry = { 50 50 }
						archers = { 125 125 }
					}
					reinforces = yes
					reinforce_rate_multiplier = 0.1
					merge = yes
					attrition = 1.0
					earmark = lodge_stand_together
					disband_on_peace = yes
					can_toggle_looting = no
				}
				spawn_unit = {
					province = PREV
					home = PREV
					owner = THIS
					match_character = THIS
					match_mult = 0.15
					troops = {
						heavy_infantry = { 125 125 }
						light_infantry = { 50 50 }
						gunpowder = { 125 125 }
					}
					reinforces = yes
					reinforce_rate_multiplier = 0.1
					merge = yes
					attrition = 1.0
					earmark = lodge_stand_together
					disband_on_peace = yes
					can_toggle_looting = no
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = { #Love
					trigger = {
						NOT = { num_of_lovers = 3 }
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { NOT = { num_of_lovers = 3 } }
						opinion = { who = event_target:scoped_slavic_savior value = -20 }
						NOT = { is_close_relative = event_target:scoped_slavic_savior }
						OR = {
							AND = {
								prefers_men_trigger = yes
								event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { is_female = no }
							}
							AND = {
								prefers_men_trigger = no
								event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
							}
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gregarious }
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { trait = gregarious }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = shy }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { trait = shy } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.25
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { martial = 15 }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.25
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { martial = 20 }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = envious }
					modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = cruel }
					modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = paranoid }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.75
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.75
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { trait = inbred }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { age = 150 } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { has_fair_trait_trigger = yes }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { trait = strong }
					}
					character_event = { id = lodge.72 days = 30 random = 15 }
				}
				25 = { #Friendship
					trigger = {
						NOT = { num_of_friends = 3 }
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { NOT = { num_of_friends = 3 } }
						opinion = { who = event_target:scoped_slavic_savior value = -20 }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gregarious }
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { trait = gregarious }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = shy }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { trait = shy } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.25
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { martial = 15 }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.25
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { martial = 20 }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						trait = drunkard
						event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { trait = drunkard }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = envious }
					modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = paranoid }
					character_event = { id = lodge.76 days = 30 random = 15 }
				}
				50 = { }
			}
		}
	}
}

		tooltip = { if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no event_target:scoped_slavic_savior = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = event_target:scoped_slavic_savior } }

		name = EVTOPTBlodge72
	}
}

# lodge.73 The thane's answer
# The thane returns the feeling (lodge.74) or does not (lodge.75).
# Scope: the thane; FROM is the caller.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.73
	desc = EVTDESClodge73
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge73
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { prefers_men_trigger = yes FROM = { is_female = no } }
				AND = { prefers_men_trigger = no FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no FROM = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = FROM }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no ROOT = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = ROOT } character_event = { id = lodge.74 } } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge73
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				AND = { prefers_men_trigger = yes FROM = { is_female = no } }
				AND = { prefers_men_trigger = no FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.75 } } }
	}
}

# lodge.80 The call to war
# From knights_call_of_the_war (rank 3, the warrior lodges and the Chaos
# warbands): the realm's levies answer faster for two years.
# Scope: the caller.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.80
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge80_ulric }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge80_knight }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge80_dawi }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge80_chaos }

	}

# lodge.81 The knightly oath
# From pledge_to_knighthood (the Knightly Orders, rank 2): swear to one of
# the orders of the Old World. Which orders are open depends on the
# region of the capital and the knight's faith. A knight who finds none
# may wait and ask again. Scope: the knight.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.81
	title = THE_KNIGHTLY_BROTHERHOOD
	desc = EVTDESClodge81
	picture = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				culture_group = estalian_group
				culture_group = tilean_group
				culture_group = kislevite_group
			}
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_imperial_knight
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { OR = { culture_group = estalian_group culture_group = tilean_group } }
		picture = GFX_evt_knighs_black_rose
	}
	picture = { trigger = { culture_group = kislevite_group } picture = GFX_evt_battle_kislev_gods }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #The Reiksguard
		name = EVTOPTlodge81_reiksguard
		trigger = { capital_scope = { region = world_old_world_empire_south } }
		add_character_modifier = { name = reiksguard_knight duration = -1 }
		set_character_flag = chose_knighthood_to_pledge_to
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 1.1 OR = { trait = ambitious trait = proud } }
		}
	}
	option = { #The Knights Panther
		name = EVTOPTlodge81_panther
		trigger = {
			capital_scope = {
				region = world_old_world_empire
				NOT = { region = world_old_world_empire_south }
			}
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = knight_of_panther duration = -1 }
		set_character_flag = chose_knighthood_to_pledge_to

###############################################################################
# The young recruit
###############################################################################

# lodge.100 The young recruit (hidden)
# From lodge.12 (a child pushed into the society by a parent passed the
# trial) and after each event of this section, until the child is 20.
# A child who never wanted to fight complains first (lodge.125). Otherwise
# the parent hears that the training crowds out the child's studies, court
# manners, wits or looks, or a Hero of the society offers to teach the
# child courage, fitness or swordplay. About one event a year.
# Scope: the child; event_target:recruiting_parent, martial_society_to_join.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.100
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		lodge_young_recruit_trigger = yes
		event_target:recruiting_parent = { same_society_as = ROOT NOT = { trait = incapable } }
	}

	immediate = {
		# First time only: did the child want to fight at all?
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					has_education_martial_trigger = yes
					has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes
					trait = brave
					trait = rowdy
					has_focus = focus_ch_struggle
					has_focus = focus_ed_martial
					has_focus = focus_war
					has_focus = focus_hunting
					has_character_flag = flag_child_is_a_warrior
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				20 = { # Never wanted this
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = wroth }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = haughty }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = slothful }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = indolent }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = fussy }
					modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = timid }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = shy }
					modifier = { factor = 2 trait = craven }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = feeble }
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = sturdy }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = robust }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = weak }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = strong }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 NOT = { martial = 6 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.75 NOT = { martial = 3 } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						is_woman_trigger = yes
						NOR = {
							has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
							has_religion_feature = religion_equal
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						is_female = no
						has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
					}
					character_event = { id = lodge.125 days = 150 random = 150 }
				}
				80 = {
					set_character_flag = flag_child_is_a_warrior
					lodge_child_training_pick_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_character_flag = flag_child_is_a_warrior
			lodge_child_training_pick_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge101
		tooltip = { FROM = { lodge_child_stress_effect = yes } }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.103 } } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
}

# lodge.119 Training with a Hero
# Scope: the child; FROM is the parent; event_target:child_hero_mentor.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.119
	title = EVTTITLElodge119
	desc = { trigger = { trait = sturdy } text = EVTDESClodge119_stout }
	desc = { trigger = { trait = feeble } text = EVTDESClodge119_feeble }
	picture = GFX_evt_guardian
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:child_hero_mentor

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge119
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } }
			random = { chance = 15 add_trait = stressed }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = sturdy }
			remove_trait = sturdy
			hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.120 days = 50 } } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { trait = feeble }
			remove_trait = feeble
			hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.121 days = 50 } } }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_focus = focus_ch_struggle }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 has_focus = focus_ed_martial }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = diligent }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge119
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.122 days = 50 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = slothful }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = indolent }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = fussy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = sturdy trait = gluttonous }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = feeble trait = temperate }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = craven }
		}
	}

	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.100 } }
	}
}

	id = lodge.124
	title = EVTTITLElodge100_my_training
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge124_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge124_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge124_greenskin }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge124_ulric }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge124_knight }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge124_dawi }
	desc = { trigger = { lodge_voice_other_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge124_any }
	picture = GFX_evt_child_sword
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:child_hero_mentor

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge124
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = wounded } } random = { chance = 25 add_trait = wounded } }
		add_one_duel_experience_modifier = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = lodge_got_mentor_blade }
				NOT = { has_artifact = sword_of_striking }
				event_target:child_hero_mentor = { is_alive = yes }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 10
				set_character_flag = lodge_got_mentor_blade
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge124_blade_TT }
				add_artifact = sword_of_striking
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.100 } }
	}
}

# lodge.125 Not a fighter
# The child never wanted this: too lazy, too bookish, too fine for the
# barracks, or too afraid. Scope: the child; event_target:recruiting_parent.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.125
	title = EVTTITLElodge125
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_lazy }
		text = EVTDESClodge125_lazy
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_scholar }
		text = EVTDESClodge125_scholar
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_lady }
		text = EVTDESClodge125_lady
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_coward }
		text = EVTDESClodge125_coward
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_child_reading
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	hide_new = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:recruiting_parent = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			20 = {
				trigger = { NOR = { trait = diligent trait = conscientious } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = indolent }
				set_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_lazy
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_focus = focus_ed_learning }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = conscientious }
				set_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_scholar
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = groomed }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = proud }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = fussy }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = uncouth }
				set_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_lady
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = timid }
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = craven }
				set_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_coward
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge125
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:recruiting_parent = { character_event = { id = lodge.126 } }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge125
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } }
			random = { chance = 25 add_trait = stressed }
		}
		lodge_child_clear_flags_effect = yes
		set_character_flag = flag_child_is_a_warrior
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.100 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# lodge.126 My child wants out
# Scope: the parent; FROM is the child.

	id = lodge.126
	title = EVTTITLElodge125
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_lazy } }
		text = EVTDESClodge126_lazy
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_scholar } }
		text = EVTDESClodge126_scholar
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_lady } }
		text = EVTDESClodge126_lady
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_coward } }
		text = EVTDESClodge126_coward
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_child_reading
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	hide_new = yes
	portrait = FROM

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # You will obey
		name = EVTOPTAlodge126
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge126_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.127 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = stubborn }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = kind }
		}
	}
	option = { # Let the child go
		name = EVTOPTBlodge126
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = stubborn } }
		FROM = {
			leave_society = yes
			set_character_flag = flag_child_abandoned_warband
			lodge_child_clear_flags_effect = yes
		}
		tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes
		detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = cruel }
		}
	}
}

# lodge.127 The answer is no
# The child gives in (and is miserable), runs away with the treasury or,
# at 16 or older, takes their own life. Scope: the child; FROM is the
# parent.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.127
	title = EVTTITLElodge125
	desc = EVTDESClodge127
	picture = GFX_evt_child_reading
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	hide_new = yes
	portrait = FROM

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_independent_ruler = {
			limit = {
				is_within_diplo_range = FROM
				NOR = {
					character = ROOT
					character = FROM
					same_society_as = FROM
					same_society_as = ROOT
				}
			}
			preferred_limit = { is_rival = FROM }
			preferred_limit = { religion = ROOT culture = ROOT }
			preferred_limit = { religion = ROOT }
			save_event_target_as = fleeing_target
		}
	}

	option = { # Give in
		name = EVTOPTAlodge127
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = depressed } }
		add_trait = depressed
		lodge_child_clear_flags_effect = yes
		set_character_flag = flag_child_is_a_warrior
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.128 days = 25 } }
			character_event = { id = lodge.100 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}
	option = { # Run away
		name = EVTOPTBlodge127
		trigger = {
			is_ruler = no
			host = { character = FROM }
			event_target:fleeing_target = { is_alive = yes }
		}
		tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				show_scope_change = no
				transfer_scaled_wealth = { to = ROOT value = 0.5 min = 100 max = 250 }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.129 } }
			move_character = event_target:fleeing_target
			set_character_flag = no_court_invites
			leave_society = yes
			set_character_flag = flag_child_abandoned_warband
			character_event = { id = lodge.131 days = 450 random = 250 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { # Take my own life
		name = EVTOPTClodge127
		trigger = { age = 16 }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.130 } } }
		death = { death_reason = death_suicide }
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
	option = { # Already miserable: endure anyway
		name = EVTOPTDlodge127
		trigger = { trait = depressed }
		lodge_child_stress_effect = yes
		lodge_child_clear_flags_effect = yes
		set_character_flag = flag_child_is_a_warrior
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.128 days = 25 } }
			character_event = { id = lodge.100 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}
}

# lodge.131 Whatever happened to the runaway? (hidden)
# Years later the runaway has become what they were afraid of becoming,
# and word reaches the parent (lodge.132 - lodge.135).
# Scope: the runaway; FROMFROM is the parent.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.131
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_ruler = no
		NOR = { top_liege = { character = FROMFROM } trait = incapable }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_lazy }
			change_martial = -3
			add_trait = sturdy
			add_trait = slothful
			add_trait = gluttonous
			add_trait = drunkard
			if = {
				limit = { has_nickname = no }
				random_list = {
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_merry }
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_drunkard }
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_fat }
					50 = { }
				}
			}
			FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.132 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_scholar }
			change_martial = -3
			add_trait = feeble
			add_trait = erudite
			add_trait = craven
			add_trait = uncouth
			if = {
				limit = { has_nickname = no }
				random_list = {
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_frail }
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_recluse }
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_wise }
					50 = { }
				}
			}
			FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.133 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_lady }
			change_martial = -3
			add_trait = dull
			add_trait = proud
			add_trait = content
			add_trait = groomed
			if = {
				limit = { has_nickname = no }
				random_list = {
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_frail }
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_fair }
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_gracious }
					50 = { }
				}
			}
			FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.134 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_prospective_child_warrior_is_coward }
			change_martial = -3
			add_trait = paranoid
			add_trait = craven
			add_trait = uncouth
			add_trait = shy
			if = {
				limit = { has_nickname = no }
				random_list = {
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_shadow }
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_whisperer }
					25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_twitchy }
					50 = { }
				}
			}
			FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.135 } }
		}
		lodge_child_clear_flags_effect = yes
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Missions

# lodge.200 The missions (hidden)
# Every two years (on_society_bi_yearly_pulse) a Hero of a warrior lodge,
# a warband or the Amazons may set a member a mission: raid a rich
# settlement, take up a warrior's focus, duel a fellow member, duel a
# rival to the death, make war (county conquest, a vassal's or a
# neighbour's lands, the old lands for Ulric, a grudge for the Dawi) or
# bring a child into the fold. The AI mostly settles missions in the
# background. A society whose leader runs its own missions (the flag
# own_mission_tombola: Bretonnia, Kislev, the Dogs of War) is skipped.
# Scope: the member; event_target:lodge_hero (the Hero who asks).
character_event = {
	id = lodge.200
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		any_society_member = {
			NOT = { character = ROOT }
			is_society_grandmaster = yes
			same_society_as = ROOT
			NOT = { has_character_flag = own_mission_tombola }
		}
		lodge_tombola_member_trigger = yes
		has_any_quest = no
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = quest_cooldown_timer }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		# The Hero who asks: a Hero of the society, else a veteran, else the
		# leader.
		random_society_member = {
			limit = { NOT = { character = ROOT } same_society_as = ROOT }
			preferred_limit = { society_rank == 4 }
			preferred_limit = { society_rank == 3 }
			save_event_target_as = lodge_hero
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = yes }
			capital_scope = { save_event_target_as = home_capital } # For distance checks
		}

		# Raid (201), focus (204), friendly duel (220), duel to the death
		# (225), war (227, 240, 242, 244, 246), a child (235); the AI often
		# settles the mission in the background.

		random_list = {
			20 = {
				trigger = { #Ruler should be allowed to loot at all in the first place
					is_landed = yes
					is_allowed_to_loot = yes
					OR = { independent = yes top_liege = { is_allowed_to_loot = yes } }
					#A lootable province must exist.
					any_independent_ruler = {
						NOT = { war_with = ROOT }
						NOR = {
							is_liege_of = ROOT
							has_non_aggression_pact_with = ROOT
							is_allied_with = ROOT
							AND = {
								is_tributary = yes
								suzerain = {
									OR = { character = ROOT any_liege = { character = ROOT } }
								}
							}
						}
						OR = {
							AND = {
								ROOT = {
									OR = {
										religion = norscan_gods
										has_religion_feature = religion_seafaring
										is_seafarer = yes
									}
								}
								NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 250 } }
							}
							NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 50 } }
						}
						any_realm_province = {
							is_occupied = no
							OR = {
								any_neighbor_province = { owner = { is_liege_or_above = ROOT } }
								any_neighbor_province = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
								ROOT = {
									any_demesne_province = {
										port = yes
										TECH_NAVAL = 1
										OR = {
											has_building = tb_reaver_port_1
											has_building = tb_shipyard_1
											has_building = ca_shipyard_1
											has_building = tp_shipyard_1
											has_building = ct_shipyard_1
										}
										can_naval_path_to = PREVPREV
									}
								}
							}
							capital_holding = { NOT = { holding_type = nomad } }
							holder_scope = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
							holder_scope = {
								NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
								top_liege = { NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT } }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				event_target:lodge_hero = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.201 } #Raid and loot barony.
				}
			}
			20 = {
				trigger = {
					is_landed = yes
					NOR = {
						has_focus = focus_war
						has_focus = focus_hunting
						has_focus = focus_rulership
						has_focus = focus_carousing
					}
				}
				event_target:lodge_hero = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.204 } #Lose Weak Focus.
				}
			}
			20 = {
				trigger = {
					age = 16
					society = {
						any_society_member = {
							ai = yes
							age = 16
							NOT = { character = ROOT }
							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
							NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
							NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
						}
					}
				}
				event_target:lodge_hero = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.220 } #Duel <someone>. First blood.
				}
			}
			5 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 society_member_of = amazon_sisterhood }
				trigger = {
					ai = no
					age = 16
					OR = {
						AND = {
							any_society_member = {
								NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
								lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
								NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
								NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
								is_adult = yes
								ai = yes
								is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
							}
						}
						AND = {
							any_rival = {
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
								NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
								is_adult = yes
								ai = yes
								ROOT = {
									capital_scope = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } }
								}
							}
						}
						AND = {
							any_realm_character = {
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
								NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
								NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
								ai = yes
								is_adult = yes
								has_minor_title = title_commander
							}
						}
						AND = {
							any_neighbor_independent_ruler = {
								any_realm_character = {
									is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
									NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
									NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
									NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
									is_adult = yes
									ai = yes
									has_minor_title = title_commander
									ROOT = {
										capital_scope = {
											distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 }
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				event_target:lodge_hero = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.225 } #Duel <someone>. To the death!
				}
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 society_member_of = amazon_sisterhood }
				trigger = {
					is_landed = yes
					NOT = { society_member_of = warriors_guild_dawi }
					any_independent_ruler = {
						any_realm_title = {
							tier = COUNT
							ROOT = {
								can_use_cb = { #against target
									target = PREVPREV
									thirdparty_title = PREV
									casus_belli = pagan_county_conquest
								}
							}
							location = {
								OR = {
									AND = { #Either you can travel via water to them...
										is_coastal = yes
										can_naval_path_to = {
											target = event_target:home_capital
											distance < 1000
										}
									}
									AND = {
										#Or they are accessible via land...
										event_target:home_capital = { is_coastal = no }
										can_land_path_to = {
											target = event_target:home_capital
											distance < 1000
										}
									}
									AND = { #Or not a coastal place...
										is_coastal = no
										can_land_path_to = {
											target = event_target:home_capital
											distance < 1000
										}
										#event_target:home_capital = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } }
									}
								}
							}
						}
						holder_scope = {
							top_liege = {
								NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
								# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 50% of your strength:
								NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
								relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.5 }
							}
						}
					}
				}

				event_target:lodge_hero = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.227 } #Wage war against someone!
				}
			}
			30 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 society_member_of = amazon_sisterhood }
				trigger = {
					independent = no
					is_landed = yes
					NOT = { society_member_of = warriors_guild_dawi }
					any_realm_lord = {
						any_realm_province = {
							county = {
								ROOT = {
									can_use_cb = { #against target
										target = PREVPREVPREV
										casus_belli = unjust_conquest
										thirdparty_title = PREV
										only_check_triggers = yes
									}
								}
								holder_scope = {
									top_liege = {
										NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
										# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 50% of your strength.
										NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
										relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.5 }
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				event_target:lodge_hero = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.244 } #Wage war against someone!
				}
			}
			30 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2.5 ai = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0 society_member_of = amazon_sisterhood }
				trigger = {
					independent = yes
					is_landed = yes
					any_neighbor_independent_ruler = {
						ROOT = {
							can_use_cb = { #against target
								target = PREV
								casus_belli = settle_grudge
								only_check_triggers = yes
							}
						}
						NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
						OR = {
							culture_group = goblin_group
							culture_group = orc_group
							AND = {
								# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 50% of your strength.
								NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
								relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.8 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				event_target:lodge_hero = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.246 } #Wage war against someone!
				}
			}
			30 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 society_member_of = amazon_sisterhood }
				trigger = {
					independent = yes
					is_landed = yes
					NOT = { society_member_of = warriors_guild_dawi }
					any_neighbor_independent_ruler = {
						any_realm_province = {
							county = {
								ROOT = {
									can_use_cb = { #against target
										target = PREVPREVPREV
										casus_belli = unjust_conquest
										thirdparty_title = PREV
										only_check_triggers = yes
									}
								}
								holder_scope = {
									top_liege = {
										NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
										# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 50% of your strength.
										NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
										relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.5 }
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				event_target:lodge_hero = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.242 } #Wage war against someone!
				}
			}
			15 = { # Reclaim the old lands (Ulric)
				modifier = { factor = 0 society_member_of = amazon_sisterhood }
				trigger = {
					society_member_of = warriors_of_ulric
					is_landed = yes
					any_neighbor_independent_ruler = {
						NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
						NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
						relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.5 }
						any_realm_province = {
							duchy = {
								ROOT = {
									can_use_cb = {
										target = PREVPREVPREV
										casus_belli = imperial_reconquest2
										thirdparty_title = PREV
										only_check_triggers = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				event_target:lodge_hero = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.240 } # Reclaim the old lands
				}
			}
			20 = {
				trigger = {
					is_playable = yes
					is_adult = yes
					any_child = {
						is_child_of = PREV
						is_in_society = no
						is_alive = yes
						age = 12
						prisoner = no
						NOT = { has_character_flag = flag_child_abandoned_warband }
						OR = { #AIs should only strive to recruit their main heir
							PREV = { ai = no }
							is_primary_heir = PREV
						}
					}
				}

				event_target:lodge_hero = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.235 } #Recruit child into warrior lodge!
				}
			}
			75 = { # AI bypasses missions at times
				trigger = { ai = yes }
				random_list = {
					70 = {
						modifier = { factor = 0.9 society_influence >= 30 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.9 society_influence >= 40 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.8 society_influence >= 50 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.7 society_influence >= 70 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.6 society_influence >= 80 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.5 society_influence >= 90 }
						add_mission_succeed_influence_effect = yes
					}
					30 = { add_mission_fail_influence_effect = yes }
				}
				random = {
					chance = 10
					character_event = { id = lodge.66 } #Small chance for tattoos to Rank 3 and 4s.
				}
			}

			200 = {
				trigger = { ai = yes }
				#Do nothing for AI
			}
		}
	}
}

# lodge.201 - lodge.203 Raid and loot
# The Hero names a rich settlement; looting it (on_loot_settlement) ends
# the mission. Also sent by the Dogs of War missions (DoWA.200).
# Scope: the Hero (201), then the member; FROM is the Hero.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.201
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { society_quest_event = { id = lodge.202 days = 7 random = 7 } }
	}
}

society_quest_event = {
	id = lodge.202
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge202_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge202_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge202_greenskin }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge202_ulric }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge202_knight }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge202_dawi }
	desc = { trigger = { lodge_voice_other_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge202_any }

	is_triggered_only = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
		has_any_quest = no
		same_society_as = FROM
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = quest_cooldown_timer }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		is_landed = yes
		is_allowed_to_loot = yes
		OR = { independent = yes top_liege = { is_allowed_to_loot = yes } }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_independent_ruler = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { war_with = ROOT }
				NOR = {
					is_liege_of = ROOT
					has_non_aggression_pact_with = ROOT
					is_allied_with = ROOT
					AND = {
						is_tributary = yes
						suzerain = { OR = { character = ROOT any_liege = { character = ROOT } } }
					}
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						ROOT = {
							OR = {
								religion = norscan_gods
								has_religion_feature = religion_seafaring
								is_seafarer = yes
							}
						}
						NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 250 } }
					}
					NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 50 } }
				}
				any_realm_province = {
					is_occupied = no
					OR = {
						any_neighbor_province = { owner = { is_liege_or_above = ROOT } }
						any_neighbor_province = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
						ROOT = {
							any_demesne_province = {
								port = yes
								TECH_NAVAL = 1
								OR = {
									has_building = tb_reaver_port_1
									has_building = tb_shipyard_1
									has_building = ca_shipyard_1
									has_building = tp_shipyard_1
									has_building = ct_shipyard_1
								}
								can_naval_path_to = PREVPREV
							}
						}
					}
					capital_holding = { NOT = { holding_type = nomad } }
					holder_scope = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
					holder_scope = {
						NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
						top_liege = { NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT } }
					}
				}
			}
			preferred_limit = { NOR = { is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes } }
			random_realm_province = {
				limit = {
					is_occupied = no
					capital_holding = { NOT = { holding_type = nomad } }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							any_neighbor_province = { owner = { is_liege_or_above = ROOT } }
							any_neighbor_province = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
							NOT = { is_member_of_any_piratic_society_trigger = yes }
						}
						ROOT = {
							any_demesne_province = {
								port = yes
								TECH_NAVAL = 1
								OR = {
									has_building = tb_reaver_port_1
									has_building = tb_shipyard_1
									has_building = ca_shipyard_1
									has_building = tp_shipyard_1
									has_building = ct_shipyard_1
								}
								can_naval_path_to = PREVPREV
							}
						}
					}
					holder_scope = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
					holder_scope = {
						NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
						top_liege = { NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT } }
					}
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								OR = {
									religion = norscan_gods
									has_religion_feature = religion_seafaring
									is_seafarer = yes
								}
							}
							NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 200 } }
						}
						NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 50 } }
					}
					capital_holding = {
						OR = { #Prefer cities and temples.
							holding_type = city
							holding_type = temple
						}
					}
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								OR = {
									is_member_of_any_piratic_society_trigger = yes
									religion = norscan_gods
									has_religion_feature = religion_seafaring
									is_seafarer = yes
								}
							}
							NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 200 } }
						}
						NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 50 } }
					}
					capital_holding = {
						holding_type = castle #Or at least not tribes.
					}
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								OR = {
									is_member_of_any_piratic_society_trigger = yes
									religion = norscan_gods
									has_religion_feature = religion_seafaring
									is_seafarer = yes
								}
							}
							NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 200 } }
						}
						NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 50 } }
					}
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								OR = {
									is_member_of_any_piratic_society_trigger = yes
									religion = norscan_gods
									has_religion_feature = religion_seafaring
									is_seafarer = yes
								}
							}
							NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 250 } }
						}
						NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 100 } }
					}
					capital_holding = {
						OR = { #Prefer cities and temples.
							holding_type = city
							holding_type = temple
						}
					}
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								OR = {
									is_member_of_any_piratic_society_trigger = yes
									religion = norscan_gods
									has_religion_feature = religion_seafaring
									is_seafarer = yes
								}
							}
							NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 250 } }
						}
						NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 100 } }
					}
					capital_holding = {
						holding_type = castle #Or at least not tribes.
					}
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							ROOT = {
								OR = {
									is_member_of_any_piratic_society_trigger = yes
									religion = norscan_gods
									has_religion_feature = religion_seafaring
									is_seafarer = yes
								}
							}
							NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 250 } }
						}
						NOT = { distance_from_realm = { who = ROOT value = 100 } }
					}
				}
				capital_holding = { save_event_target_as = wl_looting_target }
			}
		}
	}

	quest_target = event_target:wl_looting_target

	option = {
		name = ACCEPT
		event_target:wl_looting_target = {
			show_scope_change = no
			set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_raid_and_loot holder = ROOT }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = DECLINE
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }

		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }

		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# lodge.203 The raid is done (on_loot_settlement)
# Scope: the member; FROM is the looted holding.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.203
	desc = EVTDESClodge203
	picture = GFX_evt_vikings_arriving_oldgods
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
		has_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot
		FROM = { is_quest_target_of = ROOT }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge203
		clr_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot
		add_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { holding_type = tribal } }
			scaled_wealth = { value = 1.0 min = 20 max = 50 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { holding_type = castle } }
			scaled_wealth = { value = 1.0 min = 50 max = 150 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { OR = { holding_type = city holding_type = temple } } }
			scaled_wealth = { value = 1.0 min = 100 max = 250 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { destroy_random_building = FROM destroy_random_building = FROM }
	}
}

# lodge.204 - lodge.209 A warrior's focus
# The Hero tells a member who neglects the warrior's life to take up war,
# hunting, rulership or carousing; choosing that focus (on_focus_selected)
# ends the mission. Scope: the Hero (204), then the member.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.204
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { society_quest_event = { id = lodge.205 days = 7 random = 7 } }
	}
}

society_quest_event = {
	id = lodge.205
	desc = {
		trigger = { ROOT = { has_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_war } }
		text = EVTDESClodge205_war
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { ROOT = { has_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_hunting } }
		text = EVTDESClodge205_hunting
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { ROOT = { has_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_rulership } }
		text = EVTDESClodge205_rulership
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { ROOT = { has_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_carousing } }
		text = EVTDESClodge205_carousing
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
		same_society_as = FROM
		has_any_quest = no
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = quest_cooldown_timer }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		is_landed = yes
		NOR = {
			has_focus = focus_war
			has_focus = focus_hunting
			has_focus = focus_carousing
			has_focus = focus_rulership
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			25 = { set_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_war }
			25 = { set_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_hunting }
			25 = { set_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_rulership }
			25 = { set_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_carousing }
		}
	}

	quest_target = ROOT

	option = {
		name = ACCEPT
		ROOT = {
			show_scope_change = no
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_war }
				set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_weak_focus_to_war holder = ROOT }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_hunting }
				set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_weak_focus_to_hunting holder = ROOT }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_rulership }
				set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_weak_focus_to_rulership holder = ROOT }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_carousing }
				set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_weak_focus_to_carousing holder = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = DECLINE
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }

		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }

		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
	after = {
		hidden_effect = { #For the one who instantly selected the focus, bypassing the mission-trigger
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_character_flag
				flag_renounce_focus_for_war = {
					if = {
						limit = { has_focus = focus_war }
						character_event = { id = lodge.206 days = 1 }
					}
				}
				flag_renounce_focus_for_hunting = {
					if = {
						limit = { has_focus = focus_hunting }
						character_event = { id = lodge.207 days = 1 }
					}
				}
				flag_renounce_focus_for_rulership = {
					if = {
						limit = { has_focus = focus_rulership }
						character_event = { id = lodge.208 days = 1 }
					}
				}
				flag_renounce_focus_for_carousing = {
					if = {
						limit = { has_focus = focus_carousing }
						character_event = { id = lodge.209 days = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		clr_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_war
		clr_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_hunting
		clr_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_rulership
		clr_character_flag = flag_renounce_focus_for_carousing
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = lodge.206
	desc = EVTDESClodge206
	picture = GFX_evt_vikings_arriving_oldgods
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
		has_quest = quest_warband_weak_focus_to_war
		has_focus = focus_war
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge206
		clr_quest = quest_warband_weak_focus_to_war
		add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
	}
}
