# lodge.210 - lodge.211 The raid target is ours (on_new_holder*)
# If the looting target came under the member's (or the liege's) rule, the
# mission ends. Scope: the new holder; FROM is the title.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.210
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			AND = { lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes has_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot }
			any_liege = {
				lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
				has_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes has_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot }
			character_event = { id = lodge.211 days = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_liege = {
					lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
					has_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot
				}
			}
			any_liege = {
				limit = {
					lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
					has_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot
				}
				character_event = { id = lodge.211 days = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = lodge.211
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
				has_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot
				any_realm_title = { is_quest_target_of = ROOT }
			}
			clr_quest = quest_warband_raid_and_loot
		}
	}
}

# lodge.220 - lodge.221 A friendly duel
# The Hero asks the member to spar with a fellow member (or with the Hero).
# Scope: the Hero (220), then the member; FROM is the Hero.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.220
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { society_quest_event = { id = lodge.221 days = 7 random = 7 } }
	}
}

society_quest_event = {
	id = lodge.221
	is_triggered_only = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	only_capable = yes

	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:duel_target = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge221_chaos
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:duel_target = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge221_skaven
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:duel_target = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge221_greenskin
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:duel_target = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge221_ulric
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:duel_target = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge221_knight
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:duel_target = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge221_dawi
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:duel_target = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			lodge_voice_other_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge221_any
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge221_self
		trigger = { event_target:duel_target = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
	}

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
		same_society_as = FROM
		has_any_quest = no
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = quest_cooldown_timer }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		OR = { is_landed = yes ai = no }

		society = {
			any_society_member = {
				ai = yes
				age = 16
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { #best case (someone within diplo-range, not sick, not seriously injured already)...
				any_society_member = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					ai = yes
					age = 16
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { age = 60 }
					is_maimed_trigger = no
					has_medium_disability_trigger = no #limits the character to some extent
					has_severe_disability_trigger = no
					character_disease_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
				}
			}

			any_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					ai = yes
					age = 16
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { age = 60 }
					is_maimed_trigger = no
					has_medium_disability_trigger = no #limits the character to some extent
					has_severe_disability_trigger = no
					character_disease_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
				}
				score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of warrior...
					value = 10
					matching_soldier_score = yes
				}
				save_event_target_as = duel_target
			}
		}
		else_if = { #second best choice (same, but outside of diplo-range)...
			limit = {
				any_society_member = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					ai = yes
					age = 16
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { age = 60 }
					has_medium_disability_trigger = no #limits the character to some extent
					is_maimed_trigger = no #has a maimed trait
					has_severe_disability_trigger = no
					character_disease_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
				}
			}
			any_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					ai = yes
					age = 16
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { age = 60 }
					has_medium_disability_trigger = no #limits the character to some extent
					is_maimed_trigger = no #has a maimed trait
					has_severe_disability_trigger = no
					character_disease_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
				}
				score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of warrior...
					value = 10
					matching_soldier_score = yes
				}
				save_event_target_as = duel_target
			}
		}
		else_if = { #third best option (now also injured or old folks allowed)...
			limit = {
				any_society_member = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					ai = yes
					age = 16
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
				}
			}
			any_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					ai = yes
					age = 16
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
				}
				score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of warrior...
					value = 10
					matching_soldier_score = yes
				}
				save_event_target_as = duel_target
			}
		}
	}

	quest_target = event_target:duel_target #Is this how it's done?

	option = {
		name = ACCEPT
		event_target:duel_target = {
			show_scope_change = no
			set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_duel_honor holder = ROOT }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = DECLINE
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }

		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }

		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# lodge.222 - lodge.224 A mission target is gone
# On the target's death (on_death) or when the war became invalid, the
# mission fails with a notice. Also serves the Chaos cult duel quest.
# Scope: the dead target (222), then the member; FROM is the target.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.222
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_quest_target = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		any_quester_targeting_this = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_quest = quest_warband_duel_honor
					has_quest = quest_warband_duel_deadly
					has_quest = quest_chaos_cult_duel_deadly
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = lodge.223 days = 15 } #Clears duel mission
		}
		any_quester_targeting_this = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_quest = quest_warband_war
					has_quest = quest_military_war
					has_quest = quest_grudge_war
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = lodge.223 days = 15 } # Clears the war mission
		}
		any_quester_targeting_this = {
			limit = { has_quest = quest_warband_recruit }
			character_event = { id = lodge.223 days = 15 } #Clears recruit mission
		}
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = lodge.223
	picture = GFX_evt_battle
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge223_duel
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_quest = quest_warband_duel_honor
				has_quest = quest_warband_duel_deadly
				has_quest = quest_chaos_cult_duel_deadly
			}
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_warband_war }
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_warband_recruit }
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_military_war }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge223_war
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_quest = quest_warband_war
				has_quest = quest_grudge_war
				has_quest = quest_military_war
			}
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_warband_duel_honor }
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_warband_duel_deadly }
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_warband_recruit }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge223_recruit
		trigger = {
			has_quest = quest_warband_recruit
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_warband_duel_honor }
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_warband_duel_deadly }
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_warband_war }
			NOT = { has_quest = quest_military_war }
		}
	}

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_quest = quest_warband_duel_honor
			has_quest = quest_warband_duel_deadly
			has_quest = quest_warband_war
			has_quest = quest_military_war
			has_quest = quest_grudge_war
			has_quest = quest_warband_recruit
		}
	}

	notification = yes

	option = { #Alas
		name = ALAS
		character_event = { id = lodge.224 days = 1 } # ACTUAL cleanup
	}
}

character_event = { #*Actual* clean up, because the event above can't handle all the instant stuff
	id = lodge.224
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_quest = quest_warband_war
					has_quest = quest_military_war
					has_quest = quest_grudge_war
				}
			}
			add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }
		}

		clr_quest = { id = quest_warband_duel_honor failure = yes }
		clr_quest = { id = quest_warband_duel_deadly failure = yes }
		clr_quest = { id = quest_chaos_cult_duel_deadly failure = yes }
		clr_quest = { id = quest_grudge_war failure = yes }
		clr_quest = { id = quest_warband_war failure = yes }
		clr_quest = { id = quest_military_war failure = yes }
		clr_quest = { id = quest_warband_recruit failure = yes }
		clr_character_flag = deadly_duel@FROM
	}
}

# lodge.225 - lodge.226 A duel to the death
# The Hero names a champion of a rival society, a rival, a commander of
# the realm or of a neighbour. Scope: the Hero (225), then the member.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.225
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { society_quest_event = { id = lodge.226 days = 7 random = 7 } }
	}
}

society_quest_event = {
	id = lodge.226
	is_triggered_only = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
		same_society_as = FROM
		has_any_quest = no
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = quest_cooldown_timer }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		OR = { is_landed = yes ai = no }
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge226_rival
		trigger = { event_target:duel_target = { lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge226_other
		trigger = { event_target:duel_target = { lodge_mission_member_trigger = no } }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = { #Best case... (a society member of *another* warrior lodge, who matches or is below your PCS)
			limit = {
				any_society_member = {
					NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
					lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
					is_adult = yes
					ai = yes
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
					top_liege = { any_demesne_title = { lower_tier_than = DUKE } }
				}
			}
			any_society_member = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
					lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
					is_adult = yes
					ai = yes
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
					top_liege = { any_demesne_title = { lower_tier_than = DUKE } }
				}
				score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of character you wouldn't get along with...
					value = 10
					potential_rival_score = yes
				}
				score_value = { #Extra weight for characters you've encountered before...
					value = 10
					disliked_target_selection_score = yes
				}
				score_value = { #Reduced chance to pick people currently at war (unable to duel)...
					value = 10
					at_war_reduction_score = yes
				}

				save_event_target_as = duel_target
				break = yes
			}
		}
		if = { #2nd best case (a rival) - PCS is second prio...
			limit = {
				any_rival = {
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					is_adult = yes
					ai = yes
					ROOT = { capital_scope = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } } }
				}
			}
			any_rival = {
				limit = {
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					is_adult = yes
					ai = yes
					ROOT = { capital_scope = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } } }
				}
				score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of character you wouldn't get along with...
					value = 10
					potential_rival_score = yes
				}
				score_value = { #Extra weight for characters you've encountered before...
					value = 10
					disliked_target_selection_score = yes
				}
				save_event_target_as = duel_target
				break = yes
			}
		}
		if = { #3rd best case (a realm character) who fits the bill...
			limit = {
				any_realm_character = {
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
					ai = yes
					is_adult = yes
					has_minor_title = title_commander
				}
			}
			any_realm_character = {
				limit = {
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
					ai = yes
					is_adult = yes
					has_minor_title = title_commander
				}
				score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of character you wouldn't get along with...
					value = 10
					potential_rival_score = yes
				}
				score_value = { #Extra weight for characters you've encountered before...
					value = 10
					disliked_target_selection_score = yes
				}
				save_event_target_as = duel_target
				break = yes
			}
		}
		if = { #4th case (a *neighbor's* realm character) who fits the bill...
			limit = {
				any_neighbor_independent_ruler = {
					any_realm_character = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
						NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
						is_adult = yes
						ai = yes
						ROOT = { capital_scope = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } } }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
				}
			}
			random_neighbor_independent_ruler = {
				limit = {
					any_realm_character = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
						NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
						is_adult = yes
						ai = yes
						ROOT = { capital_scope = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } } }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
				}
				any_realm_character = {
					limit = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
						NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
						is_adult = yes
						ai = yes
						is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
						ROOT = { capital_scope = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } } }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
					score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of character you wouldn't get along with...
						value = 10
						potential_rival_score = yes
					}
					score_value = { #Extra weight for characters you've encountered before...
						value = 10
						disliked_target_selection_score = yes
					}
					save_event_target_as = duel_target
					break = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = { # Warrior Lodge members who are without diplomatic range, such as mercenaries
			limit = {
				any_society_member = {
					NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
					lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
					is_adult = yes
					ai = yes
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
				}
			}
			any_society_member = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
					lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = severe_illness }
					NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
					is_adult = yes
					ai = yes
					is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
				}
				score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of character you wouldn't get along with...
					value = 10
					potential_rival_score = yes
				}
				score_value = { #Extra weight for characters you've encountered before...
					value = 10
					disliked_target_selection_score = yes
				}
				score_value = { #Reduced chance to pick people currently at war (unable to duel)...
					value = 10
					at_war_reduction_score = yes
				}

				save_event_target_as = duel_target
				break = yes
			}
		}
	}

	quest_target = event_target:duel_target

	option = {
		name = ACCEPT
		set_character_flag = deadly_duel@event_target:duel_target
		event_target:duel_target = {
			show_scope_change = no
			set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_duel_deadly holder = ROOT }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = DECLINE
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }

		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }

		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# lodge.227 - lodge.228 Make war
# The Hero points the member at a county to conquer (pagan county
# conquest; a warband takes the warband war quest).
# Scope: the Hero (227), then the member; FROM is the Hero.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.227
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { society_quest_event = { id = lodge.228 days = 7 random = 7 } }
	}
}

society_quest_event = {
	id = lodge.228
	is_triggered_only = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
		same_society_as = FROM
		has_any_quest = no
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = quest_cooldown_timer }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		is_landed = yes
		any_independent_ruler = {
			any_realm_title = {
				tier = COUNT
				ROOT = {
					can_use_cb = { #against target
						target = PREVPREV
						thirdparty_title = PREV
						casus_belli = pagan_county_conquest
					}
				}
				location = {
					OR = {
						AND = { #Either you can travel via water to them...
							is_coastal = yes
							can_naval_path_to = {
								target = event_target:home_capital
								distance < 1000
							}
						}
						AND = {
							#Or they are accessible via land...
							event_target:home_capital = { is_coastal = no }
							can_land_path_to = {
								target = event_target:home_capital
								distance < 1000
							}
						}
						AND = { #Or not a coastal place...
							is_coastal = no
							can_land_path_to = {
								target = event_target:home_capital
								distance < 1000
							}
							#event_target:home_capital = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } }
						}
					}
				}
				holder_scope = {
					top_liege = {
						NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
						# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 50% of your strength.
						NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
						relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.5 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:my_war_target_holder = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge228_chaos
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:my_war_target_holder = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge228_skaven
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:my_war_target_holder = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge228_greenskin
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:my_war_target_holder = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge228_ulric
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:my_war_target_holder = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge228_knight
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:my_war_target_holder = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge228_dawi
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:my_war_target_holder = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
			lodge_voice_other_trigger = yes
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge228_any
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge228_self
		trigger = { event_target:my_war_target_holder = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
	}

	immediate = {
		#Declare a county conquest against someone
		random_independent_ruler = {
			limit = {
				any_realm_title = {
					tier = COUNT
					ROOT = {
						can_use_cb = { #against target
							target = PREVPREV
							thirdparty_title = PREV
							casus_belli = pagan_county_conquest
						}
					}
					location = {
						OR = {
							AND = { #Either you can travel via water to them...
								is_coastal = yes
								can_naval_path_to = {
									target = event_target:home_capital
									distance < 1000
								}
							}
							AND = {
								#Or they are accessible via land...
								event_target:home_capital = { is_coastal = no }
								can_land_path_to = {
									target = event_target:home_capital
									distance < 1000
								}
							}
							AND = { #Or not a coastal place...
								is_coastal = no
								can_land_path_to = {
									target = event_target:home_capital
									distance < 1000
								}
								#event_target:home_capital = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } }
							}
						}
					}
					holder_scope = {
						top_liege = {
							NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
							# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 50% of your strength.
							NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
							relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.5 }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random_realm_title = {
				limit = {
					tier = COUNT
					ROOT = {
						can_use_cb = { #against target
							target = PREVPREV
							thirdparty_title = PREV
							casus_belli = pagan_county_conquest
						}
					}
					location = {
						OR = {
							AND = { #Either you can travel via water to them...
								is_coastal = yes
								can_naval_path_to = {
									target = event_target:home_capital
									distance < 1000
								}
							}
							AND = {
								#Or they are accessible via land...
								event_target:home_capital = { is_coastal = no }
								can_land_path_to = {
									target = event_target:home_capital
									distance < 1000
								}
							}
							AND = { #Or not a coastal place...
								is_coastal = no
								can_land_path_to = {
									target = event_target:home_capital
									distance < 1000
								}
								#event_target:home_capital = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } }
							}
						}
					}
					holder_scope = {
						top_liege = {
							NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
							# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 50% of your strength.
							NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
							relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.5 }
						}
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = my_war_target
				holder_scope = { top_liege = { save_event_target_as = my_war_target_holder } }
			}
		}
	}

	quest_target = event_target:my_war_target #Is this how it's done?

	option = {
		name = ACCEPT
		event_target:my_war_target = {
			show_scope_change = no
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { is_member_of_any_warband_trigger = yes } }
				set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_war holder = ROOT }
			}
			else = { set_quest_target = { id = quest_military_war holder = ROOT } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = DECLINE
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }

		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }

		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# lodge.229 The war is over
# A Hero of the society writes about the war (won, white peace or lost)
# and the society pays out. Scope: the member; event_target:defender_target.
letter_event = {
	id = lodge.229
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_war

	portrait = event_target:lodge_hero

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_quest = quest_warband_war has_quest = quest_military_war }
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = { #Find some folks who are impressed by your victory...
				limit = { has_character_flag = won_war@event_target:defender_target }
				any_society_member = {
					limit = {
						same_society_as = ROOT
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						NOT = { character = event_target:defender_target }
					}
					count = 3
					score_value = { #Prioritizes selecting some kind of warrior...
						value = 10
						warrior_recruit_score = yes
					}
					set_character_flag = impressed@ROOT
				}
			}

			#Selects someone to congratulate you...
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					society_rank == 4
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { character = event_target:defender_target }
				}
				save_event_target_as = lodge_hero
				break = yes
			}
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					society_rank == 3
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { character = event_target:defender_target }
				}
				save_event_target_as = lodge_hero
				break = yes
			}
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					society_rank == 2
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { character = event_target:defender_target }
				}
				save_event_target_as = lodge_hero
				break = yes
			}
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					society_rank == 1 #This is likely never really ever going to be needed. But still.
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { character = event_target:defender_target }
				}
				save_event_target_as = lodge_hero
				break = yes
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge229_won
		trigger = { has_character_flag = won_war@event_target:defender_target }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge229_white_peace
		trigger = { has_character_flag = white_peace@event_target:defender_target }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge229_lost
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lost_war@event_target:defender_target }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#I understand
	option = {
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAlodge229_won
			trigger = { has_character_flag = won_war@event_target:defender_target }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAlodge229_white_peace
			trigger = { has_character_flag = white_peace@event_target:defender_target }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAlodge229_lost
			trigger = { has_character_flag = lost_war@event_target:defender_target }
		}
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_character_flag
			won_war@event_target:defender_target = {
				any_society_member = {
					limit = { has_character_flag = impressed@ROOT }
					show_scope_change = no
					opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_conquered_new_land years = 10 }
					clr_character_flag = impressed@ROOT
				}
				add_society_currency_major_effect = yes
			}
			white_peace@event_target:defender_target = { add_society_currency_medium_effect = yes }
			lost_war@event_target:defender_target = { add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes }
		}
		clr_quest = quest_warband_war
		clr_quest = quest_military_war
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = won_war@event_target:defender_target
		clr_character_flag = white_peace@event_target:defender_target
		clr_character_flag = lost_war@event_target:defender_target
	}
}

# lodge.230 - lodge.233 The war of a mission ends (on_war_ended_*)
# Scope: the defender; FROM is the attacker (the member).
character_event = {
	id = lodge.230
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		FROM = {
			lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
			OR = { has_quest = quest_warband_war has_quest = quest_military_war }
		}
		any_realm_title = { is_quest_target_of = FROM }
	}

	immediate = {
		#To handle Warrior Lodge mission "Wage War" completion...
		random_realm_title = {
			limit = { is_quest_target_of = FROM }
			save_event_target_as = target_title
		}
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = attacker_target }
		save_event_target_as = defender_target

		event_target:attacker_target = {
			set_character_flag = won_war@event_target:defender_target
			character_event = { id = lodge.229 days = 6 } # Hero character states that you completed the mission...
		}
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = lodge.233
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		FROM = {
			lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
			OR = { has_quest = quest_warband_war has_quest = quest_military_war }
		}
		any_realm_title = { is_quest_target_of = FROM }
	}

	immediate = {
		#To handle Warrior Lodge mission "Wage War" completion...
		random_realm_title = {
			limit = { is_quest_target_of = FROM }
			save_event_target_as = target_title
		}
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = attacker_target }
		save_event_target_as = defender_target

		event_target:attacker_target = {
			character_event = { id = lodge.223 days = 6 } # Mission target is no longer valid-notification...
		}
	}
}

# lodge.234 A grudge war ends without victory (on_war_ended_whitepeace,
# _defeat, _invalid): the grudge mission fails. A won grudge war ends it in
# the settle_grudge war. Scope: the defender; FROM is the attacker.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.234
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { has_quest = quest_grudge_war }
		is_quest_target_of = FROM
	}

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = defender_target
		FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.223 days = 6 } }
	}
}

# lodge.235 - lodge.236 Bring a child into the fold
# lodge.12 ends the mission when the child passes the trial.
# Scope: the Hero (235), then the member; FROM is the Hero.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.235
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { society_quest_event = { id = lodge.236 days = 7 random = 7 } }
	}
}

society_quest_event = {
	id = lodge.236
	is_triggered_only = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		is_landed = yes
		is_adult = yes
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
		same_society_as = FROM
		has_any_quest = no
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = quest_cooldown_timer }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		any_child = {
			is_child_of = PREV
			is_alive = yes
			is_in_society = no
			age = 12
			prisoner = no
			NOT = { has_character_flag = flag_child_abandoned_warband }
			OR = { #AIs should only strive to recruit their main heir
				PREV = { ai = no }
				is_primary_heir = PREV
			}
		}
	}

	desc = EVTDESClodge236

	immediate = {
		random_child = { #Select a child to recruit
			limit = {
				is_alive = yes
				age = 12
				is_in_society = no
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { has_character_flag = flag_child_abandoned_warband }
				OR = { #AIs should only strive to recruit their main heir
					PREV = { ai = no }
					is_primary_heir = PREV
				}
			}
			preferred_limit = { is_primary_heir = PREV }
			save_event_target_as = recruit_to_be
		}
	}

	quest_target = event_target:recruit_to_be

	option = {
		name = ACCEPT
		event_target:recruit_to_be = {
			show_scope_change = no
			set_quest_target = { id = quest_warband_recruit holder = ROOT }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = DECLINE
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }

		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }

		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# lodge.240 - lodge.241 Reclaim the old lands (Warriors of Ulric)
# A duchy of a neighbour, by the Imperial reconquest.
# Scope: the Hero (240), then the member; FROM is the Hero.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.240
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { society_quest_event = { id = lodge.241 days = 2 random = 2 } }
	}
}

society_quest_event = {
	id = lodge.241
	is_triggered_only = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
		society_member_of = warriors_of_ulric
		same_society_as = FROM
		has_any_quest = no
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = quest_cooldown_timer }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		is_landed = yes
		any_neighbor_independent_ruler = {
			any_realm_province = {
				county = {
					duchy = {
						ROOT = {
							can_use_cb = { #against target
								target = PREVPREVPREVPREV
								casus_belli = imperial_reconquest2
								thirdparty_title = PREV
								only_check_triggers = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				holder_scope = {
					top_liege = {
						NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
						# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 50% of your strength:
						NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
						relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.5 }
					}
				}
				location = {
					OR = {
						AND = { #Either you can travel via water to them...
							is_coastal = yes
							can_naval_path_to = {
								target = event_target:home_capital
								distance < 1000
							}
						}
						AND = {
							#Or they are accessible via land...
							event_target:home_capital = { is_coastal = no }
							can_land_path_to = {
								target = event_target:home_capital
								distance < 1000
							}
						}
						AND = { #Or not a coastal place...
							is_coastal = no
							can_land_path_to = {
								target = event_target:home_capital
								distance < 1000
							}
							#event_target:home_capital = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:my_war_target_holder = { NOT = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
		}
		text = EVTDESClodge241
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge228_self
		trigger = { event_target:my_war_target_holder = { character = event_target:lodge_hero } }
	}
	immediate = {
		#Declare a county conquest against someone
		random_neighbor_independent_ruler = {
			limit = {
				any_realm_province = {
					county = {
						duchy = {
							ROOT = {
								can_use_cb = { #against target
									target = PREVPREVPREVPREV
									casus_belli = imperial_reconquest2
									thirdparty_title = PREV
									only_check_triggers = yes
								}
							}
						}
						holder_scope = {
							top_liege = {
								NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
								# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 50% of your strength:
								NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
								relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.5 }
							}
						}
						location = {
							OR = {
								AND = { #Either you can travel via water to them...
									is_coastal = yes
									can_naval_path_to = {
										target = event_target:home_capital
										distance < 1000
									}
								}
								AND = {
									#Or they are accessible via land...
									event_target:home_capital = { is_coastal = no }
									can_land_path_to = {
										target = event_target:home_capital
										distance < 1000
									}
								}
								AND = { #Or not a coastal place...
									is_coastal = no
									can_land_path_to = {
										target = event_target:home_capital
										distance < 1000
									}
									#event_target:home_capital = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } }
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random_realm_title = {
				limit = {
					tier = DUKE
					ROOT = {
						can_use_cb = { #against target
							target = PREVPREV
							thirdparty_title = PREV
							casus_belli = imperial_reconquest2
							only_check_triggers = yes
						}
					}
					location = {
						OR = {
							AND = { #Either you can travel via water to them...
								is_coastal = yes
								can_naval_path_to = {
									target = event_target:home_capital
									distance < 1000
								}
							}
							AND = {
								#Or they are accessible via land...
								event_target:home_capital = { is_coastal = no }
								can_land_path_to = {
									target = event_target:home_capital
									distance < 1000
								}
							}
							AND = { #Or not a coastal place...
								is_coastal = no
								can_land_path_to = {
									target = event_target:home_capital
									distance < 1000
								}
								#event_target:home_capital = { distance = { where = PREV distance < 1000 } }
							}
						}
					}
					holder_scope = {
						top_liege = {
							NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
							# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 50% of your strength.
							NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
							relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.5 }
						}
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = my_war_target
				holder_scope = { top_liege = { save_event_target_as = my_war_target_holder } }
			}
		}
	}

	quest_target = event_target:my_war_target #Is this how it's done?

	option = {
		name = ACCEPT
		event_target:my_war_target = {
			show_scope_change = no
			set_quest_target = { id = quest_military_war holder = ROOT }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = DECLINE
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }

		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }

		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

		NOT = { has_character_modifier = quest_cooldown_timer }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		is_landed = yes
		any_neighbor_independent_ruler = {
			ROOT = {
				can_use_cb = { #against target
					target = PREV
					casus_belli = settle_grudge
					only_check_triggers = yes
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
			# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 80% of your strength.
			OR = {
				culture_group = goblin_group
				culture_group = orc_group
				AND = {
					# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 50% of your strength.
					NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
					relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.8 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		#Declare a county conquest against someone
		random_neighbor_independent_ruler = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					can_use_cb = { #against target
						target = PREV
						casus_belli = settle_grudge
						only_check_triggers = yes
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_truce = ROOT }
				OR = {
					culture_group = goblin_group
					culture_group = orc_group
					AND = {
						# target should not be stronger than 120% of your strength, and not weaker than 50% of your strength.
						NOT = { relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 1.2 } }
						relative_power = { who = ROOT power = 0.8 }
					}
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = my_war_target
			save_event_target_as = my_war_target_holder
		}
	}

	quest_target = event_target:my_war_target #Is this how it's done?

	option = {
		name = ACCEPT
		event_target:my_war_target = {
			show_scope_change = no
			set_quest_target = { id = quest_grudge_war holder = ROOT }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = DECLINE
		custom_tooltip = { text = decline_quest_tooltip }

		add_character_modifier = { name = quest_cooldown_timer hidden = yes days = 1000 }

		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Flavour: sparring, duels, teaching and the soulbound weapon
###############################################################################

# lodge.300 A sparring match
# A Hero of a lodge or warband (or a captain of the witch hunters) notices a
# member who fights badly and picks a sparring partner for them: an easy
# match to build confidence, an even one, or a beating as a lesson. How the
# member takes the choice shows in the report (lodge.301 - lodge.306).
# Scope: ROOT is the Hero; event_target:combatant_1 the weak member.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.300
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge300_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge300_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge300_greenskin }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge300_ulric }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge300_knight }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge300_dawi }
	desc = { trigger = { lodge_voice_other_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge300_any }
	picture = GFX_evt_melee
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:combatant_1

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_society_bi_yearly_pulse (warrior_lodge_champion_train_other_members)
	is_in_society = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
		war = no
		OR = {
			AND = { society_rank == 4 lodge_tombola_member_trigger = yes }
			AND = { society_rank == 3 is_state_witch_hunter_trigger = yes }
		}
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		is_adult = yes
		any_society_member = { count = 4 lodge_sparring_partner_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			10 = { sound_effect = fighting_instructions_01 }
			10 = { sound_effect = fighting_instructions_02 }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			save_event_target_as = hero_instigating_duel
			random_society_member = { # Someone who needs the training
				limit = { lodge_sparring_partner_trigger = yes }
				preferred_limit = { NOT = { combat_rating = 11 } }
				preferred_limit = { NOT = { combat_rating = 16 } }
				preferred_limit = { NOT = { combat_rating = 21 } }
				preferred_limit = { NOT = { combat_rating = 26 } }
				save_event_target_as = combatant_1
			}
			event_target:combatant_1 = {
				any_society_member = { # An even match
					limit = {
						lodge_sparring_partner_trigger = yes
						NOT = { character = event_target:combatant_1 }
					}
					score_value = { value = 10 matching_soldier_score = yes }
					save_event_target_as = society_practice_duelist_target_2
				}
			}
			any_society_member = { # An easy match
				limit = {
					lodge_sparring_partner_trigger = yes
					NOR = {
						character = event_target:combatant_1
						character = event_target:society_practice_duelist_target_2
					}
				}
				score_value = { value = 10 bad_soldier_outcome_score = yes }
				save_event_target_as = society_practice_duelist_target_1
			}
			any_society_member = { # A beating
				limit = {
					lodge_sparring_partner_trigger = yes
					NOR = {
						character = event_target:combatant_1
						character = event_target:society_practice_duelist_target_1
						character = event_target:society_practice_duelist_target_2
					}
				}
				score_value = { value = 10 good_soldier_outcome_score = yes }
				save_event_target_as = society_practice_duelist_target_3
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # An easy match
		name = EVTOPTAlodge300
		show_portrait = event_target:society_practice_duelist_target_1
		custom_tooltip = { text = easy_duel_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:combatant_1 = {
				set_character_flag = mock_duel
				set_character_flag = easy_duel
				set_character_flag = notify_instigating_hero
			}
			event_target:society_practice_duelist_target_1 = {
				lodge_sparring_challenge_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # An even match
		name = EVTOPTBlodge300
		show_portrait = event_target:society_practice_duelist_target_2
		custom_tooltip = { text = even_duel_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:combatant_1 = {
				set_character_flag = mock_duel
				set_character_flag = even_duel
				set_character_flag = notify_instigating_hero
			}
			event_target:society_practice_duelist_target_2 = {
				lodge_sparring_challenge_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # A beating
		name = EVTOPTClodge300
		show_portrait = event_target:society_practice_duelist_target_3
		custom_tooltip = { text = dangerous_duel_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:combatant_1 = {
				set_character_flag = mock_duel
				set_character_flag = hard_duel
				set_character_flag = notify_instigating_hero
			}
			event_target:society_practice_duelist_target_3 = {
				lodge_sparring_challenge_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Leave it
		name = EVTOPTDlodge300
		detract_society_currency_trivial_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# lodge.301 The bout is won
# The member beat the partner. Grateful, insulted or shaken, the member
# tells the Hero what they think of the choice.
# Scope: ROOT is the Hero; event_target:combatant_1 the member.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.301
	desc = EVTDESClodge301
	picture = GFX_evt_melee
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:combatant_1

	is_triggered_only = yes # common/scripted_effects/01_scripted_effects_duels.txt

	trigger = {
		event_target:combatant_1 = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		sound_effect = duel_win
		event_target:combatant_1 = { lodge_sparring_win_mood_effect = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge301
		show_portrait = event_target:combatant_2
		event_target:combatant_1 = {
			show_scope_change = no
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_character_flag
				easy_duel = { add_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes }
				even_duel = { add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes }
				hard_duel = { add_society_currency_medium_effect = yes }
			}
			lodge_sparring_verdict_effect = yes
		}
	}

	after = {
		event_target:combatant_1 = { lodge_sparring_clear_effect = yes }
	}
}

# lodge.310 A duel to the death (hidden)
# A strong AI warrior of a lodge or warband goes looking for a warrior of
# another society and another faith, in the realm or across the border,
# and challenges them to a duel to the death. Stands in for the deeds the
# warrior societies are known for. Likelier for the bold and the proud, and
# for one who has never killed in a duel.
# Scope: ROOT is the duelist.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.310
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_society_bi_yearly_pulse (warrior_lodge_members_duel_enemies)
	ai = yes
	is_in_society = yes

	trigger = {
		lodge_tombola_member_trigger = yes
		NOT = { has_character_flag = call_to_legend_active }
		OR = { combat_rating = 25 society_rank == 4 AND = { society_rank == 3 combat_rating = 15 } }
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		is_adult = yes
		in_command = no
		NOR = {
			has_injury_trigger = yes
			has_disease_trigger = yes
			has_symptom_trigger = yes
			has_character_modifier = recent_duel_timer
		}
		top_liege = {
			OR = {
				any_realm_character = { lodge_duel_foe_trigger = yes same_sex = ROOT }
				any_neighbor_independent_ruler = {
					any_realm_character = { lodge_duel_foe_trigger = yes same_sex = ROOT }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 25 NOT = { any_kill_list_character = { death_reason = death_duel } } }
		modifier = { factor = 3 society_rank == 4 }
		modifier = { factor = 2 society_rank == 3 }
		modifier = { factor = 2 combat_rating = 50 }
		modifier = { factor = 2 combat_rating = 70 }
		modifier = { factor = 2 combat_rating = 90 }
		modifier = { factor = 3 trait = ambitious }
		modifier = { factor = 3 trait = brave }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = gregarious }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = zealous }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wroth }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = diligent }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = proud }
		modifier = { factor = 0 trait = shy }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = content }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = patient }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = temperate }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = slothful }
	}

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = combatant_1
		if = {
			limit = {
				top_liege = {
					any_realm_character = { lodge_duel_foe_trigger = yes same_sex = ROOT }
				}
			}
			top_liege = {
				any_realm_character = {
					limit = { lodge_duel_foe_trigger = yes same_sex = ROOT }
					score_value = { value = 10 bad_soldier_outcome_score = yes }
					save_event_target_as = combatant_2
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			top_liege = {
				random_neighbor_independent_ruler = {
					limit = {
						any_realm_character = { lodge_duel_foe_trigger = yes same_sex = ROOT }
					}
					any_realm_character = {
						limit = { lodge_duel_foe_trigger = yes same_sex = ROOT }
						score_value = { value = 10 bad_soldier_outcome_score = yes }
						save_event_target_as = combatant_2
					}
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:combatant_2 = {
			apply_degree_of_dishonorable_duel_effect = yes
			character_event = { id = duel.1 }
		}
	}
}

# lodge.311 A member falls in a duel (hidden)
# Tells the players of the society that one of their own was killed in a
# duel (lodge.312).
# Scope: ROOT is the dead member.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.311
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death
	is_in_society = yes

	trigger = {
		lodge_tombola_member_trigger = yes
		OR = {
			death_reason = death_duel
			death_reason = death_battle_duel
			death_reason = death_mission_duel
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = lodge_member_killed
		killer = { save_event_target_as = lodge_member_killer }
		any_player = {
			limit = { same_society_as = ROOT NOT = { character = ROOT } }
			character_event = { id = lodge.312 }
		}
	}
}

# lodge.312 One of ours has fallen
# Scope: ROOT is a player of the society; FROM the dead member.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.312
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge312_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge312_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge312_greenskin }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge312_ulric }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge312_knight }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge312_dawi }
	desc = { trigger = { lodge_voice_other_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge312_any }
	picture = GFX_evt_battle
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes # lodge.311
	notification = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge312
	}
}

# lodge.320 A teaching moment (hidden)
# A brave, ambitious, patient or diligent member looks for a fellow member
# who lacks that virtue (a coward, a content soul or a sluggard first) and
# offers to teach them. Five years before either can try again.
# Scope: ROOT is the teacher; event_target:student is saved here.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.320
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_society_bi_yearly_pulse
	is_in_society = yes

	trigger = {
		lodge_tombola_member_trigger = yes
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		NOT = { has_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
		war = no
		is_adult = yes
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = teaching_moment_cooldown }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				trait = brave
				any_society_member = {
					is_available_fellow_society_member_trigger = yes
					NOT = { trait = brave }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = teaching_moment_cooldown }
				}
			}
			AND = {
				trait = ambitious
				any_society_member = {
					is_available_fellow_society_member_trigger = yes
					NOT = { trait = ambitious }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = teaching_moment_cooldown }
				}
			}
			AND = {
				trait = patient
				any_society_member = {
					is_available_fellow_society_member_trigger = yes
					NOT = { trait = patient }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = teaching_moment_cooldown }
				}
			}
			AND = {
				trait = diligent
				any_society_member = {
					is_available_fellow_society_member_trigger = yes
					NOT = { trait = diligent }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = teaching_moment_cooldown }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = teacher
		add_character_modifier = { name = teaching_moment_cooldown duration = 1825 hidden = yes }
		random_list = {
			10 = { # Courage
				trigger = {
					trait = brave
					any_society_member = {
						is_available_fellow_society_member_trigger = yes
						NOT = { trait = brave }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = teaching_moment_cooldown }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					any_society_member = {
						is_available_fellow_society_member_trigger = yes
						trait = craven
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = teaching_moment_cooldown }
					}
				}
				random_society_member = {
					limit = {
						is_available_fellow_society_member_trigger = yes
						NOT = { trait = brave }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = teaching_moment_cooldown }
					}
					preferred_limit = { trait = craven }
					preferred_limit = { NOT = { society_rank == 4 } }
					preferred_limit = { war = no }
					set_character_flag = learning_bravery@event_target:teacher
					save_event_target_as = student
				}
				set_character_flag = teaching_bravery@event_target:student
			}
			10 = { # Ambition
				trigger = {
					trait = ambitious
					any_society_member = {
						is_available_fellow_society_member_trigger = yes
						NOT = { trait = ambitious }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = teaching_moment_cooldown }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					any_society_member = {
						is_available_fellow_society_member_trigger = yes
						trait = content
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = teaching_moment_cooldown }
					}
				}
				random_society_member = {
					limit = {
						is_available_fellow_society_member_trigger = yes
						NOT = { trait = ambitious }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = teaching_moment_cooldown }
					}
					preferred_limit = { trait = content }
					preferred_limit = { NOT = { society_rank == 4 } }
					preferred_limit = { war = no }
					set_character_flag = learning_ambition@event_target:teacher
					save_event_target_as = student
				}
				set_character_flag = teaching_ambition@event_target:student
			}
			10 = { # Patience
				trigger = {
					trait = patient
					any_society_member = {
						is_available_fellow_society_member_trigger = yes
						NOT = { trait = patient }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = teaching_moment_cooldown }
					}
				}
				random_society_member = {
					limit = {
						is_available_fellow_society_member_trigger = yes
						NOT = { trait = patient }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = teaching_moment_cooldown }
					}
					preferred_limit = { trait = wroth }
					preferred_limit = { NOT = { society_rank == 4 } }
					preferred_limit = { war = no }
					set_character_flag = learning_patience@event_target:teacher
					save_event_target_as = student
				}
				set_character_flag = teaching_patience@event_target:student
			}
			10 = { # Diligence
				trigger = {
					trait = diligent
					any_society_member = {
						is_available_fellow_society_member_trigger = yes
						NOT = { trait = diligent }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = teaching_moment_cooldown }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					any_society_member = {
						is_available_fellow_society_member_trigger = yes
						trait = slothful
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = teaching_moment_cooldown }
					}
				}
				random_society_member = {
					limit = {
						is_available_fellow_society_member_trigger = yes
						NOT = { trait = diligent }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = teaching_moment_cooldown }
					}
					preferred_limit = { trait = slothful }
					preferred_limit = { NOT = { society_rank == 4 } }
					preferred_limit = { war = no }
					set_character_flag = learning_diligence@event_target:teacher
					save_event_target_as = student
				}
				set_character_flag = teaching_diligence@event_target:student
			}
		}
		event_target:student = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				name = teaching_moment_cooldown
				duration = 1825
				hidden = yes
			}
		}
		character_event = { id = lodge.321 }
	}
}

# lodge.322 Someone offers guidance
# Accept the lesson, take offence, or decline politely.
# Scope: ROOT is the student; FROM and event_target:teacher the teacher.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.322
	title = EVTTITLElodge320
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge322_bravery
		trigger = { has_character_flag = learning_bravery@event_target:teacher }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge322_ambition
		trigger = { has_character_flag = learning_ambition@event_target:teacher }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge322_patience
		trigger = { has_character_flag = learning_patience@event_target:teacher }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge322_diligence
		trigger = { has_character_flag = learning_diligence@event_target:teacher }
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_teaching_moment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:teacher
	sound = fighting_instructions_02

	is_triggered_only = yes # lodge.321

	trigger = {
		is_alive = yes
		event_target:teacher = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	option = { # Yes, teach me
		name = EVTOPTAlodge322
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge322_TT }
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_character_flag
			learning_bravery@event_target:teacher = {
				show_trait = brave
				custom_tooltip = { text = could_lead_to_brave_tt }
			}
			learning_ambition@event_target:teacher = {
				show_trait = ambitious
				custom_tooltip = { text = could_lead_to_ambitious_tt }
			}
			learning_patience@event_target:teacher = {
				show_trait = patient
				custom_tooltip = { text = could_lead_to_patient_tt }
			}
			learning_diligence@event_target:teacher = {
				show_trait = diligent
				custom_tooltip = { text = could_lead_to_diligent_tt }
			}
		}
		event_target:teacher = { character_event = { id = lodge.323 days = 3 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = { factor = 0 is_rival = FROM }
			modifier = { factor = 10 has_pleasant_personality_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 10 is_friend = FROM }
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				FROM = { ROOT_possibly_romantically_interested_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # How dare you
		name = EVTOPTBlodge322
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge322_TT }
		prestige = 10
		event_target:teacher = {
			set_character_flag = they_insulted_me
			character_event = { id = lodge.324 days = 5 }
		}
		lodge_teach_clear_student_effect = yes
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 100 is_rival = FROM }
			modifier = { factor = 0 is_friend = FROM }
			modifier = { factor = 10 is_evil_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 10 has_unpleasant_personality_trigger = yes }
		}
	}

	option = { # No, thank you
		name = EVTOPTClodge322
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTClodge322_TT }
		event_target:teacher = { character_event = { id = lodge.324 days = 3 } }
		lodge_teach_clear_student_effect = yes
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 10 trait = depressed }
			modifier = { factor = 10 trait = stressed }
			modifier = { factor = 10 is_pregnant = yes }
		}
	}
}

# lodge.323 The student accepts
# Make a show of it, or keep it simple. How the lesson goes depends on the
# student's nature: the kind and the pleasant learn, the cruel and the
# unpleasant do not.
# Scope: ROOT is the teacher; FROM and event_target:student the student.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.323
	title = EVTTITLElodge320
	desc = EVTDESClodge323
	picture = GFX_evt_teaching_moment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:student

	is_triggered_only = yes # lodge.322

	trigger = {
		is_alive = yes
		event_target:student = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	option = { # Bring the gear
		name = EVTOPTAlodge323
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge323_TT }
		if = { limit = { ai = no } scaled_wealth = { value = -1 min = -15 } }
		prestige = 5
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = proud } }
			random = {
				chance = 25
				add_trait = proud
				hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = 38261 } }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 10 trait = proud } }
	}

	option = { # Keep it simple
		name = EVTOPTBlodge323
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge323_TT }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = humble } }
			random = {
				chance = 25
				add_trait = humble
				hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = 38260 } }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 10 trait = humble } }
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				10 = { # A great lesson
					modifier = {
						factor = 10
						event_target:student = { is_benevolent_trigger = yes }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 10
						event_target:student = { has_pleasant_personality_trigger = yes }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:student = { has_pleasant_trait_trigger = yes }
					}
					event_target:student = {
						character_event = { id = lodge.326 days = 10 random = 25 }
					}
				}
				20 = { # A fair lesson
					event_target:student = {
						character_event = { id = lodge.327 days = 10 random = 25 }
					}
				}
				5 = { # A failure
					modifier = { factor = 10 event_target:student = { is_evil_trigger = yes } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 10
						event_target:student = { has_unpleasant_personality_trigger = yes }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:student = { has_unpleasant_trait_trigger = yes }
					}
					event_target:student = {
						character_event = { id = lodge.328 days = 10 random = 25 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		}

	}

# lodge.326 A great lesson
# The student gains the virtue, and may thank the teacher with a kiss.
# Scope: ROOT is the student; FROM and event_target:teacher the teacher.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.326
	title = EVTTITLElodge320
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge326_bravery
		trigger = { has_character_flag = learning_bravery@event_target:teacher }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge326_ambition
		trigger = { has_character_flag = learning_ambition@event_target:teacher }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge326_patience
		trigger = { has_character_flag = learning_patience@event_target:teacher }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge326_diligence
		trigger = { has_character_flag = learning_diligence@event_target:teacher }
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_teaching_moment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:teacher
	sound = fighting_instructions_01

	is_triggered_only = yes # lodge.323

	trigger = {
		is_alive = yes
	}

	option = { # Thank you
		name = EVTOPTAlodge326
		prestige = 15
		add_society_currency_trivial_effect = yes
		lodge_teach_great_effect = yes
		tooltip = { add_friend = FROM }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				event_target:teacher = { NOT = { any_friend = { always = yes } } }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # A kiss
		name = EVTOPTBlodge326
		trigger = {
			event_target:teacher = {
				NOT = { is_lover = ROOT }
				OR = {
					ROOT = { has_religion_feature = religion_holy_family }
					ROOT = { religion = slaanesh }
					NOT = { is_close_relative = ROOT }
				}
				root_attraction_trigger = yes
				attacted_to_root_trigger = yes
				is_adult = yes
			}
			NOT = { trait = celibate }
			is_adult = yes
		}
		prestige = 15
		add_society_currency_trivial_effect = yes
		FROM = { set_character_flag = kiss_of_gratitude }
		lodge_teach_great_effect = yes
		if = { limit = { trait = chaste } remove_trait = chaste }
		else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = lustful } } add_trait = lustful }
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:teacher = {
					OR = {
						has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_budding_romance }
						has_character_flag = carried_torch_for@ROOT
						has_character_flag = carrying_torch_for@ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge326_TT_romance }
			tooltip = { if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no event_target:teacher = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = event_target:teacher } }
			event_target:teacher = { set_character_flag = become_lovers }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:teacher = {
					root_attraction_trigger = yes
					attacted_to_root_trigger = yes
					opinion = { who = ROOT value = 15 }
					NOT = { trait = chaste }
					NOT = { trait = celibate }
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge326_TT_romance }
			tooltip = {
				opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_budding_romance years = 10 }
				reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_budding_romance years = 10 }
			}
			event_target:teacher = { set_character_flag = both_gain_romance }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { event_target:teacher = { root_attraction_trigger = yes } }
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge326_TT_student_crushes }
			tooltip = { opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_budding_romance years = 10 } }
			event_target:teacher = { set_character_flag = student_gains_crush }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { event_target:teacher = { attacted_to_root_trigger = yes } }
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge326_TT_teacher_crushes }
			tooltip = {
				reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_budding_romance years = 10 }
			}
			event_target:teacher = { set_character_flag = teacher_gains_crush }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				event_target:teacher = { prev_is_lonely_this_romance_makes_sense_trigger = yes }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 10 event_target:teacher = { has_lover = no } }
			modifier = { factor = 10 trait = lustful }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = chaste }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = eunuch }
		}
	}

	after = {
		lodge_teach_clear_student_effect = yes
		FROM = { set_character_flag = great_outcome character_event = { id = lodge.329 } }
	}
}
