# lodge.328 A failed lesson
# One step away from the virtue. Blame yourself, or blame the teacher. A
# coward who failed to learn courage in the woods may have eaten something
# that disagreed with them.
# Scope: ROOT is the student; FROM and event_target:teacher the teacher.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.328
	title = EVTTITLElodge320
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge328_bravery
		trigger = { has_character_flag = learning_bravery@event_target:teacher }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge328_ambition
		trigger = { has_character_flag = learning_ambition@event_target:teacher }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge328_patience
		trigger = { has_character_flag = learning_patience@event_target:teacher }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge328_diligence
		trigger = { has_character_flag = learning_diligence@event_target:teacher }
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_teaching_moment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:teacher
	sound = fighting_instructions_01

	is_triggered_only = yes # lodge.323

	trigger = {
		is_alive = yes
	}

	option = { # My own fault
		name = EVTOPTAlodge328
		prestige = -15
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = learning_bravery@event_target:teacher
				NOT = { trait = vomiting }
			}
			add_trait = vomiting hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { is_disease_immune_race = yes } remove_trait = vomiting } }
			set_character_flag = disease_not_serious
			hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = treatment.6 days = 10 random = 30 } }
		}
		lodge_teach_bad_step_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # The teacher's fault
		name = EVTOPTBlodge328
		FROM = { set_character_flag = they_blame_me }
		tooltip = {
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_lousy_teacher years = 10 }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_spoiled_ingrate years = 10 }
		}
		lodge_teach_bad_step_effect = yes
	}

	after = {
		lodge_teach_clear_student_effect = yes
		FROM = { set_character_flag = bad_outcome character_event = { id = lodge.329 } }
	}
}

# lodge.329 How the lesson went
# The teacher sees the result: a friend, a lover, a budding friendship, or
# bad blood.
# Scope: ROOT is the teacher; FROM and event_target:student the student.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.329
	title = EVTTITLElodge320
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge329_bravery
		trigger = { has_character_flag = teaching_bravery@event_target:student }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge329_ambition
		trigger = { has_character_flag = teaching_ambition@event_target:student }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge329_patience
		trigger = { has_character_flag = teaching_patience@event_target:student }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge329_diligence
		trigger = { has_character_flag = teaching_diligence@event_target:student }
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_teaching_moment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:student
	sound = fighting_instructions_02

	is_triggered_only = yes # lodge.326 - lodge.328

	option = { # A great student
		name = EVTOPTAlodge329
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = great_outcome
			NOT = { has_character_flag = kiss_of_gratitude }
		}
		lodge_teach_result_effect = yes
		add_friend = FROM
	}

	option = { # A kiss
		name = EVTOPTAlodge329_romance
		trigger = { has_character_flag = great_outcome has_character_flag = kiss_of_gratitude }
		lodge_teach_result_effect = yes
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_character_flag
			become_lovers = { if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no event_target:student = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = event_target:student } }
			both_gain_romance = {
				opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_budding_romance years = 10 }
				reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_budding_romance years = 10 }
			}
			student_gains_crush = {
				reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_budding_romance years = 10 }
			}
			teacher_gains_crush = {
				opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_budding_romance years = 10 }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # A fair student
		name = EVTOPTBlodge329
		trigger = { has_character_flag = good_outcome }
		lodge_teach_result_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = FROM
						modifier = opinion_budding_friendship
					}
					has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_budding_friendship }
				}
			}
			add_friend = FROM
		}
		else = {
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_budding_friendship years = 10 }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_budding_friendship years = 10 }
		}
	}

	option = { # A failure
		name = { text = EVTOPTClodge329_blamed trigger = { has_character_flag = they_blame_me } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTClodge329 trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = they_blame_me } } }
		trigger = { has_character_flag = bad_outcome }
		lodge_teach_result_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = they_blame_me }
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_spoiled_ingrate years = 10 }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_lousy_teacher years = 10 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		lodge_teach_clear_result_effect = yes
	}
}

# lodge.340 A great warrior falls (hidden)
# A warrior of note dies in battle or in a duel. A friend or a lover in the
# same society, who has not done so before in this life, may honour them
# (lodge.341); a mourner with no artifacts is likelier to be the one.
# Scope: ROOT is the dead warrior.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.340
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death (random_events)
	is_in_society = yes

	trigger = {
		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
		has_any_leadership_trait_trigger = yes
		is_adult = yes
		OR = {
			death_reason = death_battle
			death_reason = death_duel
			death_reason = death_battle_duel
			death_reason = death_mission_duel
		}
		calc_true_if = {
			amount = 2
			martial = 18
			combat_rating = 50
			trait = berserker
			has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes
			this_is_sea_raider_trigger = yes
			has_crusade_trait_trigger = yes
		}
		OR = {
			any_friend = { lodge_soulbound_mourner_trigger = yes }
			any_lover = { lodge_soulbound_mourner_trigger = yes }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_friend = {
			limit = { lodge_soulbound_mourner_trigger = yes }
			save_event_target_as = mourning_friend
		}
		random_lover = {
			limit = { lodge_soulbound_mourner_trigger = yes }
			save_event_target_as = mourning_lover
		}
		random_list = {
			40 = {
				trigger = { event_target:mourning_friend = { is_alive = yes } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					event_target:mourning_friend = { NOT = { num_of_artifacts = 1 } }
				}
				event_target:mourning_friend = { character_event = { id = lodge.341 } }
			}
			40 = {
				trigger = { event_target:mourning_lover = { is_alive = yes } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					event_target:mourning_lover = { NOT = { num_of_artifacts = 1 } }
				}
				event_target:mourning_lover = { character_event = { id = lodge.341 } }
			}
			20 = { }
		}
	}
}

# lodge.341 Mourning a warrior
# Have a weapon forged in the dead warrior's honour (if a learned courtier
# can do it), keep the dead warrior's teachings alive, or grieve alone.
# Written in the voice of the society: where the dead warrior has gone.
# Scope: ROOT is the mourner; FROM the dead warrior.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.341
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge341_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge341_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge341_greenskin }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge341_ulric }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge341_knight }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge341_dawi }
	desc = { trigger = { lodge_voice_other_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge341_any }
	picture = GFX_evt_died_battle
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes # lodge.340

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = no }
		lodge_mission_member_trigger = yes
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
	}

	immediate = {
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = dead_warrior }
	}

	option = { # A weapon in their honour
		name = EVTOPTAlodge341
		trigger = { any_courtier = { lodge_weapon_forger_trigger = yes } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge341_TT }
		set_character_flag = lodge_got_soulbound
		set_character_flag = lodge_soulbound_forging
		character_event = { id = lodge.342 days = 35 }
	}

	option = { # Their way of fighting lives on
		name = EVTOPTBlodge341
		trigger = { can_have_more_leadership_traits = yes }
		add_same_commander_trait_as_FROM = yes
	}

	option = { # Grieve alone
		name = EVTOPTClodge341
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTClodge341_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = { this_post_weight_gain_flavor_effect = yes }
	}
}

# lodge.342 The ritual
# The learned courtier knows a rite that binds the strength of the dead into
# a blade, but it needs the warrior's remains or the blood of their kin.
# Rob the grave, bleed a relative, or send the courtier away.
# Scope: ROOT is the mourner; event_target:dead_warrior.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.342
	desc = EVTDESClodge342
	picture = GFX_evt_armory
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:weapon_forger

	is_triggered_only = yes # lodge.341

	trigger = {
		any_courtier = { lodge_weapon_forger_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		event_target:dead_warrior = {
			random_close_relative = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes prisoner = no NOT = { character = ROOT } }
				save_event_target_as = potential_sacrifice
			}
		}
		lodge_find_weapon_forger_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # Rob the grave
		name = EVTOPTAlodge342
		show_portrait = event_target:dead_warrior
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge342_TT }
		set_character_flag = decided_to_dig_up
		hidden_tooltip = { lodge_soulbound_attempt_effect = yes }
	}

	option = { # The blood of kin
		name = EVTOPTBlodge342
		trigger = { event_target:potential_sacrifice = { is_alive = yes } }
		show_portrait = event_target:potential_sacrifice
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge342_TT }
		set_character_flag = decided_to_kidnap
		hidden_tooltip = { lodge_soulbound_attempt_effect = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0 is_benevolent_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }
		}
	}

	option = { # Away with you
		name = EVTOPTClodge342
		event_target:weapon_forger = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_extremely_disappointed who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
		lodge_soulbound_clear_effect = yes
	}
}

# lodge.343 Caught in the act
# Two courtiers see what the mourner was doing. Deny it, or blame the
# courtier who knew the rite. A relative who was to be bled will not forget.
# Scope: ROOT is the mourner.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.343
	desc = EVTDESClodge343
	picture = GFX_evt_armory
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:witness1

	is_triggered_only = yes # lodge_soulbound_attempt_effect

	trigger = {
		any_courtier = { count = 2 block_general_event_trigger = no }
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = { character_event = { id = lodge.344 days = 35 } }

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { block_general_event_trigger = no }
			save_event_target_as = witness1
		}
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { block_general_event_trigger = no NOT = { character = event_target:witness1 } }
			save_event_target_as = witness2
		}
	}

	option = { # It is not what you think
		name = EVTOPTAlodge343
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_heretic who = event_target:witness1 years = 5 }
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_heretic who = event_target:witness2 years = 5 }
	}

	option = { # The courtier made me do it
		name = EVTOPTBlodge343
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_heretic who = event_target:weapon_forger years = 5 }
	}

	after = {
		add_character_modifier = { name = crucible_weapon_caught_heretic duration = 1825 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = decided_to_kidnap
				event_target:potential_sacrifice = { is_alive = yes }
			}
			reverse_opinion = {
				modifier = opinion_tried_to_get_me_killed
				who = event_target:potential_sacrifice
				years = 10
			}
		}
		lodge_soulbound_clear_effect = yes
	}
}

# lodge.344 The rite is done
# The grave gave up its dead, or the kin bled out. Choose the weapon.
# Scope: ROOT is the mourner.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.344
	desc = { text = EVTDESClodge344 trigger = { has_character_flag = decided_to_dig_up } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESClodge344_blood trigger = { has_character_flag = decided_to_kidnap } }
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:dead_warrior

	is_triggered_only = yes # lodge_soulbound_attempt_effect, lodge.343

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			event_target:weapon_forger = { is_alive = yes }
			any_courtier = { lodge_weapon_forger_trigger = yes }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		lodge_find_weapon_forger_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = decided_to_kidnap
				event_target:potential_sacrifice = { is_alive = yes }
			}
			event_target:potential_sacrifice = {
				death = { death_reason = death_sacrificed killer = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # An axe
		name = EVTOPTAlodge344
		show_portrait = event_target:weapon_forger
		set_character_flag = crucible_steel_axe
	}

	option = { # A sword
		name = EVTOPTBlodge344
		show_portrait = event_target:weapon_forger
		set_character_flag = crucible_steel_sword
	}

	option = { # A mace
		name = EVTOPTClodge344
		show_portrait = event_target:weapon_forger
		set_character_flag = crucible_steel_mace
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = decided_to_kidnap
		clr_character_flag = decided_to_dig_up
		hidden_effect = { lodge_soulbound_outcome_effect = yes }
	}
}

# lodge.346 The forger is gone
# Hunt the forger down, or wait. Either way the forger may be gone for
# good, with the blade.
# Scope: ROOT is the mourner.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.346
	desc = EVTDESClodge346
	picture = GFX_evt_duel_scene
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:dead_warrior

	is_triggered_only = yes # lodge.344, lodge.351

	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = { character_event = { id = lodge.351 days = 65 } }

	immediate = {
		lodge_find_weapon_forger_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # Hunt them down
		name = EVTOPTAlodge346
		random_list = {
			30 = { # Gone with the blade
				lodge_soulbound_forger_flees_effect = yes
			}
			70 = {
				event_target:weapon_forger = {
					death = { death_reason = death_execution killer = ROOT }
				}
				remove_trait_patient_effect = yes
				add_trait_wroth_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Wait
		name = EVTOPTBlodge346
		random_list = {
			50 = { # Gone with the blade
				lodge_soulbound_forger_flees_effect = yes
				add_character_modifier = { name = crucible_weapon_very_bad_omen duration = 1825 }
			}
			50 = { # Only late
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge346_TT }
				lodge_soulbound_weapon_effect = yes
				add_trait_trusting_effect = yes
				add_trait_patient_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		lodge_soulbound_clear_effect = yes
	}
}

# lodge.347 A child and the blade
# A child of the court got hold of the new blade and hurt themselves. Throw
# the cursed thing away, or keep it.
# Scope: ROOT is the mourner.
character_event = {
	id = lodge.347
	desc = EVTDESClodge347
	picture = GFX_evt_child_play
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:dead_warrior

	is_triggered_only = yes # lodge.344, lodge.351

	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		any_courtier = { is_adult = no is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no }
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = { character_event = { id = lodge.351 days = 65 } }

	immediate = {
		lodge_find_weapon_forger_effect = yes
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { is_adult = no is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no }
			save_event_target_as = unfortunate_child
		}
	}

	option = { # Get rid of it
		name = EVTOPTAlodge347
		event_target:unfortunate_child = { show_scope_change = no add_maimed_trait_effect = yes }
	}

	option = { # Keep it
		name = EVTOPTBlodge347
		lodge_soulbound_weapon_effect = yes
		event_target:unfortunate_child = { show_scope_change = no add_maimed_trait_effect = yes }
		reverse_opinion = {
			modifier = opinion_horrified
			who = event_target:weapon_forger
			years = 5
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = crucible_weapon_very_bad_omen duration = 1825 }
	}

	after = {
		lodge_soulbound_clear_effect = yes
	}
}

	}

		name = EVTOPTAlodge348

	}

	option = { # If you say so
		name = EVTOPTBlodge348

		add_trait_cynical_effect = yes

		name = EVTOPTClodge348

		remove_trait_kind_effect = yes

		}
		else = { add_artifact = mace_tier_1_new }
		lodge_soulbound_clear_effect = yes
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The Great Hunt (was HF.25600 - HF.25631 in HF_warrior_lodges_hunting_events
#   and WB.25600 - WB.25631 in HF_warband_hunting_events)
#
# Once in a life, a Hero goes out with a partner to kill the beast that
# preys on their lands. Each faith hunts its own: Ulric's great wolf,
# a griffon of the mountains for the knights, a troll of the deeps for the
# Dawi (it must burn) and a spawn of the wastes for Chaos, Skaven and
# Greenskins. Flavour events on the way change the hunt score, which
# decides whether the hunters ambush the beast, are ambushed, or never
# find it. A knight who slays a griffon may raise its chick.
###############################################################################

# lodge.400 - The Great Hunt
# A Hero of a lodge, warband or Amazon society is called to hunt the beast of
# its faith: Ulric's great wolf, a mountain griffon (knights), a cave troll
# (Dawi) or a beast of the Chaos wastes (Chaos, Skaven, Greenskins). Pick a
# partner: a loyal friend, a strong brother or a skilled tracker.
# Scope: a Hero (society rank 3) of a lodge, warband or Amazon society; from on_actions
character_event = {
	id = lodge.400
	title = EVTTITLElodge400
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_wolf } text = EVTDESClodge400_wolf }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_griffon } text = EVTDESClodge400_griffon }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll } text = EVTDESClodge400_troll }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_beast } text = EVTDESClodge400_beast }
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_wolf }
		picture = GFX_evt_mysterious_forest
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_griffon }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_mountains
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll }
		picture = GFX_evt_troll_country
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_beast }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_chaos_wastes
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	hide_new = yes
	portrait = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter #Empty.

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_adult = yes
		in_command = no
		society_rank = 3
		prisoner = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = flag_WL_had_ritual_hunt_event }
		lodge_tombola_member_trigger = yes
		any_society_member = { count = 3 lodge_hunt_partner_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_tooltip = {
			set_character_flag = flag_WL_had_ritual_hunt_event #Fires once per character.
			WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes #Safety cleanup.
			lodge_hunt_pick_prey_effect = yes

			random_list = {
				33 = { sound_effect = pagan_cheering_01 }
				33 = { sound_effect = pagan_cheering_02 }
				33 = { sound_effect = pagan_cheering_03 }
			}
			set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
			random_society_member = { #First, a strong brother.
				limit = { lodge_hunt_partner_trigger = yes }
				preferred_limit = { trait = strong }
				preferred_limit = { combat_rating = 60 }
				preferred_limit = { combat_rating = 50 }
				preferred_limit = { combat_rating = 40 }
				preferred_limit = { combat_rating = 30 }
				preferred_limit = { combat_rating = 20 }
				preferred_limit = { combat_rating = 10 }
				preferred_limit = { combat_rating = 0 }
				preferred_limit = { is_weak_trigger = no }
				preferred_limit = { trait = brave }
				save_event_target_as = strong_hunting_partner
				set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
			}
			random_society_member = { #Then a friend: someone who likes the hunter.
				limit = {
					lodge_hunt_partner_trigger = yes
					NOT = { character = event_target:strong_hunting_partner }
					NOT = { is_rival = ROOT }
					opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
				}
				preferred_limit = { is_lover = ROOT }
				preferred_limit = { is_friend = ROOT }
				preferred_limit = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 90 } }
				preferred_limit = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 80 } }
				preferred_limit = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 70 } }
				preferred_limit = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 60 } }
				preferred_limit = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 } }
				preferred_limit = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 40 } }
				preferred_limit = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 30 } }
				save_event_target_as = loyal_hunting_partner
				set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
			}
			random_society_member = { #Finally, a clever tracker.
				limit = {
					lodge_hunt_partner_trigger = yes
					NOT = { character = event_target:loyal_hunting_partner }
					NOT = { character = event_target:strong_hunting_partner }
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					is_smart_trigger = yes
					OR = { diplomacy = 12 stewardship = 12 intrigue = 12 learning = 12 }
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					OR = { diplomacy = 12 stewardship = 12 intrigue = 12 learning = 12 }
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					is_smart_trigger = yes
					OR = { diplomacy = 10 stewardship = 10 intrigue = 10 learning = 10 }
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					OR = { diplomacy = 10 stewardship = 10 intrigue = 10 learning = 10 }
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					is_smart_trigger = yes
					OR = { diplomacy = 8 stewardship = 8 intrigue = 8 learning = 8 }
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					OR = { diplomacy = 8 stewardship = 8 intrigue = 8 learning = 8 }
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					is_smart_trigger = yes
					OR = { diplomacy = 6 stewardship = 6 intrigue = 6 learning = 6 }
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					OR = { diplomacy = 6 stewardship = 6 intrigue = 6 learning = 6 }
				}
				save_event_target_as = smart_hunting_partner
				set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Pick strong co-hunter.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge400
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge400_TT }
		trigger = { event_target:strong_hunting_partner = { is_alive = yes } }
		show_portrait = event_target:strong_hunting_partner
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:strong_hunting_partner = { save_event_target_as = scoped_WL_co_hunter }
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:loyal_hunting_partner = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:loyal_hunting_partner = { clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:smart_hunting_partner = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:smart_hunting_partner = { clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.401 days = 12 } } #Make sacrifice.
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
	option = { #Pick smart co-hunter.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge400
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge400_TT }
		trigger = { event_target:smart_hunting_partner = { is_alive = yes } }
		show_portrait = event_target:smart_hunting_partner
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:smart_hunting_partner = { save_event_target_as = scoped_WL_co_hunter }
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:loyal_hunting_partner = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:loyal_hunting_partner = { clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:strong_hunting_partner = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:strong_hunting_partner = { clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.401 days = 12 } } #Make sacrifice.
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
	option = { #Pick loyal co-hunter.
		name = EVTOPTClodge400
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTClodge400_TT }
		trigger = { event_target:loyal_hunting_partner = { is_alive = yes } }
		show_portrait = event_target:loyal_hunting_partner
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:loyal_hunting_partner = { save_event_target_as = scoped_WL_co_hunter }
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:smart_hunting_partner = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:smart_hunting_partner = { clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:strong_hunting_partner = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:strong_hunting_partner = { clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.401 days = 12 } } #Make sacrifice.
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
	option = { #Safety option, in case no partner exists.
		name = EVTOPTZlodge400
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				event_target:strong_hunting_partner = { is_alive = yes }
				event_target:smart_hunting_partner = { is_alive = yes }
				event_target:loyal_hunting_partner = { is_alive = yes }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.401 days = 12 } } #Make sacrifice.
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:strong_hunting_partner = { clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:smart_hunting_partner = { clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:loyal_hunting_partner = { clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
	option = { #Do not. - End of line.
		name = EVTOPTDlodge400
		detract_society_currency_major_effect = yes
		WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes
		tiered_piety_negative_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:strong_hunting_partner = { clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb }

		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:loyal_hunting_partner = { clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }

# lodge.401 - The offering
# Before setting out, the hunter offers something to the gods: blood, gold or
# a trophy. Ulric's and the Norscans' hunters take the hunt's tattoo,
# Greenskins their warpaint.
# Scope: the hunter
character_event = {
	id = lodge.401
	title = EVTTITLElodge400
	desc = EVTDESClodge401
	picture = GFX_evt_religious_exultation
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	portrait = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = { WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes }

	option = { #Make great sacrifice. Gain Tattoo/Warpaint, piety, hunt_variable.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge401

		if = {
			limit = { ai = no } #Cheaty AI...
			scaled_wealth = { value = -0.5 min = -50 max = -150 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 10 }
			lodge_hunt_war_paint_effect = yes
			event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { lodge_hunt_war_paint_effect = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = zealous } }
	}
	option = { #Make medium sacrifice.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge401
		if = {
			limit = { ai = no } #Cheaty AI...
			scaled_wealth = { value = -0.35 min = -25 max = -50 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 5 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = { #Make small sacrifice.
		name = EVTOPTClodge401
		if = {
			limit = { ai = no } #Cheaty AI...
			scaled_wealth = { value = -0.25 min = -5 max = -20 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 2 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}
	option = { #Make no sacrifice.
		name = EVTOPTDlodge401
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = cynical } }
	}
	after = {
		character_event = { id = lodge.402 }
	}
}

# lodge.404 - Pulled to safety
# The partner saved the hunter.
# Scope: the hunter
character_event = {
	id = lodge.404
	title = EVTTITLElodge400
	desc = EVTDESClodge404
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_wolf }
		picture = GFX_evt_mysterious_forest
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_griffon }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_mountains
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll }
		picture = GFX_evt_troll_country
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_beast }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_chaos_wastes
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = { WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes }

	#Friendship.
	#Romance.
	#Default.
	#Envious is upset.

	option = { #Befriend.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge404
		trigger = { NOT = { is_friend = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter } }
		add_friend = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 3 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #Romance.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge404
		trigger = {
			NOT = { is_lover = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter }
			event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = {
				root_attraction_trigger = yes
				attacted_to_root_trigger = yes
				NOT = { is_close_relative = ROOT }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter }
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 3 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #Default.
		name = EVTOPTClodge404
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { owes_favor_to = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter } }
			event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { add_favor = ROOT }
		}
		opinion = { who = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter modifier = opinion_saved_me years = 10 }
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 1 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #Envious.
		name = EVTOPTDlodge404
		trigger = { trait = envious }
		tooltip_info = envious
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = -1 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
}

# lodge.405 - Left to fall
# The partner did not help, and the hunter is hurt.
# Scope: the hunter
character_event = {
	id = lodge.405
	title = EVTTITLElodge400
	desc = EVTDESClodge405
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_wolf }
		picture = GFX_evt_mysterious_forest
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_griffon }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_mountains
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll }
		picture = GFX_evt_troll_country
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_beast }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_chaos_wastes
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = { WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes }

	#Rivalry.
	#Default.
	#Envious/Paranoid kills in revenge.
	#Kind forgives.

	option = { #Rivalry.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge405
		trigger = { NOT = { is_rival = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter } }
		add_rival = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = -3 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #Default.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge405
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter
			modifier = opinion_left_me_to_die
			years = 5
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = -1 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #Envious kills in revenge.
		name = EVTOPTClodge405
		trigger = { trait = envious NOT = { trait = paranoid } }
		tooltip_info = envious
		event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { death = { death_reason = death_accident } }
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 3 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #Paranoid kills in revenge.
		name = EVTOPTDlodge405
		trigger = { trait = paranoid }
		tooltip_info = paranoid
		event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { death = { death_reason = death_accident } }
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 3 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #Kind forgives.
		name = EVTOPTElodge405
		trigger = { trait = kind }
		tooltip_info = kind
		reverse_opinion = {
			who = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter
			modifier = grace_to_forgive
			years = 5

# lodge.406 - The partner falls
# The partner is about to fall. Save them, or let them go.
# Scope: the hunter
character_event = {
	id = lodge.406
	title = EVTTITLElodge400
	desc = EVTDESClodge406
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_wolf }
		picture = GFX_evt_mysterious_forest
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_griffon }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_mountains
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll }
		picture = GFX_evt_troll_country
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_beast }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_chaos_wastes
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = { WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes }

	#Combat rating saves.
	#Try to help to co-hunter.
	#Default.

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge406
		trigger = { combat_rating = 50 }
		tooltip_info = combat_rating
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge406_TT }
		tiered_prestige_reward_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 3 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 80 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge406
		trigger = { event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { is_alive = yes } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge406_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				70 = { #Co-hunter saved.
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = strong }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = robust }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { combat_rating = 10 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 martial = 10 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.75 martial = 15 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.75 martial = 20 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 martial = 25 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = craven }
					character_event = { id = lodge.407 }
				}
				30 = { #Co-hunter still injured.
					character_event = { id = lodge.427 }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 70 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTClodge406
		random_list = {
			70 = {
				add_character_modifier = { name = badly_bruised duration = 60 }
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = -2 } }
			}
			25 = {
				trigger = { NOT = { trait = wounded } }
				add_trait = wounded
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = -3 } }
			}
			5 = {
				lodge_hunt_maim_effect = yes
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = -6 } }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.416 days = 20 random = 15 } } #Find prey.
	}
}

# lodge.407 - Saved
# The hunter saved the partner.
# Scope: the hunter
character_event = {
	id = lodge.407
	title = EVTTITLElodge400
	desc = EVTDESClodge407
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_wolf }
		picture = GFX_evt_mysterious_forest
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_griffon }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_mountains
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll }
		picture = GFX_evt_troll_country
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_beast }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_chaos_wastes
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = { WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes }

	#Friendship.
	#Romance.
	#Default.

	option = { #Befriend.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge407
		trigger = { NOT = { is_friend = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter } }
		add_friend = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 3 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #Romance.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge407
		trigger = {
			NOT = { is_lover = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter }
			event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = {
				root_attraction_trigger = yes
				attacted_to_root_trigger = yes
				NOT = { is_close_relative = ROOT }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter }
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 3 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #Default.
		name = EVTOPTClodge407
		if = {
			limit = { owes_favor_to = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter }
			remove_favor = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					owes_favor_to = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter
					event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { owes_favor_to = ROOT }
				}
			}
			add_favor = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter
		}
		reverse_opinion = {
			who = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter
			modifier = opinion_saved_me
			years = 10
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 1 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
}

					hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 2 } }
				}
				50 = { #Failed Check.
					hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = -5 } }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.416 days = 20 random = 15 } } #Find prey.
	}
}

# lodge.411 - The hermit
# A hermit in the wilds may know where the beast lairs (diplomacy).
# Scope: the hunter
character_event = {
	id = lodge.411
	title = EVTTITLElodge400
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_wolf } text = EVTDESClodge411_wolf }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_griffon } text = EVTDESClodge411_griffon }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll } text = EVTDESClodge411_troll }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_beast } text = EVTDESClodge411_beast }

	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_wolf }
		picture = GFX_evt_mysterious_forest
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_griffon }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_mountains
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll }
		picture = GFX_evt_troll_country
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_beast }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_chaos_wastes
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = { WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes }

	#Special charitable override.
	#Strong override.
	#Do it yourself.
	#Let co-hunter do it.

	option = { #He must be the werewolf.
		name = EVTOPTZlodge411
		trigger = { trait = paranoid has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_wolf }
		tooltip_info = paranoid
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
		WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #charitable override.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge411
		trigger = {
			trait = charitable
			NAND = { trait = paranoid has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_wolf }
		}
		tooltip_info = charitable
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 4 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.416 days = 20 random = 15 } } #Find prey.
	}
	option = { #Strong override.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge411
		trigger = { trait = strong }
		tooltip_info = strong
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 4 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.416 days = 20 random = 15 } } #Find prey.
	}
	option = { #Robust override.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge411
		trigger = { trait = robust NOT = { trait = strong } }
		tooltip_info = robust
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 3 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.416 days = 20 random = 15 } } #Find prey.
	}
	option = { #Do it yourself.
		name = EVTOPTDlodge411
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTDlodge411_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { #Successful Check.
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = weak }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = feeble }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { combat_rating = 5 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { combat_rating = 10 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 combat_rating = 20 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 combat_rating = 25 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 combat_rating = 40 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.75 combat_rating = 60 }
					hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 2 } }
				}
				50 = { #Failed Check.
					hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = -5 } }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.416 days = 20 random = 15 } } #Find prey.
	}
	option = { #Let co-hunter do it.
		name = EVTOPTElodge411
		trigger = { event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { is_alive = yes } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTElodge411_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { #Successful Check.
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { trait = weak } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { trait = feeble }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { trait = charitable }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.75
						event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { trait = strong }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { trait = robust }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { NOT = { combat_rating = 5 } }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { NOT = { combat_rating = 10 } }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { combat_rating = 20 }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { combat_rating = 25 }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { combat_rating = 40 }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.75
						event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { combat_rating = 60 }

					hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 2 } }
				}
				50 = { #Failed Check.
					hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = -5 } }

# lodge.415 - The Wild Hunt
# A mad, possessed or wolf-cursed hunter turns on the partner. A kind hunter
# fights it off.
# Scope: the hunter
character_event = {
	id = lodge.415
	title = EVTTITLElodge415
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_modifier = lycanthropy } text = EVTDESClodge415 }
	desc = { trigger = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = lycanthropy } } text = EVTDESClodge415_b }
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_wolf }
		picture = GFX_evt_mysterious_forest
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_griffon }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_mountains
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll }
		picture = GFX_evt_troll_country
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_beast }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_chaos_wastes
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = { WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes }

	#Kill.
	#Proceed alone.
	#Kind contains.

	option = { #
		name = EVTOPTZlodge415
		trigger = { has_character_modifier = lycanthropy }
		piety = 100
		event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = {
			death = { death_reason = death_accident_generic_beast }
		}
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
		WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #
		name = EVTOPTAlodge415
		trigger = {
			OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
			NOT = { trait = possessed }
		}
		tooltip_info_custom = lunatic
		piety = 100
		event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = {
			death = { death_reason = death_accident_generic_beast }
		}
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
		WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #
		name = EVTOPTBlodge415
		trigger = {
			OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
			NOT = { trait = possessed }
		}
		tooltip_info_custom = lunatic
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge415_TT }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = wounded } } random = { chance = 25 add_trait = wounded } }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clear_event_target = scoped_WL_co_hunter
			change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = -4 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.416 days = 20 random = 15 } } #Find prey.
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #
		name = EVTOPTAlodge415
		trigger = {
			trait = possessed
			NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
		}
		tooltip_info = possessed
		piety = 100
		event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = {
			death = { death_reason = death_accident_generic_beast }
		}
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
		WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #
		name = EVTOPTBlodge415
		trigger = {
			trait = possessed
			NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
		}
		tooltip_info = possessed
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge415_TT }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = wounded } } random = { chance = 25 add_trait = wounded } }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clear_event_target = scoped_WL_co_hunter
			change_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = -4 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.416 days = 20 random = 15 } } #Find prey.
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #Kind saves.
		name = EVTOPTClodge415
		trigger = { trait = kind }
		tooltip_info = kind
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.416 days = 20 random = 15 } } #Find prey.
	}
}

# lodge.416 - Finding the prey
# After the flavour events, the hunt score decides: an ambush by the beast, an
# ambush of the beast, or no trail at all.
# Scope: the hunter
character_event = {
	id = lodge.416
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = { WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes }

	#Completely missed prey.
	#Prey ambushed hunters.
	#Hunters ambushed prey.

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				trigger = { #Completely missed prey.
					NOT = { check_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 0 } }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = imbecile }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = inbred }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { trait = imbecile } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { trait = inbred } }
				character_event = { id = lodge.417 }
			}
			25 = { #Prey ambushed hunters.
				character_event = { id = lodge.418 }
			}
			25 = { #Hunters ambushed prey.
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					NOT = { check_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = -5 } }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 check_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 3 } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.75
					check_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 6 }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.75
					check_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 9 }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.75
					check_variable = { which = wl_ritual_hunting value = 12 }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = strong }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = robust }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = weak }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = feeble }
				modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = hunter }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 combat_rating = 15 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 combat_rating = 25 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 combat_rating = 35 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 combat_rating = 45 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 combat_rating = 55 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 combat_rating = 65 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 combat_rating = 75 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { trait = strong } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { trait = robust } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { trait = weak } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { trait = feeble } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.75 event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { trait = hunter } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.25
					event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { combat_rating = 15 }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.25
					event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { combat_rating = 25 }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.25
					event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { combat_rating = 35 }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.25
					event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { combat_rating = 45 }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { combat_rating = 55 }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { combat_rating = 65 }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { combat_rating = 75 }
				}
				character_event = { id = lodge.419 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# lodge.420 - The kill
# The beast is dead. Take a trophy, burn it for the gods, share the meat with
# the society or, for a knight who slew a griffon, search its nest.
# Scope: the hunter
character_event = {
	id = lodge.420
	title = EVTTITLElodge400
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_wolf } text = EVTDESClodge420_wolf }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_griffon } text = EVTDESClodge420_griffon }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll } text = EVTDESClodge420_troll }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_beast } text = EVTDESClodge420_beast }

	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_wolf }
		picture = GFX_evt_mysterious_forest
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_griffon }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_mountains
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll }
		picture = GFX_evt_troll_country
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_beast }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_chaos_wastes
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = { WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes }

	#Pick trophy (dyn modifier).
	#Burn the carcass for the gods (Dawi: burn the troll).
	#Take meat to share with society members.
	#Knights: search the griffon nest.

	option = { #Pick trophy.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge420
		lodge_hunt_trophy_effect = yes
		tiered_prestige_reward_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	option = { #Burn the carcass for the gods (Dawi: burn the troll).
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBlodge420
			trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll } }
		}
		name = { text = EVTOPTBlodge420_troll trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll } }
		if = { limit = { society_rank == 1 } piety = 25 }
		else_if = { limit = { society_rank == 2 } piety = 50 }
		else_if = { limit = { society_rank == 3 } piety = 75 }
		else_if = { limit = { society_rank == 4 } piety = 100 }

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll }
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.426 } } #Fluff.
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	option = { #Take meat to share with society members.
		name = EVTOPTClodge420
		if = { limit = { society_rank == 1 } prestige = 50 }
		else_if = { limit = { society_rank == 2 } prestige = 100 }
		else_if = { limit = { society_rank == 3 } prestige = 150 }
		else_if = { limit = { society_rank == 4 } prestige = 250 }

		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.428 } } #Fluff.
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	option = { #Knights search the griffon nest.
		name = EVTOPTDlodge420
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_griffon }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTDlodge420_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.429 } } #Fluff.
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}

	after = {
		add_legend_progress_minor_effect = yes
		WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { is_alive = yes } }
			reverse_opinion = {
				who = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter
				modifier = opinion_hunted_with_me
				years = 8
			}
		}
	}
}

	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_beast } text = EVTDESClodge424_beast }

		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_wolf }
		picture = GFX_evt_mysterious_forest
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_griffon }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_mountains
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll }
		picture = GFX_evt_troll_country
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_beast }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_chaos_wastes
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = { WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes }

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge424
		if = {
			limit = { society_rank == 1 }
			prestige = -50
			detract_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { society_rank == 2 }
			prestige = -100
			detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { society_rank == 3 }
			prestige = -150
			detract_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { society_rank == 4 }
			prestige = -200
			detract_society_currency_major_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { is_alive = yes } }
			event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = {
				death = { death_reason = death_accident_generic_beast }
			}
		}
		WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
}

	id = lodge.425
	title = EVTTITLElodge400
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_wolf } text = EVTDESClodge424_wolf }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_griffon } text = EVTDESClodge424_griffon }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll } text = EVTDESClodge424_troll }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_beast } text = EVTDESClodge424_beast }

	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_wolf }
		picture = GFX_evt_mysterious_forest
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_griffon }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_mountains
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_troll }
		picture = GFX_evt_troll_country
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lodge_hunt_beast }
		picture = GFX_evt_questing_chaos_wastes
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = { WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes }

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge425
		if = {
			limit = { society_rank == 1 }
			prestige = -50
			detract_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { society_rank == 2 }
			prestige = -100
			detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { society_rank == 3 }
			prestige = -150
			detract_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { society_rank == 4 }
			prestige = -200
			detract_society_currency_major_effect = yes
		}
		lodge_hunt_wound_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = { is_alive = yes } }
			event_target:scoped_WL_co_hunter = {
				death = { death_reason = death_accident_generic_beast }
			}
		}
		WL_ritual_hunt_cleanup_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
}

# lodge.431 - The eagle dies
# A hunting eagle dies of age (also used by the eagle bloodline and other
# eagle sources).
# Scope: the eagle's owner
character_event = {
	id = lodge.431
	desc = EVTDESClodge431
	picture = GFX_evt_falconing
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	portrait = ROOT
	hide_new = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Invite the whole court
		name = EVTOPTAlodge431
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge431_TT }
		scaled_wealth = -0.6
		if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = greedy trait = charitable } } add_trait = charitable }
		if = { limit = { trait = greedy } remove_trait = greedy }
		remove_character_modifier = pet_hunting_eagle
	}
	option = { # Regular funeral
		name = EVTOPTBlodge431
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge431_TT }
		remove_character_modifier = pet_hunting_eagle
	}
	after = {
		send_new_eagle_event_effect = yes #Only for certain bloodlines...!
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Feasts (was HF.25200 - HF.25301 in HF_warrior_lodge_feast_events and
#   WB.25200 - WB.25301 in HF_warband_feast_events)
#
# The society feasts, and its members compete: arm-wrestling (with cheats,
# and admirers or friends for the winner), the dice game (players only),
# the flyting, a contest of insults, and a Hero's drinking contest. The
# shared steps live in common/scripted_effects/
# 05_scripted_effects_competitions.txt.
###############################################################################

# --- Arm-wrestling ---------------------------------------------------------

# lodge.500 - The arm-wrestling challenge
# At a feast of the society, a member picks an opponent: the strongest in the
# hall, a fair match or an easy one.
# Scope: a member of a lodge, warband, Amazon society or witch hunter order; from on_actions
character_event = {
	id = lodge.500
	title = EVTTITLElodge500
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge500_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge500_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge500_greenskin }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge500_ulric }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge500_knight }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge500_dawi }
	desc = { trigger = { lodge_voice_other_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge500_any }
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	hide_new = yes

	trigger = {
		is_adult = yes
		in_command = no
		prisoner = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = arm_wrestling_challenge_cooldown }
		lodge_feast_member_trigger = yes
		any_society_member = { #Make sure there are enough contenders to pick.
			same_society_as = ROOT
			is_adult = yes
			in_command = no
			prisoner = no
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = arm_wrestling_challenge_cooldown }
			NOT = { character = ROOT }
			OR = {
				is_strong_trigger = yes
				trait = duelist
				trait = skilled_tactician
				trait = barbarian_4
				trait = brilliant_strategist
				combat_rating = 60
			}
			any_society_member = {
				same_society_as = ROOT
				is_adult = yes
				in_command = no
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = arm_wrestling_challenge_cooldown }
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				NOT = { has_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { character = PREV } # Do not check for the same character.
				NOR = {
					is_strong_trigger = yes
					has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes
					combat_rating = 60

				count = 2
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {

			33 = { sound_effect = pagan_cheering_01 }
			33 = { sound_effect = pagan_cheering_02 }
			33 = { sound_effect = pagan_cheering_03 }
			33 = { sound_effect = warrior_lodge_ambient_02 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { #Pick potential contenders
			clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong #Safety cleanup
			clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
			clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak
			save_event_target_as = arm_wrestling_fighter_1
			random_society_member = { #First pick a very strong guy.
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					is_adult = yes
					in_command = no
					prisoner = no
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
					NOT = { trait = incapable }
					NOT = { has_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = arm_wrestling_challenge_cooldown }
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					OR = {
						is_strong_trigger = yes
						trait = duelist
						trait = skilled_tactician
						trait = barbarian_3
						trait = barbarian_4
						trait = brilliant_strategist
						combat_rating = 60
					}
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					ai = no
					is_strong_trigger = yes
					is_ill = no
					has_education_martial_trigger = yes
					has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes
					combat_rating = 60
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					ai = no
					is_strong_trigger = yes
					has_education_martial_trigger = yes
					has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes
					combat_rating = 60
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					ai = no
					is_strong_trigger = yes
					OR = { has_education_martial_trigger = yes has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes }
					combat_rating = 60
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					ai = no
					OR = {
						is_strong_trigger = yes
						has_education_martial_trigger = yes
						has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes
					}
					combat_rating = 60
				}
				preferred_limit = { ai = no combat_rating = 60 }
				preferred_limit = {
					is_strong_trigger = yes
					is_ill = no
					has_education_martial_trigger = yes
					has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes
					combat_rating = 20
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					is_strong_trigger = yes
					has_education_martial_trigger = yes
					has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes
					combat_rating = 20
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					is_strong_trigger = yes
					OR = { has_education_martial_trigger = yes has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes }
					combat_rating = 20
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					OR = {
						is_strong_trigger = yes
						has_education_martial_trigger = yes
						has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes
					}
					combat_rating = 20
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					ai = no
					is_strong_trigger = yes
					is_ill = no
					has_education_martial_trigger = yes
					has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes
					combat_rating = 20
				}
				preferred_limit = {

					is_strong_trigger = yes

					has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes
					combat_rating = 20

					is_strong_trigger = yes

					combat_rating = 20
				}
				preferred_limit = {

					OR = {
						is_strong_trigger = yes

						has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes

					combat_rating = 20
				}
				preferred_limit = { ai = no combat_rating = 20 }
				preferred_limit = {
					is_strong_trigger = yes
					is_ill = no
					has_education_martial_trigger = yes
					has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes
					combat_rating = 20
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					is_strong_trigger = yes
					has_education_martial_trigger = yes
					has_lifestyle_martial_trigger = yes

					combat_rating = 20

				preferred_limit = { combat_rating = 90 }
				preferred_limit = { combat_rating = 60 }

					NOT = { trait = incapable }

					NOT = { event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_1 = { character = PREV } }
					NOT = { event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_3 = { character = PREV } }

				preferred_limit = {
					is_ill = no
					has_education_martial_trigger = yes
					combat_rating = 25
				}
				preferred_limit = { has_education_martial_trigger = yes combat_rating = 25 }
				preferred_limit = { combat_rating = 25 }

					is_ill = no
					has_education_martial_trigger = yes
					combat_rating = 0
				}
				preferred_limit = { has_education_martial_trigger = yes combat_rating = 0 }
				preferred_limit = { combat_rating = 0 }
				preferred_limit = { is_weak_trigger = no }
				clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong #Safety cleanup
				clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
				clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak
				save_event_target_as = arm_wrestling_opponent_2
			}
			#Then assign flag to determine what kind of opponent you are.
			if = {
				limit = { OR = { is_strong_trigger = yes combat_rating = 60 } }
				set_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = { is_strong_trigger = yes is_weak_trigger = yes combat_rating = 60 }
					combat_rating = 25
				}
				set_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { OR = { is_weak_trigger = yes NOT = { combat_rating = 25 } } }
				set_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak
			}
			#Safety check
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong
						has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
						has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
			}
			set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = { id = lodge.601 days = 15 } #Clear flag.
			}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge500
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge500_TT }
		trigger = { event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_1 = { is_alive = yes } }
		show_portrait = event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_1
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_1 = {
				save_event_target_as = arm_wrestling_fighter_2
				set_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong
				set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.601 days = 15 } #Clear flag.
				}
				character_event = { id = lodge.501 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_2 = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_2 = {
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak
					clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_3 = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_3 = {
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak
					clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				}
			}
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge500
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge500_TT }
		trigger = { event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_2 = { is_alive = yes } }
		show_portrait = event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_2
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_2 = {
				save_event_target_as = arm_wrestling_fighter_2
				set_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
				set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.601 days = 15 } #Clear flag.
				}
				character_event = { id = lodge.501 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_1 = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_1 = {
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak
					clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_3 = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_3 = {
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak
					clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTClodge500
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTClodge500_TT }
		trigger = { event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_3 = { is_alive = yes } }
		show_portrait = event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_3
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong }
			tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_3 = {
				save_event_target_as = arm_wrestling_fighter_2
				set_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak
				set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.601 days = 15 } #Clear flag.
				}
				character_event = { id = lodge.501 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_1 = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_1 = {
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak
					clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_2 = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_2 = {
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak
					clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {

			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = brave }
		}
	}
	option = { #Not interested. -End of line.
		name = EVTOPTDlodge500
		tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes

		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium }
			detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak }
			detract_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {

				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_1 = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_opponent_1 = {
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
					clr_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak
					clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				}

# lodge.501 - Challenged
# The opponent hears the challenge. Refusing is remembered.
# Scope: the challenged member; FROM is the challenger
character_event = {
	id = lodge.501
	title = EVTTITLElodge500
	desc = EVTDESClodge501
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = { warband_arm_wrestling_cleanup_effect = yes }

	option = { #Accept Challenge.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge501
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge501_TT }
		show_portrait = FROM
		FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.503 } }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				name = arm_wrestling_challenge_cooldown
				hidden = yes
				years = 2
			}
			FROM = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					name = arm_wrestling_challenge_cooldown
					hidden = yes
					years = 2
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 95 }
	}

	option = { #Not interested. -End of line.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge501
		tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong }
			detract_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium }
			detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak }
			detract_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = craven }
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge501_TT }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				any_society_member = {
					limit = { same_society_as = ROOT NOT = { character = ROOT } }
					opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_coward years = 5 }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { trait = brave trait = craven } }
			random = {
				chance = 5
				add_trait = craven
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38257 } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = brave }
			random = {
				chance = 5
				remove_trait = brave
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38320 } }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.502 } } #Notify.
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 5 modifier = { factor = 5 trait = craven } }
	}
}

# lodge.505 - Losing ground
# The underdog of the first round may cheat: slide the elbow, wink, lean in or
# point behind the foe.
# Scope: the member who is losing
character_event = {
	id = lodge.505
	title = EVTTITLElodge500
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge505
		trigger = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = { character = ROOT } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge505_b
		trigger = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = { character = ROOT } }
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = { warband_arm_wrestling_cleanup_effect = yes }

	option = { #Slide elbow.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge505
		trigger = { intrigue = 8 }
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTDlodge505_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = { character_event = { id = lodge.506 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = { character_event = { id = lodge.506 } }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Charm distraction. (male)
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBlodge505_a
			trigger = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = { character = ROOT } }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBlodge505_b
			trigger = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = { character = ROOT } }
		}
		trigger = {
			FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
			prefers_men_trigger = no
			is_attractive_trigger = yes
			NOT = { trait = celibate }
		}
		tooltip_info_custom = available_because_pretty
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTDlodge505_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = { character_event = { id = lodge.509 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = { character_event = { id = lodge.509 } }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #Charm distraction. (female)
		name = EVTOPTClodge505
		trigger = {
			FROM = { is_female = no }
			prefers_men_trigger = yes
			is_attractive_trigger = yes
			NOT = { trait = celibate }
		}
		tooltip_info_custom = available_because_pretty
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTDlodge505_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = { character_event = { id = lodge.509 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = { character_event = { id = lodge.509 } }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #Cheap distraction.
		name = EVTOPTDlodge505
		detract_society_currency_trivial_effect = yes
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTDlodge505_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = { character = ROOT } }

		}

			factor = 10

			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = just }

			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = deceitful }
