			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = just }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = honest }
		}
	}
}

# lodge.506 - A sliding elbow
# The foe cheats by sliding an elbow.
# Scope: the member who was winning; FROM the cheat
character_event = {
	id = lodge.506
	title = EVTTITLElodge500
	desc = EVTDESClodge506
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = { warband_arm_wrestling_cleanup_effect = yes }

	option = { #Saw it!
		name = EVTOPTAlodge506
		trigger = { intrigue = 13 }
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Saw it!
		name = EVTOPTAlodge506
		trigger = { trait = genius NOT = { intrigue = 13 } }
		tooltip_info = genius
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Saw it!
		name = EVTOPTAlodge506
		trigger = { trait = quick NOR = { trait = genius intrigue = 13 } }
		tooltip_info = quick
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Saw it!
		name = EVTOPTAlodge506
		trigger = { trait = shrewd NOR = { trait = quick trait = genius intrigue = 13 } }
		tooltip_info = quick
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}

	option = { #How could this be?!
		name = EVTOPTBlodge506
		trigger = { is_dumb_trigger = no }
		set_character_flag = wl_arm_wrestling_being_tricked_1
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}

	option = { #What is happening?
		name = EVTOPTClodge506
		trigger = { is_dumb_trigger = yes }
		tooltip_info_custom = available_because_dumb
		set_character_flag = wl_arm_wrestling_being_tricked_2
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	after = {
		warband_arm_wrestling_second_round_effect = yes #Decide who wins.
	}
}

# lodge.509 - A wink
# The foe tries to charm the winner.
# Scope: the member who was winning; FROM the cheat
character_event = {
	id = lodge.509
	title = EVTTITLElodge500
	desc = { text = EVTDESClodge509 trigger = { FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESClodge509_b trigger = { FROM = { is_woman_trigger = no } } }
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = { warband_arm_wrestling_cleanup_effect = yes }
	option = { #Not interested.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge509
		trigger = { trait = celibate }
		tooltip_info = celibate
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Not interested.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge509
		trigger = {
			NOT = { trait = celibate }
			trait = chaste
			OR = { num_of_spouses = 1 num_of_lovers = 1 }
			FROM = { NOR = { is_married = ROOT is_lover = ROOT } }
		}
		tooltip_info = chaste
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Wrong sex.
		name = EVTOPTClodge509
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } prefers_men_trigger = yes }
				AND = { FROM = { is_female = no } prefers_men_trigger = no }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Failed check.
		name = EVTOPTDlodge509
		trigger = {
			NOT = { trait = lustful }
			OR = {
				AND = { FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } prefers_men_trigger = no }
				AND = { FROM = { is_female = no } prefers_men_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
		set_character_flag = wl_arm_wrestling_being_tricked_1
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
	option = { #Critical Fail check.
		name = { text = EVTOPTElodge509_a trigger = { FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTElodge509_b trigger = { FROM = { is_woman_trigger = no } } }
		trigger = {
			trait = lustful
			OR = {
				AND = { FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } prefers_men_trigger = no }
				AND = { FROM = { is_female = no } prefers_men_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
		tooltip_info = lustful
		set_character_flag = wl_arm_wrestling_being_tricked_2
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
	after = {
		warband_arm_wrestling_second_round_effect = yes #Decide who wins.
	}
}

# lodge.515 - Victory
# The winner may mock or console the loser.
# Scope: the winner
character_event = {
	id = lodge.515
	title = EVTTITLElodge500
	desc = EVTDESClodge515
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = { warband_arm_wrestling_cleanup_effect = yes }
	option = { #Mock loser.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge515
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge515_TT }
		#Influence reward based on opponent's strength.
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong
					}
					event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong
					}
				}
			}
			add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
			hidden_tooltip = { #If defeated a strong guy, gain nickname.
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOR = {
							this_has_average_nickname_trigger = yes
							this_has_cool_nickname_trigger = yes
							this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					random_list = {
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_strongarm }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_iron_grip }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_knuckle_slammer }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_gods_arm }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_bone_squeezer }
						25 = { trigger = { is_female = no } give_nickname = nick_the_bull }
						25 = { trigger = { is_woman_trigger = yes } give_nickname = nick_the_butch }
						15 = { }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
					}
					event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
					}
				}
			}
			add_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak
					}
					event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak
					}
				}
			}
			add_society_currency_trivial_effect = yes
		}
		tooltip = {
			#Notify loser.
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = {
					show_scope_change = no
					opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_lousy_winner years = 5 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = {
					show_scope_change = no
					opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_lousy_winner years = 5 }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			#Notify loser.
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = { character_event = { id = lodge.516 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = { character_event = { id = lodge.516 } }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_evil_trigger = yes } }
	}
	option = { #Kind to loser.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge515
		#Influence reward based on opponent's strength.
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong
					}
					event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong
					}
				}
			}
			add_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
			hidden_tooltip = { #If defeated a strong guy, gain nickname.
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOR = {
							this_has_average_nickname_trigger = yes
							this_has_cool_nickname_trigger = yes
							this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					random_list = {
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_strongarm }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_iron_grip }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_knuckle_slammer }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_gods_arm }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_bone_squeezer }
						25 = { trigger = { is_female = no } give_nickname = nick_the_bull }
						25 = { trigger = { is_woman_trigger = yes } give_nickname = nick_the_butch }
						15 = { }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
					}
					event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium
					}
				}
			}
			add_society_currency_trivial_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak
					}
					event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak
					}
				}
			}
			prestige = 10
		}
		tooltip = {
			#Notify loser.
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = {
					show_scope_change = no
					opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_kind_winner years = 5 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = {
					show_scope_change = no
					opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_kind_winner years = 5 }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			#Notify loser.
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = { character_event = { id = lodge.517 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_2 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:arm_wrestling_fighter_1 = { character_event = { id = lodge.517 } }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_benevolent_trigger = yes } }
	}
	after = {
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = { post_arm_wrestling_flavor_effect = yes }
	}
}

# lodge.516 - Mocked
# The loser is mocked, and may call a duel.
# Scope: the loser; FROM the winner
character_event = {
	id = lodge.516
	title = EVTTITLElodge500
	desc = EVTDESClodge516
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = { warband_arm_wrestling_cleanup_effect = yes }

	option = { #Special option. - character starts duel!
		name = EVTOPTYlodge516
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTYlodge516_TT }
		trigger = { this_can_duel_with_from_trigger = yes }
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_antagonized_3 who = ROOT years = 15 }
		#REQUIRED event targets for setting up duel evaluation!
		hidden_tooltip = {
			save_event_target_as = combatant_1 #the person issuing the duel...
			FROM = { save_event_target_as = combatant_2 } #the target of the duel...
		}
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			apply_degree_of_dishonorable_duel_effect = yes #checks a bunch of age and health statuses...
		}

		pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = recent_duel_timer duration = 365 hidden = yes }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = { character_event = { id = duel.1 days = 1 } } #Duel evaluation (hidden) + result event as follow-up.
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 40
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 is_tribal = no is_nomadic = no }
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.25
				ROOT = { attribute_diff = { character = FROM attribute = combat_rating value = 5 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.25
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = { character = FROM attribute = combat_rating value = 10 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = { character = FROM attribute = combat_rating value = 15 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.75
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = { character = FROM attribute = combat_rating value = 20 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge516
		#Influence penalty based on opponent's strength.
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_strong } }
			detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_medium } }
			detract_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = arm_wrestling_weak } }
			detract_society_currency_trivial_effect = yes
			hidden_tooltip = { #If defeated by a weak guy, gain nickname.
				if = {
					limit = { has_nickname = no }
					random_list = {
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_feeble }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_milkdrinker }
						25 = { trigger = { is_female = no } give_nickname = nick_the_girls_arm }
						25 = { trigger = { is_woman_trigger = yes } give_nickname = nick_the_flower }
						25 = { }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_lousy_winner years = 5 }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge516_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_society_member = {
				limit = { is_adult = yes same_society_as = ROOT NOT = { character = FROM } }
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_loser years = 2 }
			}
			warband_arm_wrestling_cleanup_effect = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}

# lodge.519 - An admirer
# The winner is told of the admirer's feelings.
# Scope: the winner; FROM the admirer
character_event = {
	id = lodge.519
	desc = EVTDESClodge519
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	option = { #Yes.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge519
		if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no FROM = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = FROM }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.520 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0 #Incompatible
				FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				prefers_men_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0 #Incompatible
				FROM = { is_female = no }
				prefers_men_trigger = no
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0 #Uninterested
				trait = celibate
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0 #Uninterested
				trait = chaste
				NOT = { is_married = FROM }
				is_married = yes
			}
			modifier = { factor = 0.85 NOT = { age = 30 } FROM = { age = 30 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 NOT = { age = 40 } FROM = { age = 40 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 NOT = { age = 50 } FROM = { age = 50 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { is_attractive_trigger = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.35 FROM = { is_unattractive_trigger = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_fair_trait_trigger = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = strong } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { is_strong_trigger = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { is_weak_trigger = yes } }
		}
	}
	option = { #No.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge519
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.521 } } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 modifier = { factor = 0 trait = lustful } }
	}
}

# --- The dice game ---------------------------------------------------------

# lodge.526 - The dice game
# A player member is asked to join the dice at the feast. Restored: it served
# only two vanilla lodges.
# Scope: a player member of a lodge, warband, Amazon society or witch hunter order; from on_actions
character_event = {
	id = lodge.526
	title = EVTTITLElodge526
	desc = EVTDESClodge526
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	ai = no

	trigger = {
		ai = no #Only involves one character.
		is_adult = yes
		in_command = no
		prisoner = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
		lodge_feast_member_trigger = yes
		wealth = 75
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			33 = { sound_effect = pagan_cheering_01 }
			33 = { sound_effect = pagan_cheering_02 }
			33 = { sound_effect = pagan_cheering_03 }
			33 = { sound_effect = warrior_lodge_ambient_02 }
		}
		warrior_lodge_dice_game_cleanup_effect = yes
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Start playing.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge526
		character_event = { id = lodge.528 }
	}
	option = { #Refuse to join.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge526
		detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
	}
}

# lodge.528 - The bet
# Bet on a face of the bone die: the Skull (long odds, big stake), the Sword,
# the Crown or the Coin (safe, small stake).
# Scope: the player
character_event = {
	id = lodge.528
	title = EVTTITLElodge526
	desc = EVTDESClodge528
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDlodge528
		trigger = {
			OR = { wealth = 500 AND = { OR = { is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes } wealth = 100 } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_hardest_side }
		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes } }
			custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_500_gold }
			set_character_flag = flag_betting_money_500
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes } }
			custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_100_gold }
			set_character_flag = flag_betting_money_100
		}
		set_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_skull
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTClodge528
		trigger = {
			OR = { wealth = 250 AND = { OR = { is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes } wealth = 50 } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_hard_side }
		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes } }
			custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_250_gold }
			set_character_flag = flag_betting_money_250
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes } }
			custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_50_gold }
			set_character_flag = flag_betting_money_50
		}
		set_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_sword
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge528
		trigger = {
			OR = { wealth = 100 AND = { OR = { is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes } wealth = 25 } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_easy_side }
		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes } }
			custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_100_gold }
			set_character_flag = flag_betting_money_100
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes } }
			custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_25_gold }
			set_character_flag = flag_betting_money_25
		}
		set_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_crown
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge528
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_easiest_side }
		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes } }
			custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_50_gold }
			set_character_flag = flag_betting_money_50
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes } }
			custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_10_gold }
			set_character_flag = flag_betting_money_10
		}
		set_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_coin
	}
	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.529 } }
	}
}

# lodge.529 - The throw
# Throw fair, swap in a weighted die (intrigue or schemer) or bump the table
# (intrigue or deceitful).
# Scope: the player
character_event = {
	id = lodge.529
	title = EVTTITLElodge526
	desc = EVTDESClodge529
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Use weighted die.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge529
		trigger = { intrigue = 15 }
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		random_list = {
			30 = {
				#Discovered.
				custom_tooltip = { text = TT_FAKE_DIE_DISCOVERED }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.530 } }
			}
			70 = {
				#Unnoticed.
				custom_tooltip = { text = TT_FAKE_DIE_WINS }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.531 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Use weighted die.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge529
		trigger = { trait = schemer NOT = { intrigue = 15 } }
		tooltip_info = schemer
		random_list = {
			30 = {
				#Discovered.
				custom_tooltip = { text = TT_FAKE_DIE_DISCOVERED }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.530 } }
			}
			70 = {
				#Unnoticed.
				custom_tooltip = { text = TT_FAKE_DIE_WINS }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.531 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Bump under the table.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge529
		trigger = { intrigue = 8 }
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		random_list = {
			30 = {
				#Discovered.
				custom_tooltip = { text = TT_DIE_TABLE_BUMP_DISCOVERED }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.532 } }
			}
			10 = {
				#Fails
				custom_tooltip = { text = TT_DIE_TABLE_BUMP_FAILS }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.533 } }
			}
			60 = {
				#Unnoticed.
				custom_tooltip = { text = TT_DIE_TABLE_BUMP_WINS }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.534 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Bump under the table.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge529
		trigger = { trait = deceitful NOT = { intrigue = 8 } }
		tooltip_info = deceitful
		random_list = {
			30 = {
				#Discovered.
				custom_tooltip = { text = TT_DIE_TABLE_BUMP_DISCOVERED }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.532 } }
			}
			10 = {
				#Fails
				custom_tooltip = { text = TT_DIE_TABLE_BUMP_FAILS }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.533 } }
			}
			60 = {
				#Unnoticed.
				custom_tooltip = { text = TT_DIE_TABLE_BUMP_WINS }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.534 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Play fair.
		name = EVTOPTClodge529
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTClodge529_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				60 = {
					#Coin.
					set_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_coin
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.535 } }
				}
				25 = {
					#Crown.
					set_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_crown
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.535 } }
				}
				10 = {
					#Sword.
					set_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_sword
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.535 } }
				}
				5 = {
					#Skull.
					set_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_skull
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.535 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# lodge.530 - Caught with a weighted die
# Pay, call in or give a favour, or fight a duel over it.
# Scope: the player
character_event = {
	id = lodge.530
	title = EVTTITLElodge526
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge530
		trigger = { event_target:random_society_winner = { is_alive = no } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge530_b
		trigger = { event_target:random_society_winner = { is_alive = yes } }
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_society_member = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				same_society_as = ROOT
				NOR = { character = ROOT trait = incapable }
			}
			save_event_target_as = random_society_winner
		}
	}

	option = { #Pay.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge530
		trigger = { event_target:random_society_winner = { is_alive = no } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge530_TT }
		detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		warrior_lodge_pay_dice_debt_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_society_member = {
				limit = { is_adult = yes same_society_as = ROOT NOT = { character = ROOT } }
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_cheater years = 5 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Special option. - Character starts duel!
		name = EVTOPTYlodge530
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTYlodge530_TT }
		trigger = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = {
				ai = yes
				is_alive = yes
				this_can_duel_with_from_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes
		warrior_lodge_dice_game_cleanup_effect = yes
		#REQUIRED event targets for setting up duel evaluation!
		hidden_tooltip = {
			save_event_target_as = combatant_1 #the person issuing the duel...
			event_target:random_society_winner = { save_event_target_as = combatant_2 } #the target of the duel...
		}
		event_target:random_society_winner = {
			show_scope_change = no
			apply_degree_of_dishonorable_duel_effect = yes #checks a bunch of age and health statuses...
		}

		pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = recent_duel_timer duration = 365 hidden = yes }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { character_event = { id = duel.1 days = 1 } } #Duel evaluation (hidden) + result event as follow-up.
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 is_tribal = no is_nomadic = no }
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.25
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:random_society_winner
						attribute = combat_rating
						value = 5
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.25
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:random_society_winner
						attribute = combat_rating
						value = 10
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:random_society_winner
						attribute = combat_rating
						value = 15
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.75
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:random_society_winner
						attribute = combat_rating
						value = 20
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Pay your debtor.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge530
		trigger = { event_target:random_society_winner = { is_alive = yes } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge530_TT }
		detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		warrior_lodge_pay_dice_debt_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { character_event = { id = lodge.539 } } #Notify other warrior.
			any_society_member = {
				limit = { is_adult = yes same_society_as = ROOT NOT = { character = ROOT } }
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_cheater years = 5 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Call in favor.
		name = EVTOPTClodge530
		trigger = { event_target:random_society_winner = { is_alive = yes owes_favor_to = ROOT } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge530_TT }
		detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		event_target:random_society_winner = { remove_favor = ROOT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { character_event = { id = lodge.540 } } #Notify other warrior.
			any_society_member = {
				limit = { is_adult = yes same_society_as = ROOT NOT = { character = ROOT } }
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_cheater years = 5 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Pay with favor.
		name = EVTOPTDlodge530
		trigger = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { is_alive = yes NOT = { owes_favor_to = ROOT } }
			NOT = { owes_favor_to = event_target:random_society_winner }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge530_TT }
		detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		event_target:random_society_winner = { add_favor = ROOT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { character_event = { id = lodge.541 } } #Notify other warrior.
			any_society_member = {
				limit = { is_adult = yes same_society_as = ROOT NOT = { character = ROOT } }
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_cheater years = 5 }
			}
		}
	}
	after = {
		warrior_lodge_dice_game_cleanup_effect = yes
	}
}

# lodge.531 - The weighted die wins
# Double or nothing?
# Scope: the player
character_event = {
	id = lodge.531
	title = EVTTITLElodge526
	desc = EVTDESClodge531
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Double or nothing.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge531
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_betting_money_500 }
			clr_character_flag = flag_betting_money_500
			set_character_flag = flag_betting_money_1000
			custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_1000_gold }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_betting_money_250 }
			clr_character_flag = flag_betting_money_250
			set_character_flag = flag_betting_money_500
			custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_500_gold }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_betting_money_100 }
			clr_character_flag = flag_betting_money_100
			set_character_flag = flag_betting_money_200
			custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_200_gold }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_betting_money_50 }
			clr_character_flag = flag_betting_money_50
			set_character_flag = flag_betting_money_100
			custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_100_gold }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_betting_money_25 }
			clr_character_flag = flag_betting_money_25
			set_character_flag = flag_betting_money_50
			custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_50_gold }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_betting_money_10 }
			clr_character_flag = flag_betting_money_10
			set_character_flag = flag_betting_money_20
			custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_betting_20_gold }
		}
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				#Discovered.
				custom_tooltip = { text = TT_FAKE_DIE_DISCOVERED }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.530 } }
			}
			50 = {
				#Unnoticed.
				custom_tooltip = { text = TT_FAKE_DIE_WINS }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.537 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Leave while ahead.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge531
		warrior_lodge_get_dice_bet_effect = yes
		warrior_lodge_dice_game_cleanup_effect = yes
	}
}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 is_tribal = no is_nomadic = no }
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.25
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:random_society_winner
						attribute = combat_rating
						value = 5
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.25
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:random_society_winner
						attribute = combat_rating
						value = 10
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:random_society_winner
						attribute = combat_rating
						value = 15
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.75
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:random_society_winner
						attribute = combat_rating
						value = 20
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Pay your debtor.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge530
		trigger = { event_target:random_society_winner = { is_alive = yes } }
		warrior_lodge_pay_dice_debt_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { character_event = { id = lodge.539 } } #Notify other warrior.
		}
	}
	option = { #Call in favor.
		name = EVTOPTClodge530
		trigger = { event_target:random_society_winner = { is_alive = yes owes_favor_to = ROOT } }
		event_target:random_society_winner = { remove_favor = ROOT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { character_event = { id = lodge.540 } } #Notify other warrior.
		}
	}
	option = { #Pay with favor.
		name = EVTOPTDlodge530
		trigger = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { is_alive = yes NOT = { owes_favor_to = ROOT } }
			NOT = { owes_favor_to = event_target:random_society_winner }
		}
		event_target:random_society_winner = { add_favor = ROOT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { character_event = { id = lodge.541 } } #Notify other warrior.
		}
	}
	option = { #
		name = EVTOPTAlodge533
		trigger = { event_target:random_society_winner = { is_alive = no } }
		warrior_lodge_pay_dice_debt_effect = yes
	}
	after = {
		warrior_lodge_dice_game_cleanup_effect = yes
	}
}

# lodge.534 - The bump wins
# Double or nothing?
# Scope: the player
character_event = {
	id = lodge.534
	title = EVTTITLElodge526
	desc = EVTDESClodge534
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Double or nothing.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge534
		warrior_lodge_double_dice_bet_effect = yes
		random_list = {
			40 = {
				#Discovered.
				custom_tooltip = { text = TT_DIE_TABLE_BUMP_DISCOVERED }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.532 } }
			}
			30 = {
				#Fails
				custom_tooltip = { text = TT_DIE_TABLE_BUMP_FAILS }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.533 } }
			}
			30 = {
				#Unnoticed.
				custom_tooltip = { text = TT_DIE_TABLE_BUMP_WINS }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.538 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #
		name = EVTOPTBlodge534
		warrior_lodge_get_dice_bet_effect = yes
		warrior_lodge_dice_game_cleanup_effect = yes
	}
}

# lodge.535 - The die is cast
# A fair throw: win or lose by the face that comes up.
# Scope: the player
character_event = {
	id = lodge.535
	title = EVTTITLElodge526
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge535 #You won.
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_coin
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_coin
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_crown
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_crown
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_sword
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_sword
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_skull
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_skull
				}
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge535_b #You lost.
		trigger = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { is_alive = yes }
			NOR = {
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_coin
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_coin
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_crown
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_crown
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_sword
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_sword
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_skull
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_skull
				}
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClodge535 #You lost. - No winner.
		trigger = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { is_alive = no }
			NOR = {
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_coin
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_coin
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_crown
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_crown
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_sword
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_sword
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_skull
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_skull
				}
			}
		}
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_coin
						has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_coin
					}
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_crown
						has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_crown
					}
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_sword
						has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_sword
					}
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_skull
						has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_skull
					}
				}
			}
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					is_adult = yes
					same_society_as = ROOT
					NOR = { character = ROOT trait = incapable }
				}
				save_event_target_as = random_society_winner
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #You won. Double or nothing.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge535
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_coin
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_coin
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_crown
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_crown
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_sword
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_sword
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_skull
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_skull
				}
			}
		}
		warrior_lodge_double_dice_bet_effect = yes
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clr_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_coin
			clr_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_crown
			clr_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_sword
			clr_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_skull
			random_list = {
				60 = {
					#Coin.
					set_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_coin
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.536 } }
				}
				40 = {
					#Crown.
					set_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_crown
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.536 } }
				}
				20 = {
					#Sword.
					set_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_sword
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.536 } }
				}
				10 = {
					#Skull.
					set_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_skull
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.536 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #You won. Leave while ahead.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge535
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_coin
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_coin
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_crown
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_crown
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_sword
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_sword
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_skull
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_skull
				}
			}
		}
		warrior_lodge_get_dice_bet_effect = yes
		warrior_lodge_dice_game_cleanup_effect = yes
	}
	option = { #You lost.
		name = EVTOPTClodge535
		trigger = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { is_alive = no }
			NOR = {
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_coin
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_coin
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_crown
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_crown
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_sword
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_sword
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_skull
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_skull
				}
			}
		}
		warrior_lodge_pay_dice_debt_effect = yes
		warrior_lodge_dice_game_cleanup_effect = yes
	}
	option = { #Special option. - Sore loser starts duel!
		name = EVTOPTYlodge535
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTYlodge535_TT }
		trigger = {
			trait = arbitrary
			event_target:random_society_winner = {
				ai = yes
				is_alive = yes
				this_can_duel_with_from_trigger = yes
			}
			NOR = {
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_coin
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_coin
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_crown
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_crown
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_sword
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_sword
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_skull
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_skull
				}
			}
		}
		tooltip_info = arbitrary
		tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes
		warrior_lodge_dice_game_cleanup_effect = yes
		#REQUIRED event targets for setting up duel evaluation!
		hidden_tooltip = {
			save_event_target_as = combatant_1 #the person issuing the duel...
			event_target:random_society_winner = { save_event_target_as = combatant_2 } #the target of the duel...
		}
		event_target:random_society_winner = {
			show_scope_change = no
			apply_degree_of_dishonorable_duel_effect = yes #checks a bunch of age and health statuses...
		}

		pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = recent_duel_timer duration = 365 hidden = yes }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { character_event = { id = duel.1 days = 1 } } #Duel evaluation (hidden) + result event as follow-up.
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 is_tribal = no is_nomadic = no }
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.25
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:random_society_winner
						attribute = combat_rating
						value = 5
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.25
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:random_society_winner
						attribute = combat_rating
						value = 10
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:random_society_winner
						attribute = combat_rating
						value = 15
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.75
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:random_society_winner
						attribute = combat_rating
						value = 20
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #You lost. Pay your debtor.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge530
		trigger = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { is_alive = yes }
			NOR = {
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_coin
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_coin
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_crown
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_crown
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_sword
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_sword
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_skull
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_skull
				}
			}
		}
		warrior_lodge_pay_dice_debt_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { character_event = { id = lodge.539 } } #Notify other warrior.
		}
		warrior_lodge_dice_game_cleanup_effect = yes
	}
	option = { #You lost. Call in favor.
		name = EVTOPTClodge530
		trigger = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { is_alive = yes owes_favor_to = ROOT }
			NOR = {
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_coin
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_coin
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_crown
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_crown
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_sword
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_sword
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_skull
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_skull
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:random_society_winner = { remove_favor = ROOT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { character_event = { id = lodge.540 } } #Notify other warrior.
		}
		warrior_lodge_dice_game_cleanup_effect = yes
	}
	option = { #You lost. Pay with favor.
		name = EVTOPTDlodge530
		trigger = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { is_alive = yes NOT = { owes_favor_to = ROOT } }
			NOT = { owes_favor_to = event_target:random_society_winner }
			NOR = {
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_coin
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_coin
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_crown
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_crown
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_sword
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_sword
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_skull
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_skull
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:random_society_winner = { add_favor = ROOT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:random_society_winner = { character_event = { id = lodge.541 } } #Notify other warrior.
		}
		warrior_lodge_dice_game_cleanup_effect = yes
	}
}

				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.75
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:random_society_winner
						attribute = combat_rating
						value = 20
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #You lost.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge536
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_coin
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_coin
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_crown
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_crown
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_sword
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_sword
				}
				AND = {
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_is_skull
					has_character_flag = lodge_dice_bet_skull
				}
			}
		}
		warrior_lodge_pay_dice_debt_effect = yes
	}
	option = { #You lost. Pay your debtor.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge530
		trigger = {

	}

			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_smart_trigger = yes
						has_lifestyle_diplomacy_trigger = yes
						diplomacy = 15
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = flyting_strong
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						is_smart_trigger = yes
						has_lifestyle_diplomacy_trigger = yes
						diplomacy = 15
					}
					diplomacy = 8
				}
				set_character_flag = flyting_medium
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						is_smart_trigger = yes
						has_lifestyle_diplomacy_trigger = yes
						diplomacy = 8
						has_education_diplomacy_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = flyting_weak
			}
			#Safety check
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						has_character_flag = flyting_strong
						has_character_flag = flyting_medium
						has_character_flag = flyting_weak
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = flyting_medium
			}
			set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = { id = lodge.601 days = 15 } #Clear flag.
			}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Pick tough fight.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge550
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge550_TT }
		trigger = { event_target:flyting_potential_opponent_1 = { is_alive = yes } }
		show_portrait = event_target:flyting_potential_opponent_1
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:flyting_potential_opponent_1 = {
				save_event_target_as = flyting_contender_2
				set_character_flag = flyting_strong
				set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.601 days = 15 } #Clear flag.
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:flyting_potential_opponent_2 = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:flyting_potential_opponent_2 = {
					clr_character_flag = flyting_strong
					clr_character_flag = flyting_medium
					clr_character_flag = flyting_weak
					clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:flyting_potential_opponent_3 = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:flyting_potential_opponent_3 = {
					clr_character_flag = flyting_strong
					clr_character_flag = flyting_medium
					clr_character_flag = flyting_weak
					clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				}
			}
			set_flyting_patience_effect = yes
			event_target:flyting_contender_2 = { character_event = { id = lodge.553 } }
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
	option = { #Pick average opponent.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge550
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBlodge550_TT }
		trigger = { event_target:flyting_potential_opponent_2 = { is_alive = yes } }
		show_portrait = event_target:flyting_potential_opponent_2
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:flyting_potential_opponent_2 = {
				save_event_target_as = flyting_contender_2
				set_character_flag = flyting_medium
				set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				hidden_tooltip = {

				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:flyting_potential_opponent_1 = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:flyting_potential_opponent_1 = {
					clr_character_flag = flyting_strong
					clr_character_flag = flyting_medium
					clr_character_flag = flyting_weak
					clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:flyting_potential_opponent_3 = { is_alive = yes } }
				event_target:flyting_potential_opponent_3 = {
					clr_character_flag = flyting_strong
					clr_character_flag = flyting_medium
					clr_character_flag = flyting_weak
					clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				}
			}
			set_flyting_patience_effect = yes
			event_target:flyting_contender_2 = { character_event = { id = lodge.553 } }
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	option = { #Pick lousy opponent.
		name = EVTOPTClodge550
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTClodge550_TT }
		trigger = { event_target:flyting_potential_opponent_3 = { is_alive = yes } }

					clr_character_flag = flyting_strong
					clr_character_flag = flyting_medium

				}
			}
			set_flyting_patience_effect = yes
			event_target:flyting_contender_2 = { character_event = { id = lodge.553 } }
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = brave }
		}
	}
	option = { #Not interested. -End of line.
		name = EVTOPTDlodge550
		tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes

# lodge.553 - The flyting
# Hidden: the opponent is told of the challenge.
# Scope: the challenged; FROM the challenger
character_event = {
	id = lodge.553
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = { warband_flyting_cleanup_effect = yes }

	immediate = {
		add_character_modifier = { name = flyting_challenge_cooldown duration = 1250 hidden = yes }
		FROM = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				name = flyting_challenge_cooldown
				duration = 1250
				hidden = yes
			}
			character_event = { id = lodge.554 }
		}
	}
}

# lodge.562 - The loser
# Hidden: the loser is found.
# Scope: the loser
character_event = {
	id = lodge.562
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = { #Receiver could be either.
			limit = { event_target:flyting_contender_2 = { character = ROOT } }
			event_target:flyting_contender_1 = { character_event = { id = lodge.563 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:flyting_contender_1 = { character = ROOT } }
			event_target:flyting_contender_2 = { character_event = { id = lodge.563 } }
		}
	}
}

# lodge.563 - Victory
# The foe lost their temper. Mock them or make peace.
# Scope: the winner; FROM the loser
character_event = {
	id = lodge.563
	title = EVTTITLElodge550
	desc = EVTDESClodge563
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = { warband_flyting_cleanup_effect = yes }

	option = { #Mock loser.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge563
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge563_TT }
		#Influence reward based on opponent's strength.
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					event_target:flyting_contender_1 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = flyting_strong
					}
					event_target:flyting_contender_2 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = flyting_strong
					}
				}
			}
			add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
			hidden_tooltip = { #If defeated a strong guy, gain nickname.
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOR = {
							this_has_average_nickname_trigger = yes
							this_has_cool_nickname_trigger = yes
							this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					random_list = {
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_sharp_tongue }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_trickster }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_merciless }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_word_slayer }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_grinner }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_poison_tooth }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_bellower }
						25 = { trigger = { is_female = no } give_nickname = nick_the_fox }
						25 = { trigger = { is_woman_trigger = yes } give_nickname = nick_the_vixen }
						25 = {
							trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes }
							give_nickname = nick_the_hyena
						}
						15 = { }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					event_target:flyting_contender_1 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = flyting_medium
					}
					event_target:flyting_contender_2 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = flyting_medium
					}
				}
			}
			add_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					event_target:flyting_contender_1 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = flyting_weak
					}
					event_target:flyting_contender_2 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = flyting_weak
					}
				}
			}
			add_society_currency_trivial_effect = yes
		}
		tooltip = {
			#Notify loser.
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:flyting_contender_1 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:flyting_contender_2 = {
					show_scope_change = no
					opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_lousy_winner years = 5 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:flyting_contender_2 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:flyting_contender_1 = {
					show_scope_change = no
					opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_lousy_winner years = 5 }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			#Notify loser.
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:flyting_contender_1 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:flyting_contender_2 = { character_event = { id = lodge.568 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:flyting_contender_2 = { character = ROOT } }
				event_target:flyting_contender_1 = { character_event = { id = lodge.568 } }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_evil_trigger = yes } }
	}
	option = { #Kind to loser.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge563
		#Influence reward based on opponent's strength.
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					event_target:flyting_contender_1 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = flyting_strong
					}
					event_target:flyting_contender_2 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = flyting_strong
					}
				}
			}
			add_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
			hidden_tooltip = { #If defeated a strong guy, gain nickname.
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOR = {
							this_has_average_nickname_trigger = yes
							this_has_cool_nickname_trigger = yes
							this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					random_list = {
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_sharp_tongue }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_trickster }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_merciless }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_word_slayer }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_grinner }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_poison_tooth }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_bellower }
						25 = { trigger = { is_female = no } give_nickname = nick_the_fox }
						25 = { trigger = { is_woman_trigger = yes } give_nickname = nick_the_vixen }
						25 = {
							trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes }
							give_nickname = nick_the_hyena
						}
						15 = { }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					event_target:flyting_contender_1 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = flyting_medium
					}
					event_target:flyting_contender_2 = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = flyting_medium
					}
				}
			}
			add_society_currency_trivial_effect = yes
		}

					}

						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						has_character_flag = flyting_weak
					}
				}
			}
			prestige = 10
		}

# lodge.568 - Mocked
# The loser is mocked, and may call a duel.
# Scope: the loser; FROM the winner
character_event = {
	id = lodge.568
	title = EVTTITLElodge550
	desc = EVTDESClodge568
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = { warband_flyting_cleanup_effect = yes }
	option = { #Special option. - Character starts duel!
		name = EVTOPTYlodge568
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTYlodge516_TT }
		trigger = { this_can_duel_with_from_trigger = yes }
		tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_antagonized_3 who = ROOT years = 15 }
		#Influence penalty based on opponent's strength.
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flyting_strong } }
			detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flyting_medium } }
			detract_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flyting_weak } }
			detract_society_currency_trivial_effect = yes
			hidden_tooltip = { #If defeated by a weak guy, gain nickname.
				if = {
					limit = { has_nickname = no }
					random_list = {
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_short_tempered }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_red_cheeks }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_whiner }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_moaner }
						25 = { trigger = { is_female = no } give_nickname = nick_the_blushing }
						25 = {
							trigger = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
							give_nickname = nick_the_shrinking_violet
						}
						25 = { }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		#REQUIRED event targets for setting up duel evaluation!
		hidden_tooltip = {
			save_event_target_as = combatant_1 #the person issuing the duel...
			FROM = { save_event_target_as = combatant_2 } #the target of the duel...
		}
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			apply_degree_of_dishonorable_duel_effect = yes #checks a bunch of age and health statuses...
		}

		pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = recent_duel_timer duration = 365 hidden = yes }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = { character_event = { id = duel.1 days = 1 } } #Duel evaluation (hidden) + result event as follow-up.
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 40
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 is_tribal = no is_nomadic = no }
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.25
				ROOT = { attribute_diff = { character = FROM attribute = combat_rating value = 5 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.25
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = { character = FROM attribute = combat_rating value = 10 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = { character = FROM attribute = combat_rating value = 15 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.75
				ROOT = {
					attribute_diff = { character = FROM attribute = combat_rating value = 20 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Become a rival.
		name = EVTOPTAlodge568
		trigger = {
			NOT = { num_of_rivals = 3 }
			NOT = { FROM = { num_of_rivals = 3 } }
			NOR = { is_rival = FROM is_friend = FROM is_lover = FROM }
		}
		add_rival = FROM
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.570 } } } #Notify.
		tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes
		#Influence penalty based on opponent's strength.
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flyting_strong } }
			detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flyting_medium } }
			detract_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flyting_weak } }
			hidden_tooltip = { #If defeated by a weak guy, gain nickname.
				if = {
					limit = { has_nickname = no }
					random_list = {
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_short_tempered }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_red_cheeks }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_whiner }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_moaner }
						25 = { trigger = { is_female = no } give_nickname = nick_the_blushing }
						25 = {
							trigger = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
							give_nickname = nick_the_shrinking_violet
						}
						25 = { }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_lousy_winner years = 5 }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge568_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_society_member = {
				limit = { is_adult = yes same_society_as = ROOT NOT = { character = FROM } }
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_loser years = 2 }
			}
			warband_flyting_cleanup_effect = yes #Decide who wins.
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge568
		#Influence penalty based on opponent's strength.
		tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flyting_strong } }
			detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flyting_medium } }
			detract_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flyting_weak } }
			detract_society_currency_trivial_effect = yes
			hidden_tooltip = { #If defeated by a weak guy, gain nickname.
				if = {
					limit = { has_nickname = no }
					random_list = {
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_short_tempered }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_red_cheeks }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_whiner }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_moaner }
						25 = { trigger = { is_female = no } give_nickname = nick_the_blushing }
						25 = {
							trigger = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
							give_nickname = nick_the_shrinking_violet
						}
						25 = { }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_lousy_winner years = 5 }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge568_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_society_member = {
				limit = { is_adult = yes same_society_as = ROOT NOT = { character = FROM } }

			}

		}

		}
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_lousy_winner years = 5 }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge569_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_society_member = {
				limit = { is_adult = yes same_society_as = ROOT NOT = { character = FROM } }
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_loser years = 2 }
			}
			warband_flyting_cleanup_effect = yes #Decide who wins.
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge569
		tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes
		#Influence penalty based on opponent's strength.
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flyting_strong } }
			detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flyting_medium } }
			detract_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flyting_weak } }
			detract_society_currency_trivial_effect = yes
			hidden_tooltip = { #If defeated by a weak guy, gain nickname.
				if = {
					limit = { has_nickname = no }
					random_list = {
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_short_tempered }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_red_cheeks }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_whiner }
						25 = { give_nickname = nick_the_moaner }
						25 = { trigger = { is_female = no } give_nickname = nick_the_blushing }
						25 = {
							trigger = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
							give_nickname = nick_the_shrinking_violet
						}
						25 = { }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_kind_winner years = 5 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			warband_flyting_cleanup_effect = yes #Decide who wins.
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}

# --- The drinking contest --------------------------------------------------

# lodge.580 - The drinking contest
# A Hero holds a drinking contest for the society.
# Scope: a Hero of a lodge, warband, Amazon society or witch hunter order; from on_actions
character_event = {
	id = lodge.580
	title = EVTTITLElodge580
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge580_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge580_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge580_greenskin }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge580_ulric }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge580_knight }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge580_dawi }
	desc = { trigger = { lodge_voice_other_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESClodge580_any }
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	hide_new = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_adult = yes
		in_command = no
		prisoner = no
		society_rank = 4
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
		lodge_feast_member_trigger = yes
		any_society_member = { #Make sure there are enough contenders to join in.
			same_society_as = ROOT
			is_adult = yes
			prisoner = no
			in_command = no
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
			NOT = { character = ROOT }
			count = 4 #At least a few other members should be around.
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			33 = { sound_effect = pagan_cheering_01 }
			33 = { sound_effect = pagan_cheering_02 }
			33 = { sound_effect = pagan_cheering_03 }
			33 = { sound_effect = warrior_lodge_ambient_02 }
		}
		set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
		hidden_tooltip = {
			character_event = { id = lodge.601 days = 15 } #Clear flag.
		}
	}
	option = { #Start tournament
		name = EVTOPTAlodge580
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge580_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					is_adult = yes
					in_command = no
					prisoner = no
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
					NOT = { trait = incapable }
					NOT = { has_character_flag = do_not_disturb }
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
				}
				character_event = { id = lodge.581 } #Call in contenders.
			}
			save_event_target_as = WL_host_drinking_game
			character_event = { id = lodge.583 days = 5 } #Actually start.
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lodge.582 days = 60 } } #Clear flags.
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Do not. - End of line.
		name = EVTOPTBlodge580
		detract_society_currency_major_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# lodge.590 - Caught spilling
# The cheat is exposed.
# Scope: the cheat; FROM the host
character_event = {
	id = lodge.590
	title = EVTTITLElodge580
	desc = EVTDESClodge590
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge590
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAlodge590_TT }
		tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes
		detract_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
		random = { chance = 10 add_trait = wounded }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_society_member = {
				limit = {
					is_adult = yes
					prisoner = no
					same_society_as = ROOT
					NOT = { character = FROM }
				}
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_cheater years = 5 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_nickname = no }
				random_list = {
					15 = { give_nickname = nick_the_cheater }
					15 = { give_nickname = nick_the_liar }
					15 = { give_nickname = nick_the_dry_throat }
					70 = { }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:warrior_lodge_drinking_finalist_1 = {
						is_alive = yes
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
					} #If other guy still exists, he has won.
				}
				event_target:warrior_lodge_drinking_finalist_1 = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.591 }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:warrior_lodge_drinking_finalist_2 = {
						is_alive = yes
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
					} #If other guy still exists, he has won.
				}
				event_target:warrior_lodge_drinking_finalist_2 = {
					character_event = { id = lodge.591 }
				}
			}
			else = {
				event_target:WL_host_drinking_game = { character_event = { id = lodge.586 } } #Otherwise inform host that nobody won.
			}
			warband_drinking_game_cleanup_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# lodge.593 - A winner
# The host may offer the winner an alliance, friendship or a gift.
# Scope: the host; FROM the winner
character_event = {
	id = lodge.593
	title = EVTTITLElodge580
	desc = EVTDESClodge593
	picture = GFX_evt_camaraderie
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	portrait = FROM

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Make non-aggression pact. (Trusting only, rulers only)
	#Make friend.
	#Give gold for currency.
	#Nothing.

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge593
		trigger = {
			trait = trusting
			higher_real_tier_than = BARON
			FROM = { is_landed = yes higher_real_tier_than = BARON }
			NOT = { vassal_of = FROM }
			NOT = { has_non_aggression_pact_with = FROM }
		}
		tooltip_info = trusting
		add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		tooltip = {
			opinion = {
				modifier = in_non_aggression_pact
				years = 50
				who = FROM
				origin_description = EVTOPTAlodge593
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.596 } } } #Inform winner.
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBlodge593
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				num_of_friends = 3
				is_rival = FROM
				is_friend = FROM
				is_lover = FROM
				FROM = { num_of_friends = 3 }
			}
			NOT = { trait = paranoid }
		}
		add_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		tooltip = { add_friend = FROM }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.598 } } } #Inform winner.
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = socializer }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = trusting }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTClodge593
		add_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		tooltip = { transfer_scaled_wealth = { to = FROM value = 0.5 min = 50 max = 100 } }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lodge.600 } } } #Inform winner.
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = just }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = kind }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDlodge593
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
	after = {
		tooltip = { add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes } #*actually* added only *once*, for the winner...
		hidden_tooltip = {
			warband_drinking_game_cleanup_effect = yes
			any_society_member = { #Notify all other contenders.
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					has_character_flag = flag_WL_drinking_game_contender
					NOT = { character = FROM }
					NOT = { event_target:warrior_lodge_drinking_finalist_1 = { character = PREV } }
					NOT = { event_target:warrior_lodge_drinking_finalist_2 = { character = PREV } }
				}
				character_event = { id = lodge.594 }
			}
		}
	}
}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlodge594
		prestige = 25
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = drunkard } } random = { chance = 1 add_trait = drunkard } }
		warband_drinking_game_cleanup_effect = yes
		tooltip = { add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes } #*actually* added only *once*, for the winner...
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}
