###############################################################################
# Warbands
# Namespace: warband (was WB.25000 - WB.25037 in HF_warband_powers_events,
#   WB.1 - WB.10090 in hf_warband_events, and HF.25000, HF.25021 - HF.25024
#   and HF.25030 - HF.25037 in HF_warrior_lodge_powers_events)
# Faction: Warrior societies
# Serves: the Chaos warbands (Undivided, Khorne, Nurgle, Tzeentch,
#   Slaanesh), the Skaven Warlords and the warbands of Gork and Mork; the
#   Call to Glory and the war sacrifice also serve the Warriors of Ulric,
#   and Summon Commander every warrior lodge and the Knights of Bretonnia
#
# The warband powers, each in the voice of its god, the Skaven clans or
# the greenskins.
# The Call to Glory (rank 4, not Gork and Mork, who have the Waaagh!; also
# the Warriors of Ulric): a champion at war calls on the warband, and
# fresh warriors join while the
# champion leads an army on land. The flow dries up after 20 bands unless
# the champion inspires or pays for more; rowdy bands demand plunder; the
# champion's own people flock to the capital. Rarely, once in a life, the
# Chosen (Blightkings, Stormvermin, the White Wolves) come and stay for
# good. At peace the call lapses.
# Summon Commander (rank 2): a champion joins the court and may take a
# commander's post; a warrior of a lodge or a Knight of the Realm speaks
# in the voice of that society.
# Summon Warriors (rank 3): a great host comes to the capital for one war;
# the power waits until peace.
#
# Greenskin and Skaven paths (rank 2): pick a way of war (the open field,
# the mountains, the caves or the forests; the tunnels, the Stormvermin or
# leading from the back). A new Skaven officer may raid the armour-pile.
# Chaos paths (rank 3): the duel, the art of conquest or the dungeon.
# Champion of the Gods (rank 4): the mark of the warband's god.
# Gifts: an Imp claimed by a Dark God (rank 2), Khorne's berserkers.
# Upkeep: AI recruits join every five years (Gork or Mork by temper),
# and a fallen warband rises again under a member of its faith.
# The war sacrifice (rank 3): an offering for victory in the way of each
# power; kin, slaves, a champion's blood, plague or plunder for the Dark
# Gods and the Horned Rat, a blood oath or burned spoils for Ulric.
#
# The Call to Glory            warband.1 - warband.9
# Summon Commander             warband.10
# Summon Warriors              warband.15 - warband.17
# Greenskin and Skaven paths   warband.20 - warband.21 (was WB.1 - WB.2)
# Chaos paths                  warband.30 - warband.38 (was WB.10020 - WB.10031)
# Champion of the Gods         warband.40 (was WB.10040)
# Imps and berserkers          warband.45 - warband.50 (was WB.10070 - WB.10080)
# Upkeep                       warband.60 - warband.63 (was WB.10050 - WB.10053)
# The war sacrifice            warband.70 - warband.71 (was HF.25021 - HF.25024)
#
# Saved: character modifiers call_to_glory, warband_summon_warriors_cooldown;
#   variable call_to_glory_variable; character flag warband_got_chosen;
#   earmarks call_to_glory_disciplined, call_to_glory_rowdy, warband_chosen,
#   warband_summoned_warriors; event targets invited_warband_soldier,
#   scoped_war_sacrifice, sacrificed_courtier, sacrificed_commander_khorne;
#   character flag flag_picking_war_sacrifice and modifiers
#   lodge_war_sacrifice_2 - 4, lodge_war_sacrifice_cooldown.
#
# Callers:
#   decisions/hf_warrior_lodge_decisions.txt (warband_call_to_glory: 1,
#     warband_summon_warriors: 15, warband_war_sacrifice: 70),
#   decisions/warrior_lodge_decisions.txt (warrior_lodge_call_to_glory: 1,
#     warrior_lodge_summon_commander: 10),
#   decisions/employment_decisions.txt (warband_summon_commander: 10),
#   common/on_actions/00_on_actions.txt: on_war_ended_* (8, 16),
#     on_character_leave_society (9).
#   decisions/hf_warrior_lodge_decisions.txt (warband_specialization: 20,
#     warband_choose_chaos_aspect: 30/31, warband_champion_training: 40,
#     warband_summon_familiar: 45, warband_khorne_berserker: 50),
#   decisions/wh_skaven_decisions.txt (Skaven path: 20),
#   events/mnm_misc_events.txt (Skaven promotion to rank 2: 21),
#   common/on_actions/00_on_actions_warbands.txt: on_five_year_pulse (60),
#     on_society_destroyed (63).
# Support: common/scripted_triggers/wh_warband_triggers.txt,
#   common/scripted_effects/wh_warband_effects.txt
#
# Fixed: the Warriors of Ulric had their own copy of the Call to Glory
# (HF.25030 - HF.25037), now merged; the clean-up at a war's end looked
# only at the defender, so an attacker's call never lapsed there, and a
# warband champion below rank 4 kept it. In the war sacrifice the
# aftermath had text only for vanilla pagans, so every warband saw an
# empty event; Khorne's duel was never to the death (the flag sat on the
# wrong duellist); Tzeentch's pick of the heir could match no one;
# Nurgle's plague spread the Lustrian Fever and smallpox, now the
# Creeping Buboes and the poxes, and its prosperity loss was garbled.
# Ulric's warriors had to kill kin or servants; Ulric takes no lives.
#
# Removed: a dead copy of the scarred-trait setup (WB.25020; lodge.65
# does it), dead copies of the lodge rank-up duel letters (WB.10060 -
# WB.10063; the shared HF.10060 - HF.10063 are used) and an uncalled
# Malice grant (WB.10090).
###############################################################################

namespace = warband

###############################################################################
# The Call to Glory
###############################################################################

# warband.1 The call goes out
# The champion calls the warband to war; the first warriors come in about
# a month. Scope: the caller.
character_event = {
	id = warband.1
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband1_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband1_khorne }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_nurgle_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband1_nurgle }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_tzeentch_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband1_tzeentch }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband1_slaanesh }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband1_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband1_ulric }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_chaos_warband
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_skaven_warlord }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_ulric_warriors }
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband1_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband1_khorne }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_nurgle_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband1_nurgle }
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_tzeentch_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAwarband1_tzeentch
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAwarband1_slaanesh
		}
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband1_skaven }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband1_ulric }
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTAHF25030 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = warband.2 days = 25 random = 5 } }
	}
}

# warband.2 The muster (hidden)
# Every month or so: a band arrives, the flow dries up, rowdy warriors
# grumble, the people flock to the capital or, once in a life, the Chosen
# come. Out of command it waits; at peace it ends. Scope: the caller.
character_event = {
	id = warband.2
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			30 = { #A new band
				trigger = { warband_glory_ok_trigger = yes warband_glory_leading_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = warband.3 }
			}
			50 = { #Not leading an army: wait
				trigger = { warband_glory_ok_trigger = yes warband_glory_leading_trigger = no }
				character_event = { id = warband.2 days = 35 random = 5 }
			}
			100 = { #The call has lapsed
				trigger = { warband_glory_lapsed_trigger = yes }
				warband_glory_end_effect = yes
			}
			100 = { #The flow dries up
				trigger = {
					warband_glory_lapsed_trigger = no
					warband_glory_leading_trigger = yes
					check_variable = { which = "call_to_glory_variable" value = 20 }
				}
				character_event = { id = warband.4 }
			}
			5 = { #Rowdy warriors want more plunder
				trigger = {
					warband_glory_ok_trigger = yes
					warband_glory_leading_trigger = yes
					has_earmarked_regiments = call_to_glory_rowdy
					check_variable = { which = "call_to_glory_variable" value = 5 }
				}
				character_event = { id = warband.5 }
			}
			5 = { #The people flock to the capital
				trigger = {
					warband_glory_ok_trigger = yes
					warband_glory_leading_trigger = yes
					check_variable = { which = "call_to_glory_variable" value = 5 }
				}
				character_event = { id = warband.6 }
			}
			2 = { #The Chosen (rare, once in a life)
				trigger = {
					warband_glory_ok_trigger = yes
					warband_glory_leading_trigger = yes
					check_variable = { which = "call_to_glory_variable" value = 10 }
					NOT = { has_character_flag = warband_got_chosen }
				}
				character_event = { id = warband.7 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# warband.3 A new band arrives
# Scope: the caller, leading an army on land.
character_event = {
	id = warband.3
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband3_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband3_khorne }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_nurgle_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband3_nurgle }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_tzeentch_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband3_tzeentch }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband3_slaanesh }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband3_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband3_ulric }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_chaos_warband
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_skaven_warlord }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_ulric_warriors }
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes
	notification = yes

	trigger = {
		warband_glory_ok_trigger = yes
		warband_glory_leading_trigger = yes
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = { limit = { warband_glory_lapsed_trigger = yes } warband_glory_end_effect = yes }
		else = { character_event = { id = warband.2 days = 25 random = 5 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband3_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband3_khorne }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_nurgle_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband3_nurgle }
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_tzeentch_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAwarband3_tzeentch
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAwarband3_slaanesh
		}
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband3_skaven }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband3_ulric }
		warband_glory_spawn_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = {
			change_variable = { which = "call_to_glory_variable" value = 1 }
			character_event = { id = warband.2 days = 35 random = 5 }
		}
	}
}

# warband.4 The flow dries up
# After 20 bands the warband sends no more, unless the champion inspires
# or pays for them. Scope: the caller.
character_event = {
	id = warband.4
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband4_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband4_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband4_ulric }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_chaos_warband
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_skaven_warlord }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_ulric_warriors }
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		warband_glory_lapsed_trigger = no
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = { limit = { warband_glory_lapsed_trigger = yes } warband_glory_end_effect = yes }
	}

	option = { #Inspire them
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband4_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband4_skaven }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband4_ulric }
		trigger = { combat_rating = 50 }
		tooltip_info = combat_rating
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_call_to_glory_resume_reinforcements }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			change_variable = { which = "call_to_glory_variable" value = -10 }
			character_event = { id = warband.2 days = 45 random = 5 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Pay them
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwarband4_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwarband4_skaven }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwarband4_ulric }
		trigger = { wealth = 150 }
		scaled_wealth = { value = -1.0 min = -150 max = -250 }
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_call_to_glory_resume_reinforcements }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			change_variable = { which = "call_to_glory_variable" value = -10 }
			character_event = { id = warband.2 days = 45 random = 5 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { wealth = 400 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = greedy }
		}
	}
	option = { #No more
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTCwarband4_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTCwarband4_skaven }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTCwarband4_ulric }
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_call_to_glory_end_reinforcements }
		hidden_tooltip = { set_variable = { which = "call_to_glory_variable" value = 0 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
}

# warband.5 Rowdy warriors want more plunder
# Talk them round, pay them, or let them go. Scope: the caller.
character_event = {
	id = warband.5
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband5_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband5_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband5_ulric }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_chaos_warband
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_skaven_warlord }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_ulric_warriors }
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		warband_glory_ok_trigger = yes
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = { limit = { warband_glory_lapsed_trigger = yes } warband_glory_end_effect = yes }
	}

	option = { #Talk them round
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband5_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband5_skaven }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband5_ulric }
		trigger = { diplomacy = 20 }
		tooltip_info = diplomacy
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Pay them
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwarband5_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwarband5_skaven }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwarband5_ulric }
		trigger = { wealth = 50 }
		scaled_wealth = { value = -0.8 min = -50 max = -150 }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { wealth = 200 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = greedy }
		}
	}
	option = { #Let them go
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTCwarband5_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTCwarband5_skaven }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTCwarband5_ulric }
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_rowdy_troops_leave }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			disband_event_forces = call_to_glory_rowdy
			change_variable = { which = "call_to_glory_variable" value = -3 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = warband.2 days = 25 random = 5 } }
	}
}

# warband.6 The people flock to the capital
# Train them as levies, send them home for prestige, arm them, or (Chaos)
# send them raiding by sea or (Skaven) hand them to Clan Skryre.
# Scope: the caller.
character_event = {
	id = warband.6
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband6_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband6_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband6_ulric }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_chaos_warband
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_skaven_warlord }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_ulric_warriors }
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		warband_glory_ok_trigger = yes
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = { limit = { warband_glory_lapsed_trigger = yes } warband_glory_end_effect = yes }
	}

	option = { #Train them
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband6_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband6_skaven }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband6_ulric }
		if = { limit = { is_nomadic = yes } manpower = 200 }
		else = {
			any_demesne_province = {
				add_province_modifier = {
					name = capital_call_to_glory
					duration = 150
					stacking = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Send them home
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwarband6_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwarband6_skaven }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwarband6_ulric }
		prestige = 150
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #Arm them
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTCwarband6_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTCwarband6_skaven }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTCwarband6_ulric }
		capital_scope = {
			ROOT = {
				hidden_tooltip = {
					change_variable = { which = "call_to_glory_variable" value = 1 }
				}
				spawn_unit = {
					province = PREV
					owner = ROOT
					troops = { light_infantry = { 400 400 } }
					earmark = call_to_glory_rowdy
					attrition = 0
					merge = yes
					maintenance_multiplier = 0
					disband_on_peace = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Raid by sea (Chaos)
		name = EVTOPTDwarband6_chaos
		trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes capital_scope = { is_coastal = yes } }
		spawn_fleet = {
			province = closest
			owner = ROOT
			disband_on_peace = yes
			troops = { galleys = { 50 50 } }
			earmark = call_to_glory_rowdy
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
	}
	option = { #Clan Skryre (Skaven)
		name = EVTOPTDwarband6_skaven
		trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes }
		military_techpoints = 75
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
	}
	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = warband.2 days = 45 random = 5 } }
	}
}

# warband.7 The Chosen
# Once in a life, a company of the Chosen (Blightkings for Nurgle,
# Stormvermin for the Skaven) joins the champion for good, at half upkeep.
# Scope: the caller, leading an army on land.
character_event = {
	id = warband.7
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband7_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband7_khorne }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_nurgle_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband7_nurgle }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_tzeentch_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband7_tzeentch }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband7_slaanesh }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband7_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband7_ulric }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_chaos_champions
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_skaven_warlord }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_ulric_warriors }
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		warband_glory_ok_trigger = yes
		warband_glory_leading_trigger = yes
		NOT = { has_character_flag = warband_got_chosen }
	}

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband7_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband7_skaven }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband7_ulric }
		set_character_flag = warband_got_chosen
		custom_tooltip = { text = warband7_chosen_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = { warband_chosen_spawn_effect = yes }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = warband.2 days = 35 random = 5 } }
	}
}

# warband.8 A war ends (hidden)
# On a war's end (on_war_ended_*): a day later, warband.9 checks whether
# either side's Call to Glory still holds (another war may go on).
# Scope: ROOT is the defender, FROM the attacker.
character_event = {
	id = warband.8
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_character_modifier = call_to_glory
			FROM = { has_character_modifier = call_to_glory }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = call_to_glory }
			character_event = { id = warband.9 days = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_modifier = call_to_glory } }
			FROM = { character_event = { id = warband.9 days = 1 } }
		}
	}
}

# warband.9 The Call to Glory ends (hidden)
# At peace, out of the society or below rank 4, the call lapses: the
# warriors go home and the capital's training ends. Also sent directly
# by on_character_leave_society. Scope: the caller.
character_event = {
	id = warband.9
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = call_to_glory
		warband_glory_lapsed_trigger = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		warband_glory_end_effect = yes
		any_demesne_province = { remove_province_modifier = capital_call_to_glory }
	}
}

# warband.10 A champion comes to court
# From warband_summon_commander (employment decisions) and
# warrior_lodge_summon_commander (warrior lodges, the Knights of Bretonnia,
# Gork, Mork and the Skaven): a warrior of the society joins the court and
# may take a commander's post, pushing out a weak one. Scope: the ruler;
# event_target:invited_warband_soldier.
character_event = {
	id = warband.10
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband10_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband10_khorne }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_nurgle_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband10_nurgle }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_tzeentch_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband10_tzeentch }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband10_slaanesh }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband10_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband10_greenskin }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband10_ulric }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband10_knight }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband10_dawi }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_bretonnia_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband10_bretonnia }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_chaos_champions
	}
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_skaven_warlord }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_orc_boss }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_ulric_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_ulric_warriors }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_knight_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_imperial_knight }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_dawi_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_dwarf_fighter }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_bretonnia_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_bretonnian_knight
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		event_target:invited_warband_soldier = { warband_join_ruler_society_effect = yes }
	}

	option = { #Replace a weak commander
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband10_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband10_skaven }
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAwarband10_greenskin
		}
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_lodge_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband10_lodge }
		trigger = {
			warband_commander_no_vassal_trigger = yes
			warband_commander_slots_full_trigger = yes
			warband_commander_weak_trigger = yes
		}
		random_realm_character = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { higher_tier_than = BARON }
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_commander
				NOT = { martial = 10 }
			}
			opinion = { modifier = insulted who = ROOT years = 2 }
			remove_title = title_commander
		}
		event_target:invited_warband_soldier = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_loyal_servant who = ROOT years = 100 }
			give_minor_title = title_commander
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #A free post
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwarband10_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwarband10_skaven }
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTBwarband10_greenskin
		}
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_lodge_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTBwarband10_lodge }
		trigger = {
			warband_commander_no_vassal_trigger = yes
			warband_commander_slots_full_trigger = no
		}
		event_target:invited_warband_soldier = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_loyal_servant who = ROOT years = 100 }
			give_minor_title = title_commander
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Stay at court
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTCwarband10_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTCwarband10_skaven }
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTCwarband10_greenskin
		}
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_lodge_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTCwarband10_lodge }
		event_target:invited_warband_soldier = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_loyal_servant who = ROOT years = 100 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
}

	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCwarband15_skaven
		trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes }
		capital_scope = {
			show_scope_change = no
			ROOT = {
				show_scope_change = no
				spawn_unit = {
					province = PREV
					owner = ROOT
					troops = { light_infantry = { 2000 2000 } }
					earmark = warband_summoned_warriors
					attrition = 0.5
					disband_on_peace = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCwarband15_greenskin
		trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
		capital_scope = {
			show_scope_change = no
			ROOT = {
				show_scope_change = no
				spawn_unit = {
					province = PREV
					owner = ROOT
					troops = { light_infantry = { 1500 1500 } archers = { 300 300 } }
					earmark = warband_summoned_warriors
					attrition = 0.5
					disband_on_peace = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = { #Not yet: refund
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_any_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTDwarband15_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTDwarband15_skaven }
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTDwarband15_greenskin
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			remove_character_modifier = warband_summon_warriors_cooldown
			if = {
				limit = { is_member_of_any_warband_trigger = yes }
				add_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# warband.16 A war ends (hidden)
# From on_war_ended_*: both sides check, a day later, whether the summon
# warriors cooldown can go. Scope: the defender; FROM is the attacker.
character_event = {
	id = warband.16
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_character_modifier = warband_summon_warriors_cooldown
			FROM = { has_character_modifier = warband_summon_warriors_cooldown }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = warband_summon_warriors_cooldown }
			character_event = { id = warband.17 days = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_modifier = warband_summon_warriors_cooldown } }
			FROM = { character_event = { id = warband.17 days = 1 } }
		}
	}
}

# warband.17 At peace, the warband can be summoned again (hidden)
# Scope: a ruler with the cooldown.
character_event = {
	id = warband.17
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = warband_summon_warriors_cooldown
		war = no
	}

	immediate = {
		remove_character_modifier = warband_summon_warriors_cooldown
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Greenskin and Skaven paths (rank 2)
###############################################################################

# warband.20 Choose a way of war
# From the warband_specialization decision (Gork and Mork) and the Skaven
# Warlords' path decision (decisions/wh_skaven_decisions.txt).
# Greenskins pick the open field, the mountains (the caves for Night
# Goblins) or the forests; Skaven pick the tunnels, the Stormvermin or
# leadership from the back.
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = warband.20
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband20_greenskin }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband20_skaven }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_greenskin_mound
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_skaven_rat_attack
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# The open field.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAwarband20
		trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
		add_trait = warband_warboss
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 culture_group = orc_group }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 culture = goblin_hill }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 culture = goblin_seagit }
		}
	}

	# The mountains.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBwarband20
		trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes NOT = { culture = goblin_night } }
		add_trait = warband_mountainboss
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 culture_group = goblin_group }
			modifier = { factor = 1.2 culture = orc_black }
		}
	}

	# The caves (Night Goblins).
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCwarband20
		trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes culture = goblin_night }
		add_trait = warband_ngboss
		ai_chance = { factor = 1.5 }
	}

	# The forests.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDwarband20
		trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
		add_trait = warband_forestboss
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 culture = goblin_forest }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 religion = spider_god }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 culture = orc_black }
			modifier = { factor = 0.7 culture_group = orc_group }
		}
	}

	# The tunnels.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTEwarband20
		trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes }
		add_trait = warlord_tunnelrat
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}

	# The Stormvermin.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTFwarband20
		trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes }
		add_trait = warlord_stormvermin
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}

	# Leading from the back.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTGwarband20
		trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes }
		add_trait = warlord_warlord
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}

	# Not yet.
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTHwarband20_greenskin
		}
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTHwarband20_skaven }
		add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = choosing_chaos_aspect
	}
}

# warband.21 The armour-pile
# From the Skaven promotion to rank 2 (events/mnm_misc_events.txt). Once in
# a life, a fresh Skaven officer helps himself from the clan's armour-pile.
# Scope: the member.
letter_event = {
	id = warband.21
	desc = EVTDESCwarband21
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		warband_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
		NOT = { has_character_flag = warband_raided_armour_pile }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAwarband21
		set_character_flag = warband_raided_armour_pile
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				trigger = { NOT = { has_artifact = clanrat_armour } }
				add_artifact = clanrat_armour
			}
			10 = {
				trigger = { NOT = { has_artifact = stormvermin_armour } }
				add_artifact = stormvermin_armour
			}
			5 = {
				trigger = { NOT = { has_artifact = skaven_blade_2 } }
				add_artifact = skaven_blade_2
			}
			5 = {
				trigger = { NOT = { has_artifact = skaven_blade_1 } }
				add_artifact = skaven_blade_1
			}
			5 = {
				trigger = { NOT = { has_artifact = skaven_warhammer } }
				add_artifact = skaven_warhammer
			}
			1 = {
				trigger = { NOT = { has_artifact = skaven_warglaive } }
				add_artifact = skaven_warglaive
			}
			25 = {
				trigger = { culture = moulder NOT = { has_artifact = moulder_whip } }
				add_artifact = moulder_whip
			}
			10 = {
				# Someone was looking after all.
				add_trait_wounded_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# warband.30 Change paths
# From the warband_choose_chaos_aspect decision, when the champion already walks
# a path (duelist, strategist or torturer).
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = warband.30
	desc = { trigger = { trait = duelist } text = EVTDESCwarband30_duelist }
	desc = { trigger = { trait = strategist } text = EVTDESCwarband30_strategist }
	desc = { trigger = { trait = impaler } text = EVTDESCwarband30_torturer }
	picture = GFX_evt_chaos_champions
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# The duel.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAwarband30
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = duelist } }
		remove_lifestyle_trait_effect = yes
		add_trait = duelist
	}

	# The art of conquest.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBwarband30
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = strategist } }
		remove_lifestyle_trait_effect = yes
		add_trait = strategist
	}

	# The dungeon.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCwarband30
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = impaler } }
		remove_lifestyle_trait_effect = yes
		add_trait = impaler
	}

	# Keep to my path.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDwarband30
		add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = choosing_chaos_aspect
	}
}

# warband.31 Choose a path
# From the warband_choose_chaos_aspect decision, for a champion with no
# lifestyle yet: the duel (warband.32), the art of conquest (warband.35)
# or the dungeon (warband.37).
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = warband.31
	desc = EVTDESCwarband31
	picture = GFX_evt_chaos_champions
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAwarband31
		show_trait = duelist
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = EVTOPTAwarband31_tt
			character_event = { id = warband.32 days = 20 random = 20 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 1.1 has_duel_exp_modifier_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = brave }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBwarband31
		show_trait = strategist
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = EVTOPTBwarband31_tt
			character_event = { id = warband.35 days = 20 random = 20 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_education_stewardship_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_education_martial_trigger = yes }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCwarband31
		show_trait = impaler
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = EVTOPTCwarband31_tt
			character_event = { id = warband.37 days = 20 random = 20 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 religion_slaanesh_worshipper_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = kind }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDwarband31
		add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = choosing_chaos_aspect
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# warband.32 The duel: find a teacher
# Ask a fellow champion (who hears of it in warband.33), pay a
# pit-fighter, or go alone. All roads end in warband.34.
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = warband.32
	desc = EVTDESCwarband32
	picture = GFX_evt_duel_against_chaos
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			random_society_member = {
				limit = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
					ai = yes
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { block_general_event_trigger = yes }
					trait = duelist
				}
				save_event_target_as = mentor_target
			}
		}
	}

	# Ask a fellow champion.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAwarband32
		trigger = { event_target:mentor_target = { always = yes } }
		set_character_flag = warband_duel_mentor
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = event_target:mentor_target
						modifier = opinion_budding_friendship
					}
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = event_target:mentor_target
						modifier = opinion_budding_friendship
					}
				}
			}
			tooltip = { add_friend = event_target:mentor_target }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:mentor_target = {
					potentially_interested_in_rivaling_root_trigger = no
				}
			}
			tooltip = {
				reverse_opinion = {
					who = event_target:mentor_target
					modifier = opinion_was_praised
					years = 10
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			tooltip = {
				reverse_opinion = {
					who = event_target:mentor_target
					modifier = opinion_strangely_flattered
					years = 10
				}
			}
		}
		prestige = -25
		event_target:mentor_target = { character_event = { id = warband.33 } }
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = warband.34 days = 60 random = 30 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 2 has_pleasant_personality_trigger = yes } }
	}

	# Pay a pit-fighter.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBwarband32
		set_character_flag = warband_duel_paid
		scaled_wealth = { value = -0.4 min = -40 }
		add_trait_silently_diligent_effect = yes
		remove_trait_silently_slothful_effect = yes
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = rigorous_practice duration = 3650 }
		hidden_effect = {
			clear_event_target = mentor_target
			character_event = { id = warband.34 days = 60 random = 30 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { wealth = 50 } }
		}
	}

	# Go alone.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCwarband32
		set_character_flag = warband_duel_alone
		hidden_effect = {
			clear_event_target = mentor_target
			character_event = { id = warband.34 days = 90 random = 30 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# warband.33 A fellow champion asks for lessons
# The student's training goes on without waiting for this answer.
# Scope: the teacher; FROM is the student.
character_event = {
	id = warband.33
	desc = EVTDESCwarband33
	picture = GFX_evt_duel_against_chaos
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAwarband33
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = duelist_mentor duration = 730 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBwarband33
		scaled_wealth = { value = -0.2 min = -20 }
	}

	after = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = FROM
						modifier = opinion_budding_friendship
					}
					has_opinion_modifier = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_budding_friendship }
				}
			}
			add_friend = FROM
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FROM = { root_potentially_interested_in_rivaling_this_trigger = no } }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_was_praised years = 10 }
		}
		else = {
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_strangely_flattered years = 10 }
		}
	}
}

# warband.34 A master of the blade
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = warband.34
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = warband_duel_mentor
			event_target:mentor_target = { is_alive = no }
		}
		text = EVTDESCwarband34_mentor_died
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = warband_duel_mentor
			event_target:mentor_target = { is_alive = yes }
		}
		text = EVTDESCwarband34_mentor
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = warband_duel_mentor } }
		text = EVTDESCwarband34
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_duel_against_chaos
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { has_character_flag = warband_duel_mentor }
			text = EVTOPTAwarband34_mentor
		}
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = warband_duel_paid } text = EVTOPTAwarband34_paid }
		name = {
			trigger = { has_character_flag = warband_duel_alone }
			text = EVTOPTAwarband34_alone
		}
		remove_lifestyle_trait_effect = yes
		add_trait = duelist
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = choosing_chaos_aspect
		clr_character_flag = warband_duel_mentor
		clr_character_flag = warband_duel_paid
		clr_character_flag = warband_duel_alone
	}
}

# warband.35 The art of conquest: study
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = warband.35
	desc = EVTDESCwarband35
	picture = GFX_evt_chaos_lord
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# Read the southlings' books.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAwarband35
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = patient } NOT = { trait = wroth } } add_trait = patient }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = hitting_the_books duration = 730 }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = { factor = 2 religion_tzeentch_worshipper_trigger = yes }
		}
	}

	# Watch the warband fight.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBwarband35
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = diligent } NOT = { trait = slothful } }
			add_trait = diligent
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = slothful } remove_trait = slothful }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = observing_the_troops duration = 730 }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 2 religion_khorne_worshipper_trigger = yes }
		}
	}

	after = {
		character_event = { id = warband.36 days = 60 random = 30 }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Champion of the Gods (rank 4)
###############################################################################

# warband.40 A Champion of Chaos
# From the warband_champion_training decision. The champion gets the mark
# of the warband's god and takes its faith (Beastmen become Bestigors).
# Nurgle's Kurgan champions may start the Tamurkhan infection (TAMUR.1).
# Scope: the member.
character_event = {
	id = warband.40
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband40_chaos }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_nurgle_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband40_nurgle }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_tzeentch_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband40_tzeentch }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband40_slaanesh }
	desc = { trigger = { warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCwarband40_khorne }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_chaos_chosen }
	picture = { trigger = { warband_voice_nurgle_trigger = yes } picture = GFX_evt_nurgle_sorcerer }
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_tzeentch_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_tzeentch_sorcerer
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_feast
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes }
		picture = GFX_evt_khorne_bloodletter
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband40_chaos }
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_nurgle_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband40_nurgle }
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_tzeentch_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAwarband40_tzeentch
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAwarband40_slaanesh
		}
		name = { trigger = { warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAwarband40_khorne }
		add_legend_progress_trivial_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { warband_voice_chaos_trigger = yes }
			add_trait = champion_undivided
			religion = chaos
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { warband_voice_nurgle_trigger = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { culture_group = beastman_group }
				add_trait = bestigor_nurgle
				set_graphical_culture = bestigor_nurgle
			}
			else = {
				add_trait = champion_nurgle
				hidden_effect = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							culture_group = kurgan_group
							NOT = { has_bloodline_flag = great_kurgan }
							NOT = { has_global_flag = tamurkhan_exists_flag }
							OR = { AND = { ai = yes total_years_played >= 50 } piety = 1000 }
						}
						random = {
							chance = 13
							modifier = { factor = 2 has_bloodline_flag = great_kurgan_line }
							modifier = { factor = 1.2 piety = 1500 }
							modifier = { factor = 1.2 piety = 2000 }
							modifier = { factor = 1.2 piety = 2500 }
							modifier = { factor = 1.2 piety = 3000 }
							modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_magic_trigger = yes }
							character_event = { id = TAMUR.1 days = 365 }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			religion = nurgle
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { warband_voice_tzeentch_trigger = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { culture_group = beastman_group }
				add_trait = bestigor_tzeentch
				set_graphical_culture = bestigor_tzeentch
			}
			else = { add_trait = champion_tzeentch }
			religion = tzeentch
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { warband_voice_slaanesh_trigger = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { culture_group = beastman_group }
				add_trait = bestigor_slaanesh
				set_graphical_culture = bestigor_slaanesh
			}
			else = { add_trait = champion_slaanesh }
			religion = slaanesh
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { warband_voice_khorne_trigger = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { culture_group = beastman_group }
				add_trait = bestigor_khorne
				set_graphical_culture = bestigor_khorne
			}
			else = { add_trait = champion_khorne }
			religion = khorne
		}
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = choosing_warrior_training
	}
}

# Upkeep: recruits and revival
